
===== FILE backend/app/migrations/snapshot_inline_nullable.py =====
"""Schema 128: allow a verified blob-backed run to omit duplicate inline text.

This migration only changes nullability. It does not clear snapshots or change
the current writer, which continues to store both forms.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from sqlalchemy import Connection, inspect


class SnapshotInlineNullableError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def migrate(connection: Connection) -> None:
    dialect = connection.dialect.name
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        _migrate_sqlite(connection)
    elif dialect == "mysql":
        _migrate_mysql(connection)
    elif dialect == "postgresql":
        _migrate_postgres(connection)
    else:
        raise SnapshotInlineNullableError(f"unsupported snapshot schema dialect: {dialect}")


def _migrate_sqlite(connection: Connection) -> None:
    original = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='production_runs'"
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    if original is None:  # Historical partial SQLite fixtures have no runtime table.
        return
    columns = {
        row[1]: row for row in connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA table_info(production_runs)")
    }
    snapshot = columns.get("snapshot_json")
    if snapshot is None:
        raise SnapshotInlineNullableError("production_runs lacks snapshot_json")
    if not snapshot[3]:
        return
    shadow = "production_runs_v128_new"
    if connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?", (shadow,)
    ).scalar_one_or_none():
        raise SnapshotInlineNullableError("pre-existing snapshot migration shadow table")

    replacement, table_count = re.subn(
        r'(?i)^CREATE\s+TABLE\s+(?:"production_runs"|production_runs)',
        f"CREATE TABLE {shadow}",
        original,
        count=1,
    )
    replacement, column_count = re.subn(
        r'(?i)([(,]\s*"?snapshot_json"?\s+TEXT)\s+NOT\s+NULL\b',
        r"\1",
        replacement,
        count=1,
    )
    if table_count != 1 or column_count != 1:
        raise SnapshotInlineNullableError("cannot identify inline snapshot column DDL")

    raw = connection.connection.driver_connection
    raw.commit()  # PRAGMA foreign_keys cannot change inside an open transaction.
    foreign_keys = int(raw.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys").fetchone()[0])
    legacy_alter = int(raw.execute("PRAGMA legacy_alter_table").fetchone()[0])
    raw.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
    raw.execute("PRAGMA legacy_alter_table=ON")
    try:
        raw.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        indexes = [row[0] for row in raw.execute(
            "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='index' "
            "AND tbl_name='production_runs' AND sql IS NOT NULL"
        )]
        triggers = [row[0] for row in raw.execute(
            "SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='trigger' "
            "AND tbl_name='production_runs' AND sql IS NOT NULL"
        )]
        raw.execute(replacement)
        names = [row[1] for row in raw.execute("PRAGMA table_info(production_runs)")]
        quoted = ", ".join('"' + name.replace('"', '""') + '"' for name in names)
        raw.execute(
            f"INSERT INTO {shadow} ({quoted}) SELECT {quoted} FROM production_runs"
        )
        raw.execute("DROP TABLE production_runs")
        raw.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {shadow} RENAME TO production_runs")
        for statement in indexes + triggers:
            raw.execute(statement)
        violations = raw.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").fetchmany(3)
        if violations:
            raise SnapshotInlineNullableError("snapshot migration foreign-key check failed")
        raw.commit()
    except Exception:
        if raw.in_transaction:
            raw.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        raw.execute(f"PRAGMA legacy_alter_table={legacy_alter}")
        raw.execute(f"PRAGMA foreign_keys={foreign_keys}")


def _migrate_mysql(connection: Connection) -> None:
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    if "production_runs" not in inspector.get_table_names():
        return
    columns = {item["name"]: item for item in inspector.get_columns("production_runs")}
    snapshot = columns.get("snapshot_json")
    if snapshot is None:
        raise SnapshotInlineNullableError("production_runs lacks snapshot_json")
    if not snapshot["nullable"]:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `production_runs` MODIFY COLUMN `snapshot_json` "
            "LONGTEXT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs NULL"
        )


def _migrate_postgres(connection: Connection) -> None:
    if inspect(connection).has_table("production_runs"):
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE production_runs ALTER COLUMN snapshot_json DROP NOT NULL"
        )

===== FILE backend/app/migrations/stock_exports_135.py =====
"""Add result snapshot and download-job tables without changing old exports."""
def migrate(connection):
    from ..stock_exports import StockExportSnapshot,StockExportItem,StockExportJob
    if connection.dialect.name=='mysql':
        from .mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata
        metadata=mysql_metadata()
        for name in ('stock_export_snapshots','stock_export_items','stock_export_jobs'):
            metadata.tables[name].create(connection,checkfirst=True)
    else:
        for model in (StockExportSnapshot,StockExportItem,StockExportJob):model.__table__.create(connection,checkfirst=True)

===== FILE backend/app/migrations/unique_constraints_129.py =====
"""Schema 129: two "look up, then insert" paths get a real unique constraint.

1. ``model_node_bindings``: ``UNIQUE(node_key, category_key)`` never binds the
   global binding (``category_key IS NULL``) on MySQL, nor on SQLite databases
   built by ``create_all``. A stored generated column ``category_scope`` folds
   NULL into ``''`` and ``UNIQUE(node_key, category_scope)`` closes the race.
2. ``prompt_versions``: ``(category_key, stage, version)`` was only checked by
   the application. Historical duplicates are renamed first (never deleted:
   prompt versions are referenced by frozen snapshots and runs), then the
   unique index is added.

Every step is re-entrant: MySQL DDL commits implicitly, so an interrupted
upgrade may be re-run from any point. Renames/deletes are recorded in
``audit_events`` with deterministic event keys and in the migration log.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import Connection, inspect, select, text

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MIGRATION_ACTOR = "migration:129"
PROMPT_VERSION_MAX_LENGTH = 120


class UniqueConstraintMigrationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def migrate(connection: Connection) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    dialect = connection.dialect.name
    if dialect not in {"sqlite", "mysql"}:
        raise UniqueConstraintMigrationError(f"unsupported dialect for schema 129: {dialect}")
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())
    # Historical partial SQLite fixtures may carry a table without these columns.
    required = {
        "prompt_versions": {"id", "category_key", "stage", "version", "status"},
        "model_node_bindings": {"id", "node_key", "category_key"},
    }
    for table, columns in required.items():
        if table in tables and not columns <= _column_names(connection, table):
            tables.discard(table)
    report: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {"prompt_versions": [], "model_node_bindings": []}
    # DML first: on MySQL the following DDL implicitly commits it together with
    # its audit rows; if the process dies before that, everything rolls back.
    if "prompt_versions" in tables:
        report["prompt_versions"] = dedupe_prompt_versions(connection)
    if "model_node_bindings" in tables:
        report["model_node_bindings"] = dedupe_model_node_bindings(connection)
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        _ddl_sqlite(connection, tables)
    else:
        _ddl_mysql(connection, tables)
    return report


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- dedupe (DML)


def suffixed_version(version: str, suffix: str) -> str:
    """``<version><suffix>``, truncating the original so the suffix always fits."""

    room = PROMPT_VERSION_MAX_LENGTH - len(suffix)
    if room <= 0:
        raise UniqueConstraintMigrationError(f"suffix too long for prompt version: {suffix}")
    return version[:room] + suffix


def dedupe_prompt_versions(connection: Connection) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Rename every duplicate but one per (category_key, stage, version).

    Keeper: the newest (max id) non-archived row; if the whole group is
    archived, the newest archived row. Others become ``<v>~archived-<id>``
    (archived) or ``<v>~dup-<id>`` (non-archived).
    """

    groups = connection.execute(text(
        "SELECT category_key, stage, version FROM prompt_versions "
        "GROUP BY category_key, stage, version HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 "
        "ORDER BY category_key, stage, version"
    )).all()
    renames: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for category_key, stage, version in groups:
        rows = connection.execute(text(
            "SELECT id, status FROM prompt_versions "
            "WHERE category_key = :category_key AND stage = :stage AND version = :version "
            "ORDER BY id"
        ), {"category_key": category_key, "stage": stage, "version": version}).all()
        live = [row for row in rows if row.status != "archived"]
        keeper = max(live or rows, key=lambda row: row.id)
        for row in rows:
            if row.id == keeper.id:
                continue
            tag = "archived" if row.status == "archived" else "dup"
            renamed = suffixed_version(version, f"~{tag}-{row.id}")
            clash = connection.execute(text(
                "SELECT id FROM prompt_versions WHERE category_key = :category_key "
                "AND stage = :stage AND version = :version"
            ), {"category_key": category_key, "stage": stage, "version": renamed}).first()
            if clash is not None:
                raise UniqueConstraintMigrationError(
                    f"prompt_versions id {row.id} 改名目标 {renamed!r} 已被 id {clash.id} 占用"
                )
            connection.execute(text(
                "UPDATE prompt_versions SET version = :renamed WHERE id = :id AND version = :version"
            ), {"renamed": renamed, "id": row.id, "version": version})
            entry = {
                "id": row.id,
                "status": row.status,
                "category_key": category_key,
                "stage": stage,
                "from_version": version,
                "to_version": renamed,
                "kept_id": keeper.id,
            }
            _record_audit(
                connection,
                event_key=f"schema-129:prompt_version_renamed:{row.id}",
                action="prompt_version_renamed",
                subject_type="prompt_version",
                subject_id=row.id,
                payload=entry,
            )
            logger.warning("schema 129: renamed duplicate prompt version %s", entry)
            renames.append(entry)
    return renames


def dedupe_model_node_bindings(connection: Connection) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Drop duplicate bindings per (node_key, COALESCE(category_key, '')).

    Keeps the lowest id: every reader and ``update_model_node`` select the
    binding with an unordered ``db.scalar`` and so have always hit the first
    row. The removed rows are copied in full into their audit event.
    """

    groups = connection.execute(text(
        "SELECT node_key, COALESCE(category_key, '') AS scope, MIN(id) AS keep_id "
        "FROM model_node_bindings GROUP BY node_key, COALESCE(category_key, '') "
        "HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 ORDER BY node_key, scope"
    )).all()
    removed: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node_key, scope, keep_id in groups:
        rows = connection.execute(text(
            "SELECT * FROM model_node_bindings WHERE node_key = :node_key "
            "AND COALESCE(category_key, '') = :scope AND id <> :keep_id ORDER BY id"
        ), {"node_key": node_key, "scope": scope, "keep_id": keep_id}).mappings().all()
        for row in rows:
            entry = {"row": {key: _plain(value) for key, value in row.items()}, "kept_id": keep_id}
            _record_audit(
                connection,
                event_key=f"schema-129:model_node_binding_removed:{row['id']}",
                action="model_node_binding_duplicate_removed",
                subject_type="model_node_binding",
                subject_id=row["id"],
                payload=entry,
            )
            connection.execute(
                text("DELETE FROM model_node_bindings WHERE id = :id"), {"id": row["id"]}
            )
            logger.warning("schema 129: removed duplicate model node binding %s", entry)
            removed.append(entry)
    return removed


def _plain(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, (datetime, date)):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return str(value)
    return value


def _record_audit(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    event_key: str,
    action: str,
    subject_type: str,
    subject_id: int,
    payload: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    from ..audit import canonical_json
    from ..models import AuditEvent

    if "audit_events" not in inspect(connection).get_table_names():
        return  # Historical partial SQLite fixtures; the migration log still has it.
    table = AuditEvent.__table__
    exists = connection.execute(
        select(table.c.id).where(table.c.event_key == event_key)
    ).first()
    if exists is not None:
        return
    connection.execute(table.insert().values(
        event_key=event_key,
        category="schema_migration",
        action=action,
        subject_type=subject_type,
        subject_id=str(subject_id),
        actor=MIGRATION_ACTOR,
        payload_json=canonical_json(payload),
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    ))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------- DDL


def _column_names(connection: Connection, table: str) -> set[str]:
    if connection.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        return {row[1] for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(f"PRAGMA table_xinfo({table})")}
    return {column["name"] for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(table)}


def _has_unique(connection: Connection, table: str, columns: tuple[str, ...], name: str) -> bool:
    if connection.dialect.name == "sqlite":
        # PRAGMA instead of the inspector: it also sees inline UNIQUE constraints
        # (sqlite_autoindex_*) and does not warn on expression indexes.
        for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(f"PRAGMA index_list({table})").all():
            if row[1] == name:
                return True
            if not row[2] or row[4]:  # not unique, or partial
                continue
            info = connection.exec_driver_sql(f"PRAGMA index_info('{row[1]}')").all()
            if tuple(item[2] for item in info) == columns:
                return True
        return False
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints(table):
        if item.get("name") == name or tuple(item["column_names"]) == columns:
            return True
    for item in inspector.get_indexes(table):
        if item.get("name") == name:
            return True
        if item.get("unique") and tuple(item["column_names"]) == columns:
            return True
    return False


def _ddl_sqlite(connection: Connection, tables: set[str]) -> None:
    from ..models import MODEL_NODE_CATEGORY_SCOPE_SQL, MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE, PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE

    if "model_node_bindings" in tables:
        # Generated columns are hidden from table_info; table_xinfo lists them.
        columns = {row[1] for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "PRAGMA table_xinfo(model_node_bindings)")}
        if "category_scope" not in columns:
            # SQLite ALTER TABLE can only add VIRTUAL generated columns
            # (create_all builds it STORED; only the storage differs).
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "ALTER TABLE model_node_bindings ADD COLUMN category_scope VARCHAR(40) "
                f"GENERATED ALWAYS AS ({MODEL_NODE_CATEGORY_SCOPE_SQL}) VIRTUAL")
        if not _has_unique(connection, "model_node_bindings", ("node_key", "category_scope"),
                           MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS {MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE} "
                "ON model_node_bindings (node_key, category_scope)")
    if "prompt_versions" in tables and not _has_unique(
        connection, "prompt_versions", ("category_key", "stage", "version"), PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE
    ):
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS {PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE} "
            "ON prompt_versions (category_key, stage, version)")


MYSQL_ADD_CATEGORY_SCOPE = (
    "ALTER TABLE `model_node_bindings` ADD COLUMN `category_scope` VARCHAR(40) "
    "CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs "
    "GENERATED ALWAYS AS (COALESCE(`category_key`, '')) STORED NOT NULL"
)
MYSQL_CREATE_SCOPE_UNIQUE = (
    "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX `uq_model_node_bindings_node_scope` "
    "ON `model_node_bindings` (`node_key`, `category_scope`)"
)
MYSQL_CREATE_PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE = (
    "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX `uq_prompt_versions_category_stage_version` "
    "ON `prompt_versions` (`category_key`, `stage`, `version`)"
)


def _ddl_mysql(connection: Connection, tables: set[str]) -> None:
    from ..models import MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE, PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE

    if "model_node_bindings" in tables:
        columns = {column["name"] for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("model_node_bindings")}
        if "category_scope" not in columns:
            # STORED + ADD COLUMN rebuilds the table (COPY); the table has a few dozen rows.
            connection.exec_driver_sql(MYSQL_ADD_CATEGORY_SCOPE)
        if not _has_unique(connection, "model_node_bindings", ("node_key", "category_scope"),
                           MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(MYSQL_CREATE_SCOPE_UNIQUE)
    if "prompt_versions" in tables and not _has_unique(
        connection, "prompt_versions", ("category_key", "stage", "version"), PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE
    ):
        connection.exec_driver_sql(MYSQL_CREATE_PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE)

===== FILE backend/app/migrations/upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction.py =====
"""Idempotent data upgrade from v3 grade contracts to rule deductions.

Existing EvaluationResult rows are deliberately untouched.  Only the four v3
config rows are upgraded in place: every existing dimension receives generic
placeholder rules when absent; the human-calibrated inspiration contract and
three legacy draft categories keep media penalty off.  Re-running after convergence is
a no-op and does not bump revisions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from ..category_evaluation_contract import (
    canonical_contract_hash,
    validate_category_evaluation_contract,
)
from ..dimension_composition import validate_subcategory_dimensions
from ..dimension_deduction_bridge import extract_dimension_deduction_rules
from ..dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from ..inspiration_category_seed import placeholder_deduction_rules
from ..category_evaluation_v3_revisions import sync_projected_revision
from ..models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config


TARGET_MEDIA_ENABLED = {
    "inspiration_image": False,
    "space_image": False,
    "material_image": False,
    "pdf_text": False,
}


def _upgrade_dimensions(
    subcategory_dimensions: dict[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], bool]:
    upgraded = deepcopy(subcategory_dimensions)
    changed = False
    for config in upgraded.values():
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            continue
        for group_name in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
            group = config.get(group_name)
            schema = group.get("schema_definition") if isinstance(group, dict) else None
            dimensions = schema.get("dimensions") if isinstance(schema, dict) else None
            if not isinstance(dimensions, list):
                continue
            for dimension in dimensions:
                if not isinstance(dimension, dict):
                    continue
                if not dimension.get("deduction_rules"):
                    dimension["deduction_rules"] = placeholder_deduction_rules(
                        str(dimension.get("label") or dimension.get("key") or "该维度")
                    )
                    changed = True
    return upgraded, changed


def upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction(db: Session) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Upgrade all existing target rows, committing is owned by the caller."""
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key.in_(TARGET_MEDIA_ENABLED)
        )
    ).all()
    changed_keys: list[str] = []
    for row in rows:
        contract = json.loads(row.contract_json or "{}")
        dimensions = json.loads(row.subcategory_dimensions_json or "{}")
        upgraded_dimensions, dimension_changed = _upgrade_dimensions(dimensions)

        media = contract.setdefault("common_modifiers", {}).setdefault(
            "media_type_penalty", {}
        )
        desired_enabled = TARGET_MEDIA_ENABLED[row.category_key]
        media_changed = media.get("enabled", True) != desired_enabled
        if "enabled" not in media or media_changed:
            media["enabled"] = desired_enabled

        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
        for config in upgraded_dimensions.values():
            validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)

        rules_mirror = extract_dimension_deduction_rules(upgraded_dimensions)
        serialized_contract = canonical_json(contract)
        serialized_dimensions = canonical_json(upgraded_dimensions)
        serialized_rules = canonical_json(rules_mirror)
        changed = any(
            (
                dimension_changed,
                media_changed,
                row.contract_json != serialized_contract,
                row.subcategory_dimensions_json != serialized_dimensions,
                row.dimension_deduction_rules_json != serialized_rules,
                row.media_penalty_enabled != desired_enabled,
            )
        )
        if not changed:
            continue
        # 这里原本直接改运行时行、手动 revision += 1，却不动
        # projected_revision_id——冻结版本仍指向升级前的内容。因为本函数在
        # seed_defaults 里、每次启动都跑，一旦真有行需要升级，下一次重启的
        # ensure_projected_revision 就会判定不一致而拒绝启动（2026-09-03 全站
        # 不可用就是这个形态）。改走 sync_projected_revision：追加一版冻结产物
        # 并把指针挪过去，两边始终成对。
        sync_projected_revision(
            db,
            row,
            display_name=row.display_name,
            status=row.status,
            contract_json=serialized_contract,
            classification_map_json=row.classification_map_json,
            subcategory_dimensions_json=serialized_dimensions,
            dimension_deduction_rules_json=serialized_rules,
            media_penalty_enabled=desired_enabled,
            contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(contract),
            actor=row.created_by,
        )
        changed_keys.append(row.category_key)

    db.flush()
    return {
        "target_count": len(rows),
        "changed_count": len(changed_keys),
        "changed_keys": sorted(changed_keys),
    }


def main() -> int:
    """Run the idempotent data upgrade against the configured database."""
    from ..database import init_database, session_scope

    init_database()
    with session_scope() as db:
        result = upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction(db)
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== FILE backend/app/migrations/wide_material_130.py =====
"""Schema 130: 面向下游的素材大宽表两张 + 同库下游视图两个（ADR-0111）。

SQLite（本地/测试）只建表；MySQL 额外建视图：
``downstream_material_current`` / ``downstream_material_history`` 选取 ADR 列集里
除 ``detail_json`` 以外的列，时间列用 ``CONVERT_TZ(col,'+00:00','+08:00') AS col_cst``
暴露东八区（库内仍存 UTC）。视图建在同一库；下游只读账号只授权这两个视图
（建账号是运维步骤，见 docs/verification/wide-material-20260927/README.md）。

可重入：表存在则跳过（checkfirst），视图 ``CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW``。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import MetaData, inspect
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

CURRENT_TABLE = "wide_material_current"
HISTORY_TABLE = "wide_material_history"
CURRENT_VIEW = "downstream_material_current"
HISTORY_VIEW = "downstream_material_history"

#: 视图不暴露的内部列。
HIDDEN_COLUMNS = frozenset({"detail_json", "row_hash"})


def _view_select_list(column_names: list[str], time_columns: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    parts: list[str] = []
    for name in column_names:
        if name in HIDDEN_COLUMNS:
            continue
        if name in time_columns:
            parts.append(f"CONVERT_TZ(`{name}`,'+00:00','+08:00') AS `{name}_cst`")
        else:
            parts.append(f"`{name}`")
    return ", ".join(parts)


def view_ddl() -> tuple[str, str]:
    """两个视图的 DDL（MySQL 语法）。放在函数里，避免在 import 期间碰 ORM 元数据。"""

    from ..wide_material_models import (
        TIME_COLUMNS,
        WideMaterialCurrent,
        WideMaterialHistory,
    )

    current_columns = [column.name for column in WideMaterialCurrent.__table__.columns]
    history_columns = [column.name for column in WideMaterialHistory.__table__.columns]
    current_sql = (
        f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW `{CURRENT_VIEW}` AS SELECT "
        + _view_select_list(current_columns, TIME_COLUMNS)
        + f" FROM `{CURRENT_TABLE}`"
    )
    history_sql = (
        f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW `{HISTORY_VIEW}` AS SELECT "
        + _view_select_list(history_columns, (*TIME_COLUMNS, "recorded_at"))
        + f" FROM `{HISTORY_TABLE}`"
    )
    return current_sql, history_sql


def migrate(connection: Connection, *, metadata: MetaData | None = None) -> None:
    """建两张表；MySQL 再建（或替换）两个视图。"""

    dialect = connection.dialect.name
    if metadata is None:
        # SQLite 路径直接用模型表定义；MySQL 调用方必须传 mysql_metadata()
        # （Text 列默认值、DATETIME(6)、utf8mb4 校对都在那里适配）。
        from ..wide_material_models import WideMaterialCurrent, WideMaterialHistory

        tables = [WideMaterialCurrent.__table__, WideMaterialHistory.__table__]
    else:
        tables = [metadata.tables[CURRENT_TABLE], metadata.tables[HISTORY_TABLE]]

    existing = set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
    for table in tables:
        if table.name in existing:
            continue
        table.create(bind=connection, checkfirst=True)

    if dialect != "mysql":
        return
    for statement in view_ddl():
        connection.exec_driver_sql(statement)

===== FILE backend/app/model_3d_su_category_seed.py =====
"""Seed the initial 3D/SU model-aesthetic category contract.

The builders are pure JSON assembly helpers.  ``seed_model_3d_su`` is the
small persistence adapter used by startup seeding; it only owns the new
``model_3d_su`` rows and never touches the parallel ``three_d`` profile.
"""

def build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract() -> dict[str, Any]:
    field_keys = (*PLATFORM_SEMANTIC_FIELD_KEYS, "form", "structure", "title")
    max_values = {
        "title": 1,
        "space": 1,
        "object": 3,
        "style": 2,
        "material": 3,
        "structural_features": 3,
        "architectural_element": 3,
        "soft_decoration": 3,
        "hard_decoration": 3,
        "color": 1,
        "form": 3,
        "structure": 3,
    }
    fields = {
        key: {
            "field_key": key,
            "cardinality": (
                "single" if key in {"title", "space", "color"} else "multi"
            ),
            "weight_semantics": (
                "relative_importance_level"
                if key in {"object", "material"}
                else "none"
            ),
            "localized": True,
            "vocabulary_owner": "tpeng-semantic-platform",
            "max_values": max_values[key],
            "default_value": [],
        }
        for key in field_keys
    }
    applicability = {
        key: "required" if key in {"space", "object", "style"} else "optional"
        for key in fields
    }
    quality_gates = {
        key: {
            "min_precision": 0.8,
            "min_recall": 0.7,
            "min_mapping_coverage": 0.9,
            "max_conflict_rate": 0.1,
        }
        for key in fields
    }
    field_supply = {
        key: {
            "field_key": key,
            "fact_namespace": "semantic",
            "object_grain": "asset",
            "production_method": (
                "source_direct"
                if key == "title"
                else "hybrid" if key == "space" else "model"
            ),
            "source_authority": (
                "upstream-source+tpeng-label-platform"
                if key == "title"
                else "tpeng-label-platform"
            ),
            "owner": (
                "tpeng-content-data"
                if key == "title"
                else "tpeng-semantic-platform"
            ),
            "freshness_sla_hours": 24,
            "null_semantics": [
                "not_applicable",
                "not_detected",
                "unknown",
            ],
            "rollback_strategy": "previous_release",
        }
        for key in fields
    }
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "tag-demand-contract-v2",
        "semantic_schema": {
            "schema_version": "semantic-tag-schema-v1",
            "fields": fields,
        },
        "source_identity": {
            "source_system": "aliyun_3d66_dw",
            "object_grain": "asset",
            "identity_fields": ["res_type", "ll_id"],
            "optional_disambiguator": "res_id",
            "version_field": "dt",
            "deletion_field": "is_delete",
            "uniqueness_status": "unverified",
            "verification_evidence_hash": None,
        },
        "field_supply": field_supply,
        "category_applicability": {MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY: applicability},
        "execution_variants": [
            {
                "site_scope": site_scope,
                "asset_scope": asset_scope,
                "locale": locale,
                "category_key": MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
                "prompt_variant": asset_scope,
                "prompt_version": (
                    f"model-3d-su-semantic-{asset_scope}-v1"
                    if site_scope == "domestic"
                    else f"model-3d-su-semantic-overseas-{asset_scope}-v1"
                ),
                "model_version": "fixture-model-v1",
                "field_applicability_overrides": (
                    {"space": "not_applicable"}
                    if asset_scope == "single"
                    else {}
                ),
            }
            for site_scope, locale in (("domestic", "zh"), ("overseas", "en"))
            for asset_scope in ("whole", "single")
        ],
        "quality_gates": quality_gates,
        "projection_targets": [
            {
                "target_key": "domestic_material_tags",
                "mode": "dry_run",
                "locale": "zh",
            },
            {
                "target_key": "overseas_material_tags",
                "mode": "dry_run",
                "locale": "en",
            },
            {"target_key": "knowledge_graph", "mode": "dry_run", "locale": "zh"},
            {"target_key": "knowledge_graph", "mode": "dry_run", "locale": "en"},
        ],
    }
    definition = validate_tag_demand_contract(payload).model_dump(mode="json")
    for variant in definition["execution_variants"]:
        variant.setdefault("field_applicability_overrides", {})
    return definition


def _seed_model_3d_su_semantic_contract(db: Session) -> TagDemandContract:
    definition = validate_tag_demand_contract(build_model_3d_su_semantic_contract())
    contract_hash = semantic_contract_hash(definition)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(TagDemandContract)
        .where(
            TagDemandContract.contract_key == MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY,
            TagDemandContract.contract_hash == contract_hash,
        )
        .order_by(TagDemandContract.version.asc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    latest = db.scalar(
        select(TagDemandContract)
        .where(TagDemandContract.contract_key == MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY)
        .order_by(TagDemandContract.version.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    row = TagDemandContract(
        contract_key=MODEL_3D_SU_SEMANTIC_CONTRACT_KEY,
        version=(latest.version + 1) if latest else 1,
        status="draft",
        definition_json=canonical_json(definition.model_dump(mode="json")),
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        created_by=MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _rule(rule_id: str, description: str, deduction: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "rule_id": rule_id,
        "description": description,
        "deduction": deduction,
        "tags": ["模型美感"],
    }


def _dimension(
    key: str,
    label: str,
    weight: float,
    *,
    minor: str,
    obvious: str,
    severe: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "key": key,
        "label": label,
        "weight": weight,
        "grade_points": dict(_LINEAR_GRADE_POINTS),
        "deduction_rules": [
            _rule("minor_defect", f"微瑕：{minor}", 20),
            _rule("obvious_defect", f"明显缺陷：{obvious}", 50),
            _rule("severe_defect", f"严重硬伤：{severe}", 80),
        ],
    }


def _track_dimensions(track_key: str, weights: list[float]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    details = _dimension(
        "model_detail",
        "模型细节",
        weights[0],
        minor="少量边缘倒角、接缝、螺丝或褶皱实体化不足",
        obvious="核心棱角无倒角，缝线/卡扣/沟槽主要依赖平面贴图",
        severe="主体比例畸形，大量结构细节缺失或软质材料呈硬质平板",
    )
    material = _dimension(
        "material_rendering",
        "质感渲染",
        weights[1],
        minor="粗糙度或微观印痕略有偏差",
        obvious="材质属性混淆、纹理重复或高光过曝",
        severe="贴图模糊/UV 拉伸，反光参数混乱且材质与现实属性脱节",
    )
    lighting = _dimension(
        "lighting",
        "光感表现",
        weights[2],
        minor="暗部细节略欠缺或局部补光略突兀",
        obvious="阴影单薄、边缘生硬、无来源亮光或接触阴影缺失",
        severe="光线违背物理衰减，出现大面积过曝/死黑或多光源冲突",
    )
    design = _dimension(
        "design_trend",
        "设计感及流行度",
        weights[3],
        minor="风格混搭生硬、设计平庸，或功能比例略别扭",
        obvious="整体过时、造型同质化，或功能布局明显不合理",
        severe="造型严重违背审美/制造/使用逻辑，形成虚假功能设计",
    )
    composition = _dimension(
        "visual_composition",
        "视觉构图",
        weights[4],
        minor="主体重心略偏或边缘有少量无关物件",
        obvious="构图过满/过空，主体裁切或被遮挡超过约 30%",
        severe="无视觉重心、元素堆砌，主体被杂乱背景淹没",
    )
    dimensions = [details, material, lighting, design, composition]
    if abs(sum(item["weight"] for item in dimensions) - 1.0) > 1e-9:
        raise ValueError(f"{track_key} 维度权重必须合计为 1.0")
    return {
        "format_version": SUBCATEGORY_DIMENSIONS_FORMAT_VERSION,
        "sub_category_key": track_key,
        "dimension_max": 100,
        "b_aesthetic_foundation": {
            "format_version": "b-aesthetic-foundation-v1",
            "source": "call_b",
            "score_range": [0, 100],
            "evidence_required": True,
        },
        "grade_output_contract": {
            "format_version": "dimension-grade-output-v1",
            "require_exact_keys": True,
            "evidence_required": True,
        },
        "common_group": {
            "group_weight": 1.0,
            "schema_definition": {
                "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
                "schema_key": MODEL_3D_SU_SCHEMA_KEY,
                "version": MODEL_3D_SU_SCHEMA_VERSION,
                "dimensions": dimensions,
            },
        },
        "specific_group": {
            "schema_definition": {
                "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
                "schema_key": f"{MODEL_3D_SU_SCHEMA_KEY}_specific",
                "version": MODEL_3D_SU_SCHEMA_VERSION,
                "dimensions": [],
            }
        },
    }


def _normalize_weight_points(points: list[int]) -> list[float]:
    """Convert document weight points into the strict contract fraction form."""
    total = sum(points)
    if total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("维度权重点数之和必须大于 0")
    return [point / total for point in points]

def build_model_3d_su_classification_map() -> dict[str, Any]:
    mapping = {
        "家装": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
        "工装": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
        "景观": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
        "建筑": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
        "空间": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
        "软装": TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING,
        "家具": TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING,
        "家居": TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING,
        "摆件": TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING,
        "电子电器": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "五金配件": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "交通工具": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "厨卫日常用品": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "工业机械与零部件": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "办公实用物品": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "设施器械": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        "功能性模型": TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
    }
    result = {
        "format_version": CLASSIFICATION_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION,
        "min_confidence": 0.7,
        "category_to_subcategory": mapping,
        "out_of_scope_subcategory": TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
    }
    validate_classification_map(
        result,
        valid_track_keys={
            TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING,
            TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING,
            TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
        },
    )
    return result


def build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """3D/SU 上游类型前置短路默认内容：全景图固定 L2（《3D&SU模型分类定级规则》），跳过调用 A/B。"""
    from .upstream_type_short_circuit import validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content

    content: dict[str, Any] = {
        "format_version": "upstream-type-short-circuit-v1",
        "enabled": True,
        "field_path": "material_type",
        "priority": 900,
        "skip_roles": ["classification", "grading", "level_matching"],
        "rules": [
            {
                "key": "panorama",
                "label": "全景图",
                "match_any": ["全景图", "全景", "panorama", "panoramic"],
                "level": "L2",
                "reason": "全景图缩略图角度不好但模型空间完整，规则固定 L2，不进美感评级",
            }
        ],
    }
    validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(content)
    return content


def build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    configs = {
        TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING: _track_dimensions(
            TRACK_SPACE_BUILDING, [0.20, 0.25, 0.20, 0.20, 0.15]
        ),
        TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING: _track_dimensions(
            TRACK_SOFT_FURNISHING, [0.25, 0.25, 0.20, 0.20, 0.10]
        ),
        TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL: _track_dimensions(
            # The source document expresses this track as 35:25:20:15:10
            # points (105 total).  Preserve that relative priority while
            # serializing the v3 contract's required sum-to-one fractions.
            TRACK_FUNCTIONAL_MODEL,
            _normalize_weight_points([35, 25, 20, 15, 10]),
        ),
    }
    for config in configs.values():
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
    return configs


def model_3d_su_pipeline() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return validate_pipeline_config(
        {
            "schema_version": "category-pipeline-v1",
            "input_kind": "image",
            "allowed_suffixes": [".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".gif"],
            "processors": [
                {"module": "image.prepare", "enabled": True, "config": {}},
                {"module": "image.animated_contact_sheet", "enabled": True, "config": {}},
            ],
            "prompt_mode": "ab",
            "prompt_context": {
                "instruction": "按 3D/SU 模型合同区分空间建筑、软装家具和功能性模型；SU 白模/线框仅标记未渲染，不因白底或二维码触发红线。"
            },
            "dimensions": {"enabled": True, "mode": "all", "enabled_keys": []},
            "model_nodes": {
                "evaluation_main": True,
                "pdf_summary": False,
                "optimization": True,
                "benchmark": True,
                "diagnostic": True,
            },
        }
    )


def _read_prompt(settings: Any, filename: str) -> str:
    project_root = Path(settings.project_root)
    candidates = (
        project_root / "backend" / "prompts" / filename,
        project_root / "prompts" / filename,
        _ASSET_DIR / filename,
    )
    for path in candidates:
        if path.is_file():
            return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    searched = ", ".join(str(path) for path in candidates)
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到 3D/SU 提示词 {filename}，已检查：{searched}")


def _seed_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    stage: str,
    version: str,
    name: str,
    system_prompt: str,
    change_note: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    expected_identity = {
        "stage": stage,
        "category_key": MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
        "pipeline_scope": "shared",
        "name": name,
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": "",
        "rubric_version": MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION,
        "status": "published",
        "source": "imported",
        "change_note": change_note,
        "created_by": MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
    }

    def matches(row: PromptVersion) -> bool:
        return all(
            getattr(row, field_name) == expected_value
            for field_name, expected_value in expected_identity.items()
        )

    for attempt in range(100):
        candidate_version = (
            version if attempt == 0 else f"{version}-system-seed-{attempt}"
        )
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(PromptVersion.version == candidate_version)
        )
        if existing is not None:
            if matches(existing):
                return existing
            if existing.created_by in _MODEL_3D_SU_SYSTEM_OWNERS:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"冻结提示词 {candidate_version} 已存在但内容或身份不匹配"
                )
            # Operators may legitimately use any version string. Keep their
            # immutable row and allocate a deterministic platform-owned alias.
            continue

        row = PromptVersion(
            stage=stage,
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            pipeline_scope="shared",
            name=name,
            version=candidate_version,
            system_prompt=system_prompt,
            user_prompt="",
            rubric_version=MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION,
            status="published",
            source="imported",
            change_note=change_note,
            created_by=MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        return row
    raise RuntimeError(f"无法为 {version} 分配可用的系统提示词版本")


def seed_model_3d_su(db: Session, settings: Any) -> None:
    """Idempotently seed the active profile and v3 contract for 3D/SU."""
    primary = db.scalar(
        select(ModelConfig).where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True)).order_by(ModelConfig.id.asc())
    )
    if primary is None:
        raise RuntimeError("缺少 active 评测模型，无法启用 3D/SU 模型类目")

    _seed_model_3d_su_semantic_contract(db)

    prompt_a = _seed_prompt(
        db,
        stage="A",
        version=MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_A_VERSION,
        name="3D/SU 模型分类与字段预检",
        system_prompt=_read_prompt(settings, "model_3d_su_call_a_v3.txt"),
        change_note="2026-08-19 3D/SU 五维 grade 与详细扣分合同 v3 调用 A；输出平台通用字段与类目标记。",
    )
    prompt_b = _seed_prompt(
        db,
        stage="B",
        version=MODEL_3D_SU_CALL_B_VERSION,
        name="3D/SU 模型五维美感评审",
        system_prompt=_read_prompt(settings, "model_3d_su_call_b_v3.txt"),
        change_note="2026-08-19 3D/SU 五维 grade 与详细扣分合同 v3 调用 B；输出严格五维等级与证据，不输出最终等级。",
    )

    pipeline = model_3d_su_pipeline()
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
        )
    )
    profile_values = {
        "display_name": "3D & SU 模型",
        "description": "model-3d-su-v4：以调用B aesthetic_score为初始分，按三赛道五维合同执行命中扣分；SU未渲染和风险字段只做标记。",
        "status": "active",
        "allowed_mime_types_json": canonical_json(
            ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif"]
        ),
        "preprocess_config_json": canonical_json({"preprocess": "image", "su_unrendered_marker": True}),
        "pipeline_config_json": canonical_json(pipeline),
        "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id,
        "prompt_b_id": prompt_b.id,
        "model_config_id": primary.id,
        "rubric_version": MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION,
        "dimension_schema_key": None,
        "dimension_schema_version": None,
        "created_by": MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
    }
    if profile is None:
        db.add(EvaluationCategoryProfile(category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY, pipeline_revision=1, **profile_values))
    elif profile.created_by not in _MODEL_3D_SU_SYSTEM_OWNERS:
        raise RuntimeError("model_3d_su 已存在运营/外部类目配置，拒绝覆盖")
    elif profile.rubric_version not in {*_MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSIONS, ""}:
        raise RuntimeError("model_3d_su 已存在未知 rubric 类目配置，拒绝覆盖")
    else:
        profile.prompt_a_id = prompt_a.id
        profile.prompt_b_id = prompt_b.id
        profile.model_config_id = profile.model_config_id or primary.id
        profile.created_by = MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY
        if profile.pipeline_config_json in {"", "{}"}:
            profile.pipeline_config_json = profile_values["pipeline_config_json"]
            profile.pipeline_revision = (profile.pipeline_revision or 0) + 1
        profile.rubric_version = MODEL_3D_SU_RUBRIC_VERSION

    contract = build_model_3d_su_contract(
        call_a_version=prompt_a.version,
        call_b_version=prompt_b.version,
    )
    classification_map = build_model_3d_su_classification_map()
    subcategory_dimensions = build_model_3d_su_subcategory_dimensions()
    contract_json = canonical_json(contract)
    config_values = {
        "display_name": "3D & SU 模型",
        "status": "active",
        "contract_json": contract_json,
        "classification_map_json": canonical_json(classification_map),
        "subcategory_dimensions_json": canonical_json(subcategory_dimensions),
        "dimension_deduction_rules_json": canonical_json(
            extract_dimension_deduction_rules(subcategory_dimensions)
        ),
        "media_penalty_enabled": False,
        "contract_hash": canonical_contract_hash(contract),
        "created_by": MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
    }
    row = db.scalar(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        row = CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
            category_key=MODEL_3D_SU_CATEGORY_KEY,
            revision=1,
            **config_values,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
        ensure_projected_revision(db, row)
        return
    if row.created_by not in _MODEL_3D_SU_SYSTEM_OWNERS:
        raise RuntimeError("model_3d_su v3 合同已存在运营/外部版本，拒绝覆盖")
    try:
        existing_contract = json.loads(row.contract_json or "{}")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
        existing_contract = {}
    if existing_contract.get("spec_version") == MODEL_3D_SU_SPEC_VERSION:
        mismatched_fields = [
            field_name
            for field_name, expected_value in config_values.items()
            if getattr(row, field_name) != expected_value
        ]
        if mismatched_fields:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "model_3d_su v3 冻结合同内容或身份不匹配："
                + "、".join(mismatched_fields)
            )
        ensure_projected_revision(db, row)
        return
    sync_projected_revision(
        db,
        row,
        display_name=config_values["display_name"],
        status=config_values["status"],
        contract_json=config_values["contract_json"],
        classification_map_json=config_values["classification_map_json"],
        subcategory_dimensions_json=config_values["subcategory_dimensions_json"],
        dimension_deduction_rules_json=config_values[
            "dimension_deduction_rules_json"
        ],
        media_penalty_enabled=config_values["media_penalty_enabled"],
        contract_hash=config_values["contract_hash"],
        actor=MODEL_3D_SU_CREATED_BY,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/model_3d_su_legacy_projection.py =====
"""把 3D/SU 最新机制的调用 A 输出投影为旧豆包链路的单值字段。

旧链路（3d66.new-cli ai:resLLM）的 ``reason`` / ``hwreason`` 是汉字单值枚举，未触发为空串；
新版调用 A 把这些枚举拆成 ``model_3d_su_fields`` 下的独立字段（触发输出短语、未触发为空）。
这里只做确定性的压缩与文案映射，不做任何模型判断；``score`` 取机制最终分，不取 A 的参考分。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any, Mapping

# 旧链路 hwreason 枚举顺序即优先级：多项同时命中时取最靠前的一项。
HWREASON_PRIORITY: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    ("brand_trademark", "有知名品牌logo/商标"),
    ("brand_name", "有知名品牌名称"),
    ("chinese", "有明显中文字样"),
    ("film_ip", "是影视/综艺相关IP"),
    ("celebrities", "是名人/明星形象"),
    ("game_ip", "是游戏/动漫相关IP"),
    ("other_ip", "是热门IP形象"),  # 新字段文案为“是其它热门IP”，旧枚举为“是热门IP形象”
    ("qr_code", "有二维码"),
    ("landmark", "有地标建筑"),
    ("religion", "有宗教元素"),
)

RISK_FIELD_PHRASES: dict[str, str] = {
    "watermark": "有水印",
    "unrendered": "是未渲染模型图",
    **{key: phrase for key, phrase in HWREASON_PRIORITY},
}
# 调用 A 对 other_ip 的提示词文案
RISK_FIELD_PHRASES["other_ip"] = "是其它热门IP"

REASON_WATERMARK = "有水印"
REASON_UNRENDERED = "是未渲染模型图"
REASON_ILLEGAL = "是色情暴力违规涉政内容"
_ILLEGAL_MARKERS = ("色情", "暴力", "违规", "涉政", "illegal", "nsfw", "political")


# 否定表述（“无违规内容”“未发现涉政”“no nsfw”）不是违规信号；先整体剔除再找标记词。
_NEGATED_ILLEGAL = re.compile(
    r"(?:无|没有|未发现|未见|未检测到|未涉及|不含|不涉及|不存在|并非|非|不)"
    r"(?:任何|明显|相关|的|是|属于|包含|涉及)*"
    r"(?:色情|暴力|违规|涉政|[、/与和及]|内容|元素)+"
    r"|\b(?:no|non|not|without)[\s_-]*(?:illegal|nsfw|political)\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)


def _text(value: Any) -> str:
    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""


def _triggered(fields: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_text(fields.get(key)))


def _illegal_signal(stage_a: Mapping[str, Any], fields: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    candidates: list[str] = []
    high_risk = fields.get("high_risk")
    if isinstance(high_risk, list):
        candidates.extend(str(item) for item in high_risk)
    production = stage_a.get("production_fields")
    if isinstance(production, Mapping):
        reason = production.get("reason")
        if isinstance(reason, list):
            candidates.extend(str(item) for item in reason)
        elif isinstance(reason, str):
            candidates.append(reason)
        image_defects = production.get("image_defects")
        if isinstance(image_defects, list):
            candidates.extend(str(item) for item in image_defects)
        else:
            candidates.append(_text(image_defects))
    lowered = [_NEGATED_ILLEGAL.sub(" ", item).lower() for item in candidates]
    return any(marker.lower() in item for item in lowered for marker in _ILLEGAL_MARKERS)


def legacy_3d_su_projection(
    stage_a: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    score: Any = None,
    res_type: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """返回旧链路字段视图：title/seotitle/category/style/tags/cons/design/score/reason/hwreason。

    - ``res_type=1``（Max）时旧枚举不含“是未渲染模型图”，未渲染信号降级为空串并在 ``notes`` 说明；
    - ``res_type`` 未知时保留“是未渲染模型图”，由下游按类型再裁剪；
    - ``score`` 为机制最终分；缺失时为 ``None``，不回退到调用 A 的参考分。
    """

    stage = stage_a if isinstance(stage_a, Mapping) else {}
    fields = stage.get("model_3d_su_fields")
    fields = fields if isinstance(fields, Mapping) else {}
    production = stage.get("production_fields")
    production = production if isinstance(production, Mapping) else {}
    notes: list[str] = []

    reason = ""
    if _triggered(fields, "watermark"):
        reason = REASON_WATERMARK
    elif _triggered(fields, "unrendered"):
        if res_type == 1:
            notes.append("Max 旧链路 reason 枚举不含“是未渲染模型图”，已降级为空串")
        else:
            reason = REASON_UNRENDERED
    elif _illegal_signal(stage, fields):
        reason = REASON_ILLEGAL
    if not fields:
        notes.append("调用 A 输出缺少 model_3d_su_fields，reason/hwreason 无法投影")

    hwreason = ""
    triggered_hw = [phrase for key, phrase in HWREASON_PRIORITY if _triggered(fields, key)]
    if triggered_hw:
        hwreason = triggered_hw[0]
        if len(triggered_hw) > 1:
            notes.append("hwreason 同时命中：" + "、".join(triggered_hw) + "；按旧枚举顺序取第一项")

    tags = production.get("tags")
    tags_list = [str(item) for item in tags] if isinstance(tags, list) else []
    final_score = score if isinstance(score, int) and not isinstance(score, bool) else None
    if final_score is None:
        notes.append("机制最终分缺失，未回退到调用 A 参考分")
    return {
        "schema_version": "model-3d-su-legacy-projection-v1",
        "res_type": res_type,
        "title": _text(production.get("title")),
        "seotitle": _text(production.get("seotitle")),
        "category": _text(production.get("category")),
        "style": _text(production.get("style")),
        "tags": tags_list,
        "cons": _text(production.get("cons")),
        "design": _text(production.get("design")),
        "score": final_score,
        "reason": reason,
        "hwreason": hwreason,
        "triggered_hwreason": triggered_hw,
        "notes": notes,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/model_3d_su_strict_release.py =====
"""把严格结构化输出合同挂到 3D/SU 最新美感评测机制并生成新版本。

- 调用 A：``model_3d_su_call_a``（含 V29 分类定级表字段、独立风险字段、机器标记）；
- 调用 B：按工作副本冻结的 ``dimension_scoring`` 规则模块动态生成（rule_id 枚举、confidence、evidence）。

预览模式零写入；应用模式保存工作副本并生成一个新的不可变评测机制版本，不激活、不切换增量绑定。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .dimension_deduction_bridge import build_dimension_deduction_output_contract
from .models import RuleModuleVersion, WorkflowDefinition
from .strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract
from .workflow_working_copies import (
    generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
    require_workflow_working_copy,
    save_workflow_working_copy,
)

WORKFLOW_KEY = "model_3d_su.evaluation"


class Model3dSuStrictReleaseError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _prompt_node(nodes: list[dict[str, Any]], *, node_id: str, role: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for node in nodes:
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt" and node.get("node_instance_id") == node_id:
            return node
    for node in nodes:
        config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping) else {}
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt" and config.get("execution_role") == role:
            return node
    raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError(
        "model_3d_su_prompt_node_missing", f"工作副本缺少提示词节点 {node_id}（{role}）"
    )


def _dimension_scoring_content(db: Session, nodes: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    matches = [
        node for node in nodes
        if node.get("kind") == "rule_module"
        and isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping)
        and node["config"].get("module_type_key") == "dimension_scoring"
    ]
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError(
            "model_3d_su_dimension_scoring_ambiguous", "工作副本必须恰好有一个 dimension_scoring 规则模块节点"
        )
    version_id = matches[0]["config"].get("rule_module_version_id")
    row = db.get(RuleModuleVersion, version_id) if isinstance(version_id, int) else None
    if row is None:
        raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError(
            "model_3d_su_dimension_scoring_missing", "dimension_scoring 规则模块版本不存在"
        )
    content = json.loads(row.content_json or "{}")
    if not isinstance(content, dict):
        raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError(
            "model_3d_su_dimension_scoring_invalid", "dimension_scoring 规则模块内容损坏"
        )
    return content


def _summarize(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    schema = contract.get("schema") if isinstance(contract.get("schema"), Mapping) else {}
    props = schema.get("properties") if isinstance(schema.get("properties"), Mapping) else {}
    summary: dict[str, Any] = {"name": contract.get("name"), "fields": sorted(props)}
    dimensions = props.get("dimensions", {}).get("properties") if isinstance(props.get("dimensions"), Mapping) else None
    if isinstance(dimensions, Mapping):
        summary["dimensions"] = {
            key: {
                "rule_ids": (
                    ((value.get("properties") or {}).get("hit_rules") or {}).get("items", {}).get("properties", {}).get("rule_id", {}).get("enum")
                )
            }
            for key, value in dimensions.items()
        }
    risk = props.get("model_3d_su_fields", {}).get("properties") if isinstance(props.get("model_3d_su_fields"), Mapping) else None
    if isinstance(risk, Mapping):
        summary["risk_fields"] = sorted(risk)
    return summary


def attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    apply: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == WORKFLOW_KEY))
    if definition is None:
        raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError("model_3d_su_workflow_missing", f"工作流 {WORKFLOW_KEY} 不存在")
    working = require_workflow_working_copy(db, definition.id)
    manifest = json.loads(working.manifest_json)
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise Model3dSuStrictReleaseError("model_3d_su_manifest_invalid", "工作副本节点列表损坏")
    prompt_a = _prompt_node(nodes, node_id="prompt_a", role="classification")
    prompt_b = _prompt_node(nodes, node_id="prompt_b", role="grading")
    contract_a = strict_output_contract("model_3d_su", "A", evaluation_contract={})
    contract_b = build_dimension_deduction_output_contract(
        _dimension_scoring_content(db, nodes),
        name="model_3d_su_call_b",
        description="3D/SU 五维规则命中与美感基础分（严格结构化输出）",
    )
    before = {
        "prompt_a": (prompt_a["config"].get("output_contract") or {}).get("mode"),
        "prompt_b": (prompt_b["config"].get("output_contract") or {}).get("mode"),
    }
    for node, contract in ((prompt_a, contract_a), (prompt_b, contract_b)):
        config = node["config"]
        config["output_contract"] = contract
        config["structured_output"] = True
        # 合同变化后由生成版本重新冻结快照
        config.pop("output_contract_snapshot", None)
    receipt: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "model-3d-su-strict-release-v1",
        "mode": "apply" if apply else "preview",
        "workflow_key": WORKFLOW_KEY,
        "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
        "working_copy_lock_version": working.lock_version,
        "before": before,
        "contract_a": _summarize(contract_a),
        "contract_b": _summarize(contract_b),
    }
    if not apply:
        return receipt
    saved = save_workflow_working_copy(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
        manifest=manifest,
        actor=actor,
    )
    version = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        expected_lock_version=saved.lock_version,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    try:
        report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        report = {}
    receipt.update(
        {
            "workflow_version_id": version.id,
            "workflow_version": version.version,
            "validation_ok": report.get("ok") if isinstance(report, dict) else None,
            "validation_errors": report.get("errors") if isinstance(report, dict) else None,
        }
    )
    return receipt

===== FILE backend/app/model_3d_su_upstream_gate_release.py =====
"""把「上游类型前置短路」节点接进 3D/SU 最新评测机制并生成新版本。

步骤：确保类目下有 ``upstream_type_short_circuit`` 规则模块资产（默认内容：全景图 → L2）并生成模块版本；
在工作副本里把节点插到 ``upstream`` 与其后继之间（已存在则只刷新模块版本）；保存工作副本并生成新的
不可变评测机制版本。预览模式零写入；应用模式不激活、不切增量绑定。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .evaluation_components import (
    create_component_asset,
    generate_component_version,
    get_component_working_copy,
    save_component_working_copy,
)
from .model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit
from .models import WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowNodeAsset
from .upstream_type_short_circuit import (
    UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE,
    short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type,
)
from .workflow_working_copies import (
    generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
    require_workflow_working_copy,
    save_workflow_working_copy,
)

WORKFLOW_KEY = "model_3d_su.evaluation"
CATEGORY_KEY = "model_3d_su"
ASSET_KEY = "upstream_type_short_circuit"
NODE_ID = "upstream_type_short_circuit_0"
DISPLAY_NAME = "上游类型前置短路"


class Model3dSuUpstreamGateError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _ensure_module_version(
    db: Session, *, content: Mapping[str, Any], actor: str, apply: bool
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    asset = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == CATEGORY_KEY,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == ASSET_KEY,
        )
    )
    created = False
    if asset is None:
        asset = create_component_asset(
            db,
            category_key=CATEGORY_KEY,
            asset_key=ASSET_KEY,
            kind="rule_module",
            display_name="3D&SU 上游类型前置短路",
            module_type_key=UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE,
            description="按上游素材类型（全景图）直接定级，跳过调用 A/B",
            created_by=actor,
        )
        created = True
    elif asset.module_type_key != UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE:
        raise Model3dSuUpstreamGateError(
            "model_3d_su_upstream_gate_asset_conflict",
            f"资产 {ASSET_KEY} 已存在但模块类型是 {asset.module_type_key}",
        )
    working = get_component_working_copy(db, asset.id)
    working = save_component_working_copy(
        db,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
        content=content,
        updated_by=actor,
    )
    ref = generate_component_version(db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by=actor)
    return {
        "asset_id": asset.id,
        "asset_created": created,
        "rule_module_version_id": ref.id,
        "rule_module_version": ref.version,
        "content_hash": ref.content_hash,
        "working_copy_lock_version": working.lock_version,
    }


def _upstream_node_id(nodes: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    for node in nodes:
        if node.get("kind") == "upstream_connector":
            return str(node["node_instance_id"])
    raise Model3dSuUpstreamGateError(
        "model_3d_su_upstream_missing", "工作副本缺少读取上游节点"
    )


def _insert_gate_node(
    manifest: dict[str, Any], *, rule_module_version_id: int
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    nodes = manifest.get("nodes")
    edges = manifest.get("edges")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not isinstance(edges, list):
        raise Model3dSuUpstreamGateError("model_3d_su_manifest_invalid", "工作副本节点或边损坏")
    upstream_id = _upstream_node_id(nodes)
    existing = next((n for n in nodes if n.get("node_instance_id") == NODE_ID), None)
    if existing is not None:
        existing["config"]["rule_module_version_id"] = rule_module_version_id
        existing["config"].pop("component_snapshot", None)
        return {"inserted": False, "node_instance_id": NODE_ID, "after": upstream_id}
    successors = [e for e in edges if e.get("from") == upstream_id]
    if len(successors) != 1:
        raise Model3dSuUpstreamGateError(
            "model_3d_su_upstream_edges_ambiguous",
            f"读取上游节点必须恰好有一条出边，当前 {len(successors)} 条",
        )
    next_id = successors[0]["to"]
    node = {
        "node_instance_id": NODE_ID,
        "kind": "rule_module",
        "display_name": DISPLAY_NAME,
        "config": {
            "asset_key": ASSET_KEY,
            "module_type_key": UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE,
            "rule_module_version_id": rule_module_version_id,
            "input_bindings": {"upstream_input": f"{upstream_id}.output"},
            "scope": "default",
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0},
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "trace_id"],
    }
    upstream_index = next(i for i, n in enumerate(nodes) if n.get("node_instance_id") == upstream_id)
    nodes.insert(upstream_index + 1, node)
    edges.remove(successors[0])
    edges.insert(0, {"from": upstream_id, "to": NODE_ID})
    edges.insert(1, {"from": NODE_ID, "to": next_id})
    return {"inserted": True, "node_instance_id": NODE_ID, "after": upstream_id, "before": next_id}


def attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    apply: bool = False,
    content: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == WORKFLOW_KEY))
    if definition is None:
        raise Model3dSuUpstreamGateError("model_3d_su_workflow_missing", f"工作流 {WORKFLOW_KEY} 不存在")
    module_content = dict(content) if content is not None else build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit()
    working = require_workflow_working_copy(db, definition.id)
    manifest = json.loads(working.manifest_json)
    receipt: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "model-3d-su-upstream-gate-release-v1",
        "mode": "apply" if apply else "preview",
        "workflow_key": WORKFLOW_KEY,
        "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
        "working_copy_lock_version": working.lock_version,
        "module_content": module_content,
        "derived_short_circuit_rules": short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type(module_content),
    }
    module = _ensure_module_version(db, content=module_content, actor=actor, apply=apply)
    receipt["module"] = module
    receipt["node"] = _insert_gate_node(manifest, rule_module_version_id=int(module["rule_module_version_id"]))
    receipt["node_order"] = [n.get("node_instance_id") for n in manifest["nodes"]]
    if not apply:
        return receipt
    saved = save_workflow_working_copy(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
        manifest=manifest,
        actor=actor,
    )
    version = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        expected_lock_version=saved.lock_version,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    try:
        report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        report = {}
    receipt.update(
        {
            "workflow_version_id": version.id,
            "workflow_version": version.version,
            "validation_ok": report.get("ok") if isinstance(report, dict) else None,
            "validation_errors": report.get("errors") if isinstance(report, dict) else None,
        }
    )
    return receipt

===== FILE backend/app/model_media_usage.py =====
"""Prepare replayable target/anchor media without exposing source locations."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Mapping, Sequence

from PIL import Image, UnidentifiedImageError
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .prepared_media_memo import sha256_file_memo
from .lossless_image_transport import (
    LosslessImageTransportError,
    LosslessTransportResult,
    prepare_lossless_transport,
)
from .media import (
    MODEL_DELIVERY_SHORT_EDGE,
    anchor_reference_cache_key,
    anchor_reference_cache_path,
    model_delivery_cache_path,
    model_delivery_size,
    prepare_anchor_reference_image,
    prepare_model_image,
    prepare_vision_geometry,
)
from .models import WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowModelMediaUsage
from .model_pricing import (
    MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS,
    ModelPricingError,
    parse_pricing_contract,
    select_pricing_tier,
)
from .vision_input_policy import (
    CustomPreprocess,
    DetailFidelity,
    OriginalPreprocess,
    PixelLimitFidelity,
    ShortEdgePreprocess,
    VisionCapabilityProfile,
    VisionFidelity,
    VisionInputPolicy,
    VisionRolePolicy,
    estimate_image_tokens,
    vision_policy_hash,
)


_SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
MediaRole = Literal["target", "anchor"]


class ModelMediaPreparationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.evidence = dict(evidence or {})


class ModelMediaUsageError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelMediaPolicy:
    version: Literal["legacy-default", "vision-input-policy-v1"] = "legacy-default"
    target: VisionRolePolicy | None = None
    anchor: VisionRolePolicy | None = None
    target_max_frames: int = 1
    delivery_short_edge: int = MODEL_DELIVERY_SHORT_EDGE

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= self.target_max_frames <= 24:
            raise ValueError("target_max_frames 必须在 1 到 24 之间")
        if not 64 <= self.delivery_short_edge <= 4096:
            raise ValueError("delivery_short_edge 必须在 64 到 4096 之间")
        if self.version == "legacy-default":
            if self.target is not None or self.anchor is not None:
                raise ValueError("历史图片策略不能携带显式角色配置")
            return
        if self.version != "vision-input-policy-v1":
            raise ValueError("图片策略版本不受支持")
        if not isinstance(self.target, VisionRolePolicy) or not isinstance(
            self.anchor,
            VisionRolePolicy,
        ):
            raise ValueError("显式图片策略必须配置 target 和 anchor")

    @classmethod
    def legacy(
        cls,
        *,
        target_max_frames: int = 1,
        delivery_short_edge: int = MODEL_DELIVERY_SHORT_EDGE,
    ) -> ModelMediaPolicy:
        return cls(
            version="legacy-default",
            target_max_frames=target_max_frames,
            delivery_short_edge=delivery_short_edge,
        )

    @property
    def is_legacy_default(self) -> bool:
        return self.version == "legacy-default"

    def role_policy(self, role: MediaRole) -> VisionRolePolicy:
        resolved = self.target if role == "target" else self.anchor
        if self.is_legacy_default or resolved is None:
            raise ValueError("历史图片策略没有显式角色配置")
        return resolved

    def as_vision_policy(
        self,
        capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    ) -> VisionInputPolicy:
        if self.is_legacy_default:
            raise ValueError("历史图片策略不生成新策略哈希")
        return VisionInputPolicy(
            version="vision-input-policy-v1",
            provider_profile=capability.profile_key,
            roles={
                "target": self.role_policy("target"),
                "anchor": self.role_policy("anchor"),
            },
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelMediaItem:
    role: MediaRole
    ordinal: int
    source_path: Path = field(repr=False)
    mime_type: str
    content_sha256: str
    asset_id: int | None = None
    anchor_level: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreparedModelMedia:
    role: MediaRole
    ordinal: int
    asset_id: int | None
    anchor_level: str | None
    sent_path: Path = field(repr=False)
    sent_mime_type: str
    original_width: int
    original_height: int
    original_bytes: int
    original_format: str
    sent_width: int
    sent_height: int
    sent_bytes: int
    sent_format: str
    content_hash: str
    sent_hash: str
    compression_cache_key: str | None
    cache_outcome: str
    fallback_reason_code: str | None = None
    fidelity_mode: str = "legacy_default"
    fidelity_value: str = ""
    preprocess_mode: str = "legacy_default"
    preprocess_value: str = ""
    policy_hash: str | None = None
    capability_profile_key: str = ""
    normalized_pixel_hash: str | None = None
    compression_ms: int = 0
    saved_bytes: int = 0
    saved_ratio_ppm: int = 0
    provider_max_bytes: int = 0
    operational_max_bytes: int = 0
    size_gate_result: str = "legacy_default"
    image_tokens: int = 0
    estimation_formula_version: str = "legacy_default"
    compressor_name: str = "legacy"
    compressor_version: str = "legacy"

    def as_evidence(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return only database-safe facts; filesystem paths are intentionally absent."""

        return {
            "schema_version": "prepared-model-media-v2",
            "role": self.role,
            "ordinal": self.ordinal,
            "asset_id": self.asset_id,
            "anchor_level": self.anchor_level,
            "original": {
                "width": self.original_width,
                "height": self.original_height,
                "bytes": self.original_bytes,
                "format": self.original_format,
                "content_hash": self.content_hash,
            },
            "sent": {
                "width": self.sent_width,
                "height": self.sent_height,
                "bytes": self.sent_bytes,
                "format": self.sent_format,
                "mime_type": self.sent_mime_type,
                "content_hash": self.sent_hash,
            },
            "compression_cache_key": self.compression_cache_key,
            "cache_outcome": self.cache_outcome,
            "fallback_reason_code": self.fallback_reason_code,
            "vision_policy": {
                "fidelity_mode": self.fidelity_mode,
                "fidelity_value": self.fidelity_value,
                "preprocess_mode": self.preprocess_mode,
                "preprocess_value": self.preprocess_value,
                "policy_hash": self.policy_hash,
                "capability_profile_key": self.capability_profile_key,
            },
            "lossless_transport": {
                "normalized_pixel_hash": self.normalized_pixel_hash,
                "compressor_name": self.compressor_name,
                "compressor_version": self.compressor_version,
                "compression_ms": self.compression_ms,
                "saved_bytes": self.saved_bytes,
                "saved_ratio_ppm": self.saved_ratio_ppm,
                "provider_max_bytes": self.provider_max_bytes,
                "operational_max_bytes": self.operational_max_bytes,
                "size_gate_result": self.size_gate_result,
            },
            "token_estimate": {
                "image_tokens": self.image_tokens,
                "formula_version": self.estimation_formula_version,
            },
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreparedMediaBundle:
    items: tuple[PreparedModelMedia, ...]

    @property
    def target(self) -> PreparedModelMedia:
        return next(item for item in self.items if item.role == "target")

    @property
    def anchors(self) -> tuple[PreparedModelMedia, ...]:
        return tuple(item for item in self.items if item.role == "anchor")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _ImageFacts:
    width: int
    height: int
    size_bytes: int
    image_format: str


def _sha256_file_uncached(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    # 每次准备都要核对 NAS 原图、几何派生图与发送文件的摘要；同一文件身份
    # （路径、mtime、大小）在本进程内只读一次，文件一变立刻重算。
    return sha256_file_memo(path, _sha256_file_uncached)


def _image_facts(path: Path) -> _ImageFacts:
    try:
        size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
        with Image.open(path) as image:
            image_format = str(image.format or "").upper()
            width, height = image.size
            image.load()
    except (OSError, UnidentifiedImageError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            "media_not_decodable",
            "模型图片无法解码",
        ) from exc
    if size_bytes <= 0 or width <= 0 or height <= 0 or not image_format:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            "media_not_decodable",
            "模型图片缺少有效尺寸或格式",
        )
    return _ImageFacts(width, height, size_bytes, image_format)


def _target_cache_key(item: ModelMediaItem, policy: ModelMediaPolicy) -> str:
    contract = {
        "schema_version": "target-model-media-v1",
        "content_sha256": item.content_sha256,
        "mime_type": item.mime_type,
        "max_frames": policy.target_max_frames,
        "short_edge": policy.delivery_short_edge,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(contract, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _target_cache_candidates(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    cache_dir: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    if item.mime_type == "image/gif":
        rendered_identity = f"{item.content_sha256}-{policy.target_max_frames}"
        return (
            cache_dir / f"{rendered_identity}.png",
            model_delivery_cache_path(
                rendered_identity,
                cache_dir=cache_dir,
                short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
            ),
        )
    return (
        model_delivery_cache_path(
            item.content_sha256,
            cache_dir=cache_dir,
            short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
        ),
    )


def _valid_target_cache_paths(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    cache_dir: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
) -> set[Path]:
    valid: set[Path] = set()
    for candidate in _target_cache_candidates(
        item,
        cache_dir=cache_dir,
        policy=policy,
    ):
        if candidate.is_symlink():
            try:
                candidate.unlink()
            except OSError as exc:
                raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                    "target_cache_cleanup_failed",
                    "目标图缓存异常且无法清理",
                ) from exc
            continue
        if not candidate.is_file():
            continue
        try:
            _image_facts(candidate)
        except ModelMediaPreparationError:
            try:
                candidate.unlink()
            except OSError as exc:
                raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                    "target_cache_cleanup_failed",
                    "目标图缓存异常且无法清理",
                ) from exc
            continue
        valid.add(candidate.resolve())
    return valid
