def _validate_items(items: Sequence[ModelMediaItem]) -> tuple[ModelMediaItem, ...]:
    frozen = tuple(items)
    if not frozen:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_items_empty", "模型请求缺少图片")
    targets = [item for item in frozen if item.role == "target"]
    if len(targets) != 1 or targets[0].ordinal != 0:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            "media_target_invalid",
            "每次模型请求必须且只能有一张序号为 0 的目标图",
        )
    identities: set[tuple[str, int]] = set()
    for item in frozen:
        if item.role not in {"target", "anchor"}:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_role_invalid", "图片角色不合法")
        if item.ordinal < 0 or isinstance(item.ordinal, bool):
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_ordinal_invalid", "图片序号不合法")
        identity = (item.role, item.ordinal)
        if identity in identities:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_identity_duplicate", "图片序号重复")
        identities.add(identity)
        if not item.mime_type.startswith("image/"):
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_mime_invalid", "图片类型不合法")
        if not _SHA256.fullmatch(item.content_sha256):
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError("media_hash_invalid", "图片摘要不合法")
        if item.role == "target" and item.anchor_level is not None:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                "media_anchor_level_invalid", "目标图不能配置锚点等级"
            )
        if item.role == "anchor" and not item.anchor_level:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                "media_anchor_level_invalid", "锚点图必须配置等级"
            )
    return frozen


def _prepared(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    original: _ImageFacts,
    sent_path: Path,
    sent_mime_type: str,
    cache_key: str | None,
    cache_outcome: str,
    fallback_reason_code: str | None = None,
) -> PreparedModelMedia:
    sent = _image_facts(sent_path)
    return PreparedModelMedia(
        role=item.role,
        ordinal=item.ordinal,
        asset_id=item.asset_id,
        anchor_level=item.anchor_level,
        sent_path=sent_path,
        sent_mime_type=sent_mime_type,
        original_width=original.width,
        original_height=original.height,
        original_bytes=original.size_bytes,
        original_format=original.image_format,
        sent_width=sent.width,
        sent_height=sent.height,
        sent_bytes=sent.size_bytes,
        sent_format=sent.image_format,
        content_hash=item.content_sha256,
        sent_hash=_sha256_file(sent_path),
        compression_cache_key=cache_key,
        cache_outcome=cache_outcome,
        fallback_reason_code=fallback_reason_code,
    )


def _fidelity_evidence_value(role_policy: VisionRolePolicy) -> str:
    fidelity = role_policy.fidelity
    if isinstance(fidelity, DetailFidelity):
        return fidelity.value
    if isinstance(fidelity, PixelLimitFidelity):
        return canonical_json(
            {
                "min_pixels": fidelity.min_pixels,
                "max_pixels": fidelity.max_pixels,
            }
        )
    raise ValueError("图片保真策略不受支持")


def _preprocess_evidence_value(role_policy: VisionRolePolicy) -> str:
    preprocess = role_policy.preprocess
    if isinstance(preprocess, OriginalPreprocess):
        return ""
    if isinstance(preprocess, ShortEdgePreprocess):
        return str(preprocess.pixels)
    if isinstance(preprocess, CustomPreprocess):
        return canonical_json(
            {
                "max_width": preprocess.max_width,
                "max_height": preprocess.max_height,
                "allow_upscale": False,
            }
        )
    raise ValueError("图片预处理策略不受支持")


def _prepared_from_lossless(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    original: _ImageFacts,
    role_policy: VisionRolePolicy,
    frozen_policy_hash: str,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    transport: LosslessTransportResult,
) -> PreparedModelMedia:
    sent = _image_facts(transport.prepared_path)
    return PreparedModelMedia(
        role=item.role,
        ordinal=item.ordinal,
        asset_id=item.asset_id,
        anchor_level=item.anchor_level,
        sent_path=transport.prepared_path,
        sent_mime_type=transport.output_mime_type,
        original_width=original.width,
        original_height=original.height,
        original_bytes=original.size_bytes,
        original_format=original.image_format,
        sent_width=sent.width,
        sent_height=sent.height,
        sent_bytes=transport.sent_bytes,
        sent_format=sent.image_format,
        content_hash=item.content_sha256,
        sent_hash=transport.sent_sha256,
        compression_cache_key=transport.cache_key,
        cache_outcome=transport.cache_outcome,
        fallback_reason_code=transport.fallback_reason_code,
        fidelity_mode=role_policy.fidelity.mode,
        fidelity_value=_fidelity_evidence_value(role_policy),
        preprocess_mode=role_policy.preprocess.mode,
        preprocess_value=_preprocess_evidence_value(role_policy),
        policy_hash=frozen_policy_hash,
        capability_profile_key=capability.profile_key,
        normalized_pixel_hash=transport.normalized_sent_pixel_hash,
        compression_ms=transport.compression_ms,
        saved_bytes=transport.saved_bytes,
        saved_ratio_ppm=transport.saved_ratio_ppm,
        provider_max_bytes=transport.provider_max_bytes,
        operational_max_bytes=transport.operational_max_bytes,
        size_gate_result=transport.size_gate_result,
        image_tokens=estimate_image_tokens(
            capability,
            role_policy,
            sent.width,
            sent.height,
        ),
        estimation_formula_version=capability.token_formula,
        compressor_name=transport.compressor_name,
        compressor_version=transport.compressor_version,
    )


def needs_oversize_fallback(sent_bytes: int, capability) -> bool:
    """是否超过供应商安全字节上限。"""

    return int(sent_bytes) > int(capability.operational_single_image_bytes)


def oversize_fallback_pixels(fidelity, capability) -> int:
    """兜底降像素的目标：该 fidelity 档供应商实际会使用的像素预算。

    降到这个值不损失模型可见细节——供应商本来就会把图等比预处理到该预算，
    也不改变图片 token 计费（同档固定）。
    """

    return int(capability.effective_pixels(fidelity, 4096, 4096))


def _oversize_fallback_preprocess(source_path: Path, target_pixels: int):
    """按目标像素预算换算出一个只缩不放的显式几何策略。"""

    with Image.open(source_path) as probe:
        width, height = probe.size
    pixels = max(1, int(width) * int(height))
    if pixels <= target_pixels:
        return None
    scale = math.sqrt(target_pixels / pixels)
    # 向上取整并留 1px 余量：宁可略高于预算，也不能因取整而低于「供应商反正会用」
    # 的像素预算——那会白白损失模型可见细节。
    max_width = max(1, math.ceil(width * scale) + 1)
    max_height = max(1, math.ceil(height * scale) + 1)
    return CustomPreprocess(max_width=max_width, max_height=max_height)


def _prepare_explicit(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    original: _ImageFacts,
    cache_root: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    frozen_policy_hash: str,
) -> PreparedModelMedia:
    role_policy = policy.role_policy(item.role)
    try:
        preprocessed_path, preprocessed_mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
            item.source_path,
            mime_type=item.mime_type,
            content_sha256=item.content_sha256,
            cache_dir=cache_root / "vision-geometry",
            preprocess=role_policy.preprocess,
            animation=role_policy.animation,
        )
        preprocessed_sha256 = _sha256_file(preprocessed_path)
        try:
            transport = prepare_lossless_transport(
                preprocessed_path,
                preprocessed_sha256,
                preprocessed_mime,
                capability,
                cache_root / "lossless-transport",
            )
        except LosslessImageTransportError as size_exc:
            # 平台级兜底：图片在无损优化后仍超过供应商字节上限时，按该 fidelity
            # 档供应商实际会使用的像素预算降像素后重试。供应商本就会缩到该预算，
            # 因此不损失模型可见细节，也不改变图片 token 计费。正常尺寸图片不受影响。
            if size_exc.code != "image_payload_too_large_after_lossless":
                raise
            fallback = _oversize_fallback_preprocess(
                preprocessed_path,
                oversize_fallback_pixels(role_policy.fidelity, capability),
            )
            if fallback is None:
                raise
            preprocessed_path, preprocessed_mime = prepare_vision_geometry(
                preprocessed_path,
                mime_type=preprocessed_mime,
                content_sha256=preprocessed_sha256,
                cache_dir=cache_root / "vision-geometry-oversize",
                preprocess=fallback,
                animation=role_policy.animation,
            )
            preprocessed_sha256 = _sha256_file(preprocessed_path)
            transport = prepare_lossless_transport(
                preprocessed_path,
                preprocessed_sha256,
                preprocessed_mime,
                capability,
                cache_root / "lossless-transport",
            )
    except LosslessImageTransportError as exc:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            exc.code,
            str(exc),
            evidence=exc.evidence,
        ) from None
    except Exception as exc:
        message = (
            "目标图无法执行冻结图片策略"
            if item.role == "target"
            else "锚点图无法执行冻结图片策略"
        )
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            f"{item.role}_policy_prepare_failed",
            message,
        ) from exc
    return _prepared_from_lossless(
        item,
        original=original,
        role_policy=role_policy,
        frozen_policy_hash=frozen_policy_hash,
        capability=capability,
        transport=transport,
    )


def _prepare_target(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    original: _ImageFacts,
    cache_root: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
) -> PreparedModelMedia:
    cache_dir = cache_root / "evaluation"
    known_valid_cache_paths = _valid_target_cache_paths(
        item,
        cache_dir=cache_dir,
        policy=policy,
    )
    try:
        sent_path, sent_mime_type = prepare_model_image(
            item.source_path,
            mime_type=item.mime_type,
            content_sha256=item.content_sha256,
            cache_dir=cache_dir,
            max_frames=policy.target_max_frames,
            short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
        )
        if sent_path == item.source_path:
            return _prepared(
                item,
                original=original,
                sent_path=sent_path,
                sent_mime_type=sent_mime_type,
                cache_key=None,
                cache_outcome="not_applicable",
            )
        return _prepared(
            item,
            original=original,
            sent_path=sent_path,
            sent_mime_type=sent_mime_type,
            cache_key=_target_cache_key(item, policy),
            cache_outcome=(
                "hit" if sent_path.resolve() in known_valid_cache_paths else "miss"
            ),
        )
    except ModelMediaPreparationError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            "target_prepare_failed",
            "目标图无法生成供应商安全载荷",
        ) from exc


def _prepare_anchor(
    item: ModelMediaItem,
    *,
    original: _ImageFacts,
    cache_root: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
) -> PreparedModelMedia:
    cache_dir = cache_root / "anchor-reference"
    cache_key = anchor_reference_cache_key(
        item.content_sha256,
        short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
    )
    cache_path = anchor_reference_cache_path(
        item.content_sha256,
        cache_dir=cache_dir,
        short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
    )
    cache_was_valid = False
    if cache_path.is_file():
        try:
            facts = _image_facts(cache_path)
            expected_size = model_delivery_size(
                original.width,
                original.height,
                short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
            )
            cache_was_valid = (
                facts.image_format == "JPEG"
                and (facts.width, facts.height)
                in {expected_size, tuple(reversed(expected_size))}
            )
        except ModelMediaPreparationError:
            cache_was_valid = False
    try:
        sent_path, sent_mime_type = prepare_anchor_reference_image(
            item.source_path,
            content_sha256=item.content_sha256,
            cache_dir=cache_dir,
            short_edge=policy.delivery_short_edge,
        )
        return _prepared(
            item,
            original=original,
            sent_path=sent_path,
            sent_mime_type=sent_mime_type,
            cache_key=cache_key,
            cache_outcome="hit" if cache_was_valid else "miss",
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        # Never bypass the frozen short-edge policy with the original anchor:
        # doing so can exceed both the provider payload and paid token budget.
        raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
            "anchor_prepare_failed",
            "锚点图无法生成供应商安全载荷",
        ) from exc


def prepare_model_media(
    items: Sequence[ModelMediaItem],
    *,
    cache_root: Path,
    policy: ModelMediaPolicy,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile | None = None,
) -> PreparedMediaBundle:
    """Prepare one target and its anchors with content-addressed reuse."""

    frozen = _validate_items(items)
    frozen_policy_hash: str | None = None
    if not policy.is_legacy_default:
        if capability is None:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                "vision_capability_profile_required",
                "显式图片策略缺少冻结模型能力档案",
            )
        frozen_policy_hash = vision_policy_hash(
            policy.as_vision_policy(capability),
            capability,
        )
    cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    prepared: list[PreparedModelMedia] = []
    for item in frozen:
        if not item.source_path.is_file():
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                "media_source_missing",
                "模型图片文件不存在",
            )
        actual_hash = _sha256_file(item.source_path)
        if actual_hash != item.content_sha256:
            raise ModelMediaPreparationError(
                "media_source_hash_mismatch",
                "模型图片内容与冻结摘要不一致",
            )
        original = _image_facts(item.source_path)
        if not policy.is_legacy_default:
            assert capability is not None and frozen_policy_hash is not None
            result = _prepare_explicit(
                item,
                original=original,
                cache_root=cache_root,
                policy=policy,
                capability=capability,
                frozen_policy_hash=frozen_policy_hash,
            )
        elif item.role == "target":
            result = _prepare_target(
                item,
                original=original,
                cache_root=cache_root,
                policy=policy,
            )
        else:
            result = _prepare_anchor(
                item,
                original=original,
                cache_root=cache_root,
                policy=policy,
            )
        prepared.append(result)
    prepared.sort(key=lambda item: (0 if item.role == "target" else 1, item.ordinal))
    return PreparedMediaBundle(tuple(prepared))


def _media_identity(role: str, ordinal: int) -> str:
    return f"{role}:{ordinal}"


def _media_rows(
    db: Session,
    invocation_id: int,
) -> list[WorkflowModelMediaUsage]:
    return list(
        db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage)
            .where(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.invocation_id == invocation_id)
            .order_by(
                WorkflowModelMediaUsage.media_role.desc(),
                WorkflowModelMediaUsage.ordinal,
            )
        ).all()
    )


def _prepared_row_matches(
    row: WorkflowModelMediaUsage,
    item: PreparedModelMedia,
    invocation_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> bool:
    return all(
        (
            row.media_role == item.role,
            row.ordinal == item.ordinal,
            row.asset_id == item.asset_id,
            row.anchor_level == item.anchor_level,
            row.original_width == item.original_width,
            row.original_height == item.original_height,
            row.original_bytes == item.original_bytes,
            row.original_format == item.original_format,
            row.sent_width == item.sent_width,
            row.sent_height == item.sent_height,
            row.sent_bytes == item.sent_bytes,
            row.sent_format == item.sent_format,
            row.content_hash == item.content_hash,
            row.sent_hash == item.sent_hash,
            row.compression_cache_key == item.compression_cache_key,
            row.cache_outcome == item.cache_outcome,
            row.fidelity_mode == item.fidelity_mode,
            row.fidelity_value == item.fidelity_value,
            row.preprocess_mode == item.preprocess_mode,
            row.preprocess_value == item.preprocess_value,
            row.policy_hash == item.policy_hash,
            row.capability_profile_key == item.capability_profile_key,
            row.normalized_pixel_hash == item.normalized_pixel_hash,
            row.compression_ms == item.compression_ms,
            row.saved_bytes == item.saved_bytes,
            row.saved_ratio_ppm == item.saved_ratio_ppm,
            row.provider_max_bytes == item.provider_max_bytes,
            row.operational_max_bytes == item.operational_max_bytes,
            row.size_gate_result == item.size_gate_result,
            row.estimation_formula_version == item.estimation_formula_version,
            row.evidence_json
            == canonical_json(
                _prepared_media_evidence(item, invocation_context)
            ),
        )
    )


def _prepared_media_evidence(
    item: PreparedModelMedia,
    invocation_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    evidence = item.as_evidence()
    if invocation_context is not None:
        evidence["workflow_invocation"] = dict(invocation_context)
    return evidence


def record_prepared_media(
    db: Session,
    invocation_id: int,
    bundle: PreparedMediaBundle,
    *,
    invocation_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[WorkflowModelMediaUsage, ...]:
    """Persist every outbound image before any provider side effect."""

    invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
    if invocation is None:
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_invocation_missing",
            "模型图片找不到对应调用记录",
        )
    if invocation.status != "reserved":
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_invocation_not_reserved",
            "模型调用已不处于发送前状态",
        )
    if invocation_context is not None:
        expected_context = {
            "parent_step_attempt_id": invocation.step_attempt_id,
            "invocation_key": invocation.invocation_key,
            "foreach_item_key": invocation_context.get("foreach_item_key"),
        }
        if (
            dict(invocation_context) != expected_context
            or not isinstance(expected_context["foreach_item_key"], str)
            or not expected_context["foreach_item_key"]
        ):
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_invocation_context_invalid",
                "模型图片的 foreach_image 调用身份不一致",
            )
    existing = _media_rows(db, invocation_id)
    expected = {
        (item.role, item.ordinal): item
        for item in bundle.items
    }
    if existing:
        if len(existing) != len(expected) or any(
            (row.media_role, row.ordinal) not in expected
            or not _prepared_row_matches(
                row,
                expected[(row.media_role, row.ordinal)],
                invocation_context,
            )
            for row in existing
        ):
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_preparation_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同模型调用已绑定不同图片载荷",
            )
        return tuple(existing)
    rows = []
    for item in bundle.items:
        row = WorkflowModelMediaUsage(
            invocation_id=invocation_id,
            asset_id=item.asset_id,
            media_role=item.role,
            ordinal=item.ordinal,
            anchor_level=item.anchor_level,
            original_width=item.original_width,
            original_height=item.original_height,
            original_bytes=item.original_bytes,
            original_format=item.original_format,
            sent_width=item.sent_width,
            sent_height=item.sent_height,
            sent_bytes=item.sent_bytes,
            sent_format=item.sent_format,
            content_hash=item.content_hash,
            sent_hash=item.sent_hash,
            compression_cache_key=item.compression_cache_key,
            cache_outcome=item.cache_outcome,
            fidelity_mode=item.fidelity_mode,
            fidelity_value=item.fidelity_value,
            preprocess_mode=item.preprocess_mode,
            preprocess_value=item.preprocess_value,
            policy_hash=item.policy_hash,
            capability_profile_key=item.capability_profile_key,
            normalized_pixel_hash=item.normalized_pixel_hash,
            compression_ms=item.compression_ms,
            saved_bytes=item.saved_bytes,
            saved_ratio_ppm=item.saved_ratio_ppm,
            provider_max_bytes=item.provider_max_bytes,
            operational_max_bytes=item.operational_max_bytes,
            size_gate_result=item.size_gate_result,
            estimation_formula_version=item.estimation_formula_version,
            image_tokens=item.image_tokens,
            usage_source=(
                "official_estimate"
                if item.estimation_formula_version != "legacy_default"
                else "unavailable"
            ),
            estimated_cost_micros=0,
            actual_cost_micros=None,
            evidence_json=canonical_json(
                _prepared_media_evidence(item, invocation_context)
            ),
        )
        db.add(row)
        rows.append(row)
    db.flush()
    return tuple(rows)


def _nonnegative_int(value: object, *, field_name: str) -> int | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 0:
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_usage_invalid",
            f"模型图片用量字段 {field_name} 不合法",
        )
    return value


def _allocate_integer_total(
    total: int,
    weights: Mapping[str, int],
) -> dict[str, int]:
    if not weights:
        return {}
    weight_sum = sum(max(0, value) for value in weights.values())
    if total == 0:
        return {key: 0 for key in weights}
    if weight_sum <= 0:
        ordered = sorted(weights)
        return {
            key: total // len(ordered) + (index < total % len(ordered))
            for index, key in enumerate(ordered)
        }
    allocated = {
        key: total * max(0, weight) // weight_sum
        for key, weight in weights.items()
    }
    remainder = total - sum(allocated.values())
    ranked = sorted(
        weights,
        key=lambda key: (
            -(total * max(0, weights[key]) % weight_sum),
            key,
        ),
    )
    for key in ranked[:remainder]:
        allocated[key] += 1
    return allocated


def _safe_correlation_id(value: object) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{1,200}", value):
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_usage_correlation_invalid",
            "供应商请求关联标识不合法",
        )
    return value


def _settlement_plan(
    rows: Sequence[WorkflowModelMediaUsage],
    *,
    provider_usage: Mapping[str, Any],
    pricing_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    version = pricing_snapshot.get("version")
    pricing_tier_key: str | None = None
    if version in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS:
        raw_contract = pricing_snapshot.get("pricing_contract")
        request_input_tokens = _nonnegative_int(
            pricing_snapshot.get("request_input_tokens"),
            field_name="request_input_tokens",
        )
        if not isinstance(raw_contract, Mapping) or request_input_tokens is None:
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
                "模型图片缺少请求级阶梯计价快照",
            )
        try:
            contract = parse_pricing_contract(raw_contract)
            tier = select_pricing_tier(contract, request_input_tokens)
        except ModelPricingError as exc:
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(exc.code, str(exc)) from exc
        # The media ledger is a component breakdown only.  The authoritative
        # request cost is settled once on WorkflowModelInvocation.
        price = tier.input_micros_per_1k * 1_000
        pricing_tier_key = tier.tier_key
    else:
        price = _nonnegative_int(
            pricing_snapshot.get("input_micros_per_million_tokens"),
            field_name="input_micros_per_million_tokens",
        )
    if price is None or not isinstance(version, str) or not version.strip():
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_pricing_snapshot_invalid",
            "模型图片缺少冻结计价快照",
        )
    correlation_id = _safe_correlation_id(
        provider_usage.get("request_correlation_id")
    )
    identities = {
        _media_identity(row.media_role, row.ordinal): row for row in rows
    }
    total = _nonnegative_int(
        provider_usage.get("total_image_tokens"),
        field_name="total_image_tokens",
    )
    raw_tokens = provider_usage.get("tokens_by_identity")
    tokens_by_identity: dict[str, int] | None = None
    usage_source = "unavailable"
    estimator_version: str | None = None
    if raw_tokens is not None:
        if not isinstance(raw_tokens, Mapping) or set(raw_tokens) != set(identities):
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_usage_identity_mismatch",
                "供应商分图用量与实际发送图片不一致",
            )
        tokens_by_identity = {
            str(key): _nonnegative_int(value, field_name=str(key)) or 0
            for key, value in raw_tokens.items()
        }
        calculated_total = sum(tokens_by_identity.values())
        if total is not None and total != calculated_total:
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_usage_reconciliation_failed",
                "供应商分图用量与图片总用量不一致",
            )
        total = calculated_total
        usage_source = "provider_actual"
    elif total is not None and len(rows) == 1:
        tokens_by_identity = {next(iter(identities)): total}
        usage_source = "provider_actual"
    elif all(
        row.usage_source == "official_estimate"
        and row.estimation_formula_version != "legacy_default"
        for row in rows
    ):
        # A provider-wide image-token total is not per-image evidence.  Keep
        # every frozen official estimate intact unless genuine per-image usage
        # is returned; the aggregate remains recorded separately below.
        tokens_by_identity = {
            identity: row.image_tokens for identity, row in identities.items()
        }
        usage_source = "official_estimate"
        formula_versions = {
            row.estimation_formula_version for row in rows
        }
        estimator_version = (
            next(iter(formula_versions))
            if len(formula_versions) == 1
            else "frozen-official-estimate-v1"
        )
    elif total is not None:
        tokens_by_identity = _allocate_integer_total(
            total,
            {
                identity: row.sent_width * row.sent_height
                for identity, row in identities.items()
            },
        )
        usage_source = "official_estimate"
        estimator_version = "provider-aggregate-pixel-allocation-v1"
    else:
        tokens_by_identity = {identity: 0 for identity in identities}

    total_cost = math.ceil(sum(tokens_by_identity.values()) * price / 1_000_000)
    costs = _allocate_integer_total(total_cost, tokens_by_identity)
    plan: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for identity, row in identities.items():
        settlement = {
            "schema_version": "model-media-settlement-v1",
            "usage_source": usage_source,
            "image_tokens": tokens_by_identity[identity],
            "pricing_version": version,
            "input_micros_per_million_tokens": price,
            "pricing_tier_key": pricing_tier_key,
            "estimated_cost_micros": costs[identity],
            "provider_correlation_id": correlation_id,
            "provider_total_image_tokens": total,
            "estimator_version": estimator_version,
        }
        try:
            evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_evidence_invalid",
                "模型图片准备证据损坏",
            ) from exc
        if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_evidence_invalid",
                "模型图片准备证据必须是对象",
            )
        evidence["settlement"] = settlement
        plan[identity] = {
            **settlement,
            "evidence_json": canonical_json(evidence),
        }
    return plan


def settle_media_usage(
    db: Session,
    invocation_id: int,
    provider_usage: Mapping[str, Any],
    pricing_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[WorkflowModelMediaUsage, ...]:
    """Settle per-image usage without pretending estimates are provider actuals."""

    rows = _media_rows(db, invocation_id)
    if not rows:
        raise ModelMediaUsageError(
            "media_usage_rows_missing",
            "模型调用缺少发送前图片事实",
        )
    plan = _settlement_plan(
        rows,
        provider_usage=provider_usage,
        pricing_snapshot=pricing_snapshot,
    )
    if any(row.settled_at is not None for row in rows):
        if not all(row.settled_at is not None for row in rows):
            raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                "media_usage_idempotency_conflict",
                "模型图片用量只完成了部分结算",
            )
        for row in rows:
            expected = plan[_media_identity(row.media_role, row.ordinal)]
            if (
                row.image_tokens != expected["image_tokens"]
                or row.usage_source != expected["usage_source"]
                or row.estimated_cost_micros
                != expected["estimated_cost_micros"]
                or row.actual_cost_micros is not None
                or row.provider_correlation_id
                != expected["provider_correlation_id"]
                or row.evidence_json != expected["evidence_json"]
            ):
                raise ModelMediaUsageError(
                    "media_usage_idempotency_conflict",
                    "相同模型调用已使用不同图片用量完成结算",
                )
        return tuple(rows)
    settled_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for row in rows:
        expected = plan[_media_identity(row.media_role, row.ordinal)]
        row.image_tokens = expected["image_tokens"]
        row.usage_source = expected["usage_source"]
        row.estimated_cost_micros = expected["estimated_cost_micros"]
        row.actual_cost_micros = None
        row.provider_correlation_id = expected["provider_correlation_id"]
        row.evidence_json = expected["evidence_json"]
        row.settled_at = settled_at
    db.flush()
    return tuple(rows)

===== FILE backend/app/model_media_usage_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from sqlalchemy import case, distinct, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .models import User, WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowModelMediaUsage


ROLE_LABELS = {"target": "目标图", "anchor": "锚点图"}
CACHE_LABELS = {
    "not_applicable": "无需缓存",
    "hit": "缓存命中",
    "miss": "首次生成",
    "fallback_source": "已回退原图",
}
USAGE_LABELS = {
    "provider_actual": "供应商实际",
    "official_estimate": "官方估算",
    "unavailable": "供应商未返回",
}


def _require_runtime_read(user: User) -> None:
    if not has_permission(user, "runtime:read"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail={
                "code": "model_media_usage_read_forbidden",
                "message": "当前账号无权查看模型用量",
            },
        )


def _totals(rows: Sequence[WorkflowModelMediaUsage]) -> dict[str, int]:
    original_bytes = sum(row.original_bytes for row in rows)
    sent_bytes = sum(row.sent_bytes for row in rows)
    return {
        "image_count": len(rows),
        "target_count": sum(row.media_role == "target" for row in rows),
        "anchor_count": sum(row.media_role == "anchor" for row in rows),
        "original_bytes": original_bytes,
        "sent_bytes": sent_bytes,
        "saved_bytes": max(0, original_bytes - sent_bytes),
        "cache_hit_count": sum(row.cache_outcome == "hit" for row in rows),
        "cache_miss_count": sum(row.cache_outcome == "miss" for row in rows),
        "fallback_count": sum(
            row.cache_outcome == "fallback_source" for row in rows
        ),
        "image_tokens": sum(row.image_tokens for row in rows),
        "estimated_cost_micros": sum(
            row.estimated_cost_micros for row in rows
        ),
        "actual_cost_micros": sum(
            row.actual_cost_micros or 0 for row in rows
        ),
    }


def _item(row: WorkflowModelMediaUsage) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "asset_id": row.asset_id,
        "media_role": row.media_role,
        "role_label": ROLE_LABELS[row.media_role],
        "ordinal": row.ordinal,
        "anchor_level": row.anchor_level,
        "original": {
            "width": row.original_width,
            "height": row.original_height,
            "bytes": row.original_bytes,
            "format": row.original_format,
        },
        "sent": {
            "width": row.sent_width,
            "height": row.sent_height,
            "bytes": row.sent_bytes,
            "format": row.sent_format,
        },
        "cache_outcome": row.cache_outcome,
        "cache_outcome_label": CACHE_LABELS[row.cache_outcome],
        "image_tokens": row.image_tokens,
        "usage_source": row.usage_source,
        "usage_source_label": USAGE_LABELS[row.usage_source],
        "estimated_cost_micros": row.estimated_cost_micros,
        "actual_cost_micros": row.actual_cost_micros,
        "provider_correlation_id": row.provider_correlation_id,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "settled_at": row.settled_at,
    }


def _summary_totals(db: Session, cutoff: datetime) -> dict[str, int]:
    row = db.execute(
        select(
            func.count(distinct(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.invocation_id)),
            func.count(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.id),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(
                    case(
                        (WorkflowModelMediaUsage.media_role == "target", 1),
                        else_=0,
                    )
                ),
                0,
            ),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(
                    case(
                        (WorkflowModelMediaUsage.media_role == "anchor", 1),
                        else_=0,
                    )
                ),
                0,
            ),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.original_bytes), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.sent_bytes), 0),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(
                    case(
                        (WorkflowModelMediaUsage.cache_outcome == "hit", 1),
                        else_=0,
                    )
                ),
                0,
            ),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(
                    case(
                        (WorkflowModelMediaUsage.cache_outcome == "miss", 1),
                        else_=0,
                    )
                ),
                0,
            ),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(
                    case(
                        (
                            WorkflowModelMediaUsage.cache_outcome
                            == "fallback_source",
                            1,
                        ),
                        else_=0,
                    )
                ),
                0,
            ),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.image_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.estimated_cost_micros), 0
            ),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.actual_cost_micros), 0
            ),
        ).where(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.created_at >= cutoff)
    ).one()
    original_bytes = int(row[4])
    sent_bytes = int(row[5])
    return {
        "invocation_count": int(row[0]),
        "image_count": int(row[1]),
        "target_count": int(row[2]),
        "anchor_count": int(row[3]),
        "original_bytes": original_bytes,
        "sent_bytes": sent_bytes,
        "saved_bytes": max(0, original_bytes - sent_bytes),
        "cache_hit_count": int(row[6]),
        "cache_miss_count": int(row[7]),
        "fallback_count": int(row[8]),
        "image_tokens": int(row[9]),
        "estimated_cost_micros": int(row[10]),
        "actual_cost_micros": int(row[11]),
    }


def build_model_media_usage_router(
    require_user: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(tags=["model-media-usage"])

    @router.get("/api/model-invocations/{invocation_id}/media-usage")
    def invocation_media_usage(
        invocation_id: int,
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_runtime_read(user)
        invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
        if invocation is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404,
                detail={
                    "code": "model_invocation_missing",
                    "message": "模型调用不存在",
                },
            )
        rows = list(
            db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage)
                .where(
                    WorkflowModelMediaUsage.invocation_id == invocation_id
                )
                .order_by(
                    case(
                        (WorkflowModelMediaUsage.media_role == "target", 0),
                        else_=1,
                    ),
                    WorkflowModelMediaUsage.ordinal,
                )
            ).all()
        )
        return {
            "invocation": {
                "id": invocation.id,
                "production_run_id": invocation.production_run_id,
                "baseline_run_id": invocation.baseline_run_id,
                "node_instance_id": invocation.node_instance_id,
                "status": invocation.status,
                "provider": invocation.provider,
                "model_id": invocation.model_id,
                "request_correlation_id": invocation.request_correlation_id,
            },
            "items": [_item(row) for row in rows],
            "totals": _totals(rows),
        }

    @router.get("/api/model-media-usage/summary")
    def media_usage_summary(
        days: int = Query(default=7, ge=1, le=90),
        user: User = Depends(require_user),
        db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        _require_runtime_read(user)
        cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=days)
        grouped = {
            str(source): int(count)
            for source, count in db.execute(
                select(
                    WorkflowModelMediaUsage.usage_source,
                    func.count(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.id),
                )
                .where(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.created_at >= cutoff)
                .group_by(WorkflowModelMediaUsage.usage_source)
            ).all()
        }
        return {
            "window_days": days,
            "from": cutoff,
            "totals": _summary_totals(db, cutoff),
            "usage_sources": {
                key: {
                    "label": label,
                    "image_count": grouped.get(key, 0),
                }
                for key, label in USAGE_LABELS.items()
            },
        }

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/model_pricing.py =====
"""Immutable, fail-closed model token pricing contracts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .audit import canonical_json


OFFICIAL_VOLCENGINE_PRICING_URL = (
    "https://docs.volcengine.com/docs/82379/1544106?lang=zh"
)
_VOLCENGINE_PROVIDERS = {"ark", "doubao", "volcengine", "volcengine-ark"}
_DOUBAO_SEED_20_LITE_KEY = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite"
MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS = frozenset(
    {"model-pricing-v2", "model-pricing-v3"}
)


def is_volcengine_ark_provider(provider: object) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(provider, str)
        and _normalized_identity(provider) in _VOLCENGINE_PROVIDERS
    )


class ModelPricingError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PricingTier:
    tier_key: str
    min_input_tokens: int
    max_input_tokens: int
    input_micros_per_1k: int
    output_micros_per_1k: int
    cached_input_micros_per_1k: int | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PricingContract:
    schema_version: str
    model_key: str
    currency: str
    unit_tokens: int
    source_url: str
    effective_at: str
    tiers: tuple[PricingTier, ...]

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        tiers: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for tier in self.tiers:
            serialized = {
                "tier_key": tier.tier_key,
                "min_input_tokens": tier.min_input_tokens,
                "max_input_tokens": tier.max_input_tokens,
                "input_micros_per_1k": tier.input_micros_per_1k,
                "output_micros_per_1k": tier.output_micros_per_1k,
            }
            if self.schema_version == "model-pricing-v3":
                serialized["cached_input_micros_per_1k"] = (
                    tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k
                )
            tiers.append(serialized)
        return {
            "schema_version": self.schema_version,
            "model_key": self.model_key,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "unit_tokens": self.unit_tokens,
            "source_url": self.source_url,
            "effective_at": self.effective_at,
            "tiers": tiers,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PricedUsage:
    tier_key: str
    input_tokens: int
    cached_input_tokens: int
    uncached_input_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    uncached_input_cost_micros: int
    cached_input_cost_micros: int
    input_cost_micros: int
    output_cost_micros: int
    total_cost_micros: int
    full_price_cost_micros: int
    cache_savings_micros: int
    pricing_contract_hash: str


def _nonnegative_int(value: Any, *, field: str) -> int:
    if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 0:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            f"{field} 必须是非负整数",
        )
    return value


def _positive_int(value: Any, *, field: str) -> int:
    parsed = _nonnegative_int(value, field=field)
    if parsed == 0:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            f"{field} 必须大于 0",
        )
    return parsed


def _nonempty_string(value: Any, *, field: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            f"{field} 不能为空",
        )
    return value.strip()


def _effective_at(value: Any) -> str:
    text = _nonempty_string(value, field="effective_at")
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(text.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "effective_at 必须是带时区的 ISO-8601 时间",
        ) from exc
    if parsed.tzinfo is None or parsed.utcoffset() is None:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "effective_at 必须包含时区",
        )
    return text


def _parse_tiers(
    raw_tiers: Any,
    *,
    schema_version: str,
) -> tuple[PricingTier, ...]:
    if not isinstance(raw_tiers, Sequence) or isinstance(
        raw_tiers, (str, bytes)
    ):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
            "pricing tiers 必须是非空数组",
        )
    tiers: list[PricingTier] = []
    for index, raw in enumerate(raw_tiers):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                f"第 {index + 1} 个 pricing tier 不是对象",
            )
        try:
            tier = PricingTier(
                tier_key=_nonempty_string(
                    raw.get("tier_key"), field="tier_key"
                ),
                min_input_tokens=_nonnegative_int(
                    raw.get("min_input_tokens"), field="min_input_tokens"
                ),
                max_input_tokens=_nonnegative_int(
                    raw.get("max_input_tokens"), field="max_input_tokens"
                ),
                input_micros_per_1k=_nonnegative_int(
                    raw.get("input_micros_per_1k"),
                    field="input_micros_per_1k",
                ),
                output_micros_per_1k=_nonnegative_int(
                    raw.get("output_micros_per_1k"),
                    field="output_micros_per_1k",
                ),
                cached_input_micros_per_1k=(
                    _nonnegative_int(
                        raw.get("cached_input_micros_per_1k"),
                        field="cached_input_micros_per_1k",
                    )
                    if schema_version == "model-pricing-v3"
                    else None
                ),
            )
        except ModelPricingError as exc:
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                str(exc),
            ) from exc
        if tier.max_input_tokens < tier.min_input_tokens:
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                "pricing tier 上界不能小于下界",
            )
        if (
            tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k is not None
            and tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k
            > tier.input_micros_per_1k
        ):
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                "pricing tier 的缓存输入费率不能高于普通输入费率",
            )
        tiers.append(tier)
    if not tiers or len({tier.tier_key for tier in tiers}) != len(tiers):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
            "pricing tiers 不能为空且 tier_key 必须唯一",
        )
    if tiers[0].min_input_tokens != 0:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
            "pricing tiers 必须从 0 token 开始",
        )
    for previous, current in zip(tiers, tiers[1:]):
        if current.min_input_tokens != previous.max_input_tokens + 1:
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                "pricing tiers 必须按输入 token 连续且不可重叠",
            )
        if (
            current.input_micros_per_1k < previous.input_micros_per_1k
            or current.output_micros_per_1k < previous.output_micros_per_1k
            or (
                current.cached_input_micros_per_1k is not None
                and previous.cached_input_micros_per_1k is not None
                and current.cached_input_micros_per_1k
                < previous.cached_input_micros_per_1k
            )
        ):
            raise ModelPricingError(
                "model_pricing_tiers_invalid",
                "pricing tiers 的普通输入、缓存输入与输出费率都必须单调不降",
            )
    return tuple(tiers)


def parse_pricing_contract(
    raw: Mapping[str, Any] | PricingContract,
) -> PricingContract:
    if isinstance(raw, PricingContract):
        raw = raw.to_dict()
    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "pricing contract 必须是对象",
        )
    schema_version = _nonempty_string(
        raw.get("schema_version"), field="schema_version"
    )
    if schema_version not in MODEL_PRICING_SCHEMA_VERSIONS:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "pricing contract 只接受 model-pricing-v2 或 model-pricing-v3",
        )
    currency = _nonempty_string(raw.get("currency"), field="currency")
    if currency != "CNY":
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "pricing contract 当前只接受 CNY",
        )
    unit_tokens = _positive_int(raw.get("unit_tokens"), field="unit_tokens")
    if unit_tokens != 1_000:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "model-pricing-v2/v3 的价格单位必须是每 1000 tokens",
        )
    source_url = _nonempty_string(raw.get("source_url"), field="source_url")
    if not source_url.startswith("https://"):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_contract_invalid",
            "source_url 必须是 HTTPS 官方来源",
        )
    return PricingContract(
        schema_version=schema_version,
        model_key=_nonempty_string(raw.get("model_key"), field="model_key"),
        currency=currency,
        unit_tokens=unit_tokens,
        source_url=source_url,
        effective_at=_effective_at(raw.get("effective_at")),
        tiers=_parse_tiers(
            raw.get("tiers"),
            schema_version=schema_version,
        ),
    )


def _normalized_identity(value: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", value.strip().lower()).strip("-")


def validate_pricing_contract_binding(
    raw: Mapping[str, Any] | PricingContract,
    *,
    provider: str,
    model_id: str,
) -> PricingContract:
    """Bind a price contract to the provider/model it will actually bill."""

    contract = parse_pricing_contract(raw)
    normalized_provider = _normalized_identity(provider)
    normalized_model = _normalized_identity(model_id)
    normalized_key = _normalized_identity(contract.model_key)
    if not normalized_model or not (
        normalized_model == normalized_key
        or normalized_model.startswith(f"{normalized_key}-")
    ):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_model_mismatch",
            "pricing contract 的 model_key 与当前模型 ID 不匹配",
        )
    if (
        contract.source_url == OFFICIAL_VOLCENGINE_PRICING_URL
        or normalized_key == _DOUBAO_SEED_20_LITE_KEY
    ) and (
        not is_volcengine_ark_provider(normalized_provider)
        or normalized_key != _DOUBAO_SEED_20_LITE_KEY
    ):
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_provider_mismatch",
            "豆包 Seed 2.0 Lite 官方价格只能用于兼容的火山方舟模型",
        )
    return contract


def pricing_contract_hash(raw: Mapping[str, Any] | PricingContract) -> str:
    contract = parse_pricing_contract(raw)
    return hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(contract.to_dict()).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def select_pricing_tier(
    raw: Mapping[str, Any] | PricingContract,
    input_tokens: int,
) -> PricingTier:
    tokens = _nonnegative_int(input_tokens, field="input_tokens")
    contract = parse_pricing_contract(raw)
    for tier in contract.tiers:
        if tier.min_input_tokens <= tokens <= tier.max_input_tokens:
            return tier
    raise ModelPricingError(
        "model_pricing_tier_not_found",
        f"输入长度 {tokens} tokens 不在冻结价格合同覆盖范围内",
    )


def price_usage(
    raw: Mapping[str, Any] | PricingContract,
    *,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    cached_input_tokens: int = 0,
) -> PricedUsage:
    contract = parse_pricing_contract(raw)
    input_count = _nonnegative_int(input_tokens, field="input_tokens")
    output_count = _nonnegative_int(output_tokens, field="output_tokens")
    cached_count = _nonnegative_int(
        cached_input_tokens,
        field="cached_input_tokens",
    )
    if cached_count > input_count:
        raise ModelPricingError(
            "model_pricing_usage_invalid",
            "cached_input_tokens 不能大于 input_tokens",
        )
    tier = select_pricing_tier(contract, input_count)
    if contract.schema_version == "model-pricing-v2":
        cached_count = 0
    uncached_count = input_count - cached_count

    def component_cost(tokens: int, rate: int) -> int:
        return (tokens * rate + contract.unit_tokens - 1) // contract.unit_tokens

    uncached_input_cost = component_cost(
        uncached_count,
        tier.input_micros_per_1k,
    )
    cached_input_cost = component_cost(
        cached_count,
        tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k or 0,
    )
    input_cost = uncached_input_cost + cached_input_cost
    output_cost = component_cost(output_count, tier.output_micros_per_1k)
    full_price_cost = (
        component_cost(input_count, tier.input_micros_per_1k) + output_cost
    )
    total_cost = input_cost + output_cost
    return PricedUsage(
        tier_key=tier.tier_key,
        input_tokens=input_count,
        cached_input_tokens=cached_count,
        uncached_input_tokens=uncached_count,
        output_tokens=output_count,
        uncached_input_cost_micros=uncached_input_cost,
        cached_input_cost_micros=cached_input_cost,
        input_cost_micros=input_cost,
        output_cost_micros=output_cost,
        total_cost_micros=total_cost,
        full_price_cost_micros=full_price_cost,
        cache_savings_micros=max(0, full_price_cost - total_cost),
        pricing_contract_hash=pricing_contract_hash(contract),
    )


def doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Official CNY cash rates, frozen from the cited Volcengine table."""

    return {

===== FILE backend/app/model_quota.py =====
"""Database-backed admission for provider-owned model quota scopes."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
import secrets
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, inspect, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
from .models import ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaMinuteWindow, ModelQuotaPool


_OWNER_KEY = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{0,239}")
_COOLDOWN_REASON = re.compile(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,79}")
_MAX_RESERVATION = 2_000_000_000
_MAX_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 3_600
LOCAL_QUOTA_WAIT_CODES = frozenset({
    "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted", "quota_pool_cooling_down",
})


class ModelQuotaUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        pool_key: str | None = None,
        retry_after_seconds: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.pool_key = pool_key
        self.retry_after_seconds = retry_after_seconds
        self.retryable = retry_after_seconds is not None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QuotaReservation:
    pool_id: int
    pool_key: str
    lease_token: str
    owner_key: str
    status: str
    acquired_at: datetime
    expires_at: datetime
    reserved_tokens: int
    reserved_images: int
    reused: bool
    submission_state: dict[str, Any] | None = None


def _now(value: datetime | None) -> datetime:
    candidate = value or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if candidate.tzinfo is None:
        return candidate.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return candidate.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _as_utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def _retry_seconds(deadline: datetime, now: datetime) -> int:
    return max(1, math.ceil((_as_utc(deadline) - now).total_seconds()))


def _validate_pool_id(quota_pool_id: int) -> None:
    if (
        not isinstance(quota_pool_id, int)
        or isinstance(quota_pool_id, bool)
        or quota_pool_id < 1
    ):
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_id_invalid", "模型配额池标识无效"
        )


def _validate_concurrency_headroom(concurrency_headroom: int) -> None:
    if (
        not isinstance(concurrency_headroom, int)
        or isinstance(concurrency_headroom, bool)
        or concurrency_headroom < 0
    ):
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_concurrency_headroom_invalid", "模型并发预留数量无效"
        )


def batch_concurrency_headroom(pool_max: int) -> int:
    """Keep up to two slots inside the shared ceiling for online requests."""
    if not isinstance(pool_max, int) or isinstance(pool_max, bool) or pool_max < 1:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_concurrency_invalid", "模型配额池并发上限无效"
        )
    return min(2, max(0, pool_max - 1))


def _validate_acquisition(
    quota_pool_id: int,
    owner_key: str,
    reserved_tokens: int,
    reserved_images: int,
) -> str:
    _validate_pool_id(quota_pool_id)
    normalized_owner = str(owner_key or "").strip()
    if _OWNER_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_owner) is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_owner_key_invalid", "模型调用身份无效"
        )
    for value, label in (
        (reserved_tokens, "token"),
        (reserved_images, "图片"),
    ):
        if (
            not isinstance(value, int)
            or isinstance(value, bool)
            or value < 0
            or value > _MAX_RESERVATION
        ):
            raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
                "quota_reservation_invalid", f"预留{label}数量无效"
            )
    return normalized_owner


def _locked_pool(db: Session, quota_pool_id: int) -> ModelQuotaPool:
    pool = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaPool)
        .where(ModelQuotaPool.id == quota_pool_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if pool is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_missing", "模型配额池不存在"
        )
    return pool


def _snapshot(row: ModelQuotaLease, pool: ModelQuotaPool, *, reused: bool) -> QuotaReservation:
    if row.id is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_lease_missing", "模型配额租约未持久化"
        )
    return QuotaReservation(
        pool_id=pool.id,
        pool_key=pool.pool_key,
        lease_token=row.lease_token,
        owner_key=row.owner_key,
        status=row.status,
        acquired_at=_as_utc(row.acquired_at),
        expires_at=_as_utc(row.expires_at),
        reserved_tokens=row.reserved_tokens,
        reserved_images=row.reserved_images,
        reused=reused,
    )


def _expire_leases(
    db: Session,
    *,
    pool_id: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    # Do not fetch affected IDs or hydrate the pool's lease history. All lease
    # writers acquire the pool lock first, so expiration shares their ordering.
    db.execute(
        update(ModelQuotaLease)
        .where(
            ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool_id,
            ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
            ModelQuotaLease.expires_at <= now,
        )
        .values(status="expired", released_at=now)
        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
    )
    # Preserve same-session callers holding a lease object without a SELECT for
    # every expired row. Missing attributes already refresh on their next read.
    for row in db.identity_map.values():
        if not isinstance(row, ModelQuotaLease):
            continue
        cached = inspect(row).dict
        if cached.get("pool_id") not in (None, pool_id):
            continue
        if cached.get("status") not in (None, "active"):
            continue
        deadline = cached.get("expires_at")
        if deadline is None or _as_utc(deadline) <= now:
            db.expire(row, ["status", "released_at"])


def _minute_window(
    db: Session,
    *,
    pool_id: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> ModelQuotaMinuteWindow:
    start = now.replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow)
        .where(
            ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.pool_id == pool_id,
            ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.window_start == start,
        )
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if row is None:
        row = ModelQuotaMinuteWindow(pool_id=pool_id, window_start=start)
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    return row


def _check_rate_limit(
    *,
    pool: ModelQuotaPool,
    window: ModelQuotaMinuteWindow,
    reserved_tokens: int,
    reserved_images: int,
    now: datetime,
) -> None:
    retry_after = _retry_seconds(
        now.replace(second=0, microsecond=0) + timedelta(minutes=1), now
    )
    checks = (
        (
            pool.requests_per_minute,
            window.request_count + 1,
            "quota_pool_requests_per_minute_exhausted",
            "模型供应商每分钟请求额度已用完",
        ),
        (
            pool.tokens_per_minute,
            window.token_count + reserved_tokens,
            "quota_pool_tokens_per_minute_exhausted",
            "模型供应商每分钟 token 额度已用完",
        ),
        (
            pool.images_per_minute,
            window.image_count + reserved_images,
            "quota_pool_images_per_minute_exhausted",
            "模型供应商每分钟图片额度已用完",
        ),
    )
    for limit, projected, code, message in checks:
        if limit is not None and projected > limit:
            raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
                code,
                message,
                pool_key=pool.pool_key,
                retry_after_seconds=retry_after,
            )


def _check_pool_ready(pool: Any, current: datetime) -> None:
    if not pool.enabled:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_disabled",
            "模型配额池已停用",
            pool_key=pool.pool_key,
        )
    if pool.cooldown_until is not None and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > current:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_cooling_down",
            "模型供应商正在限流冷却，请稍后重试",
            pool_key=pool.pool_key,
            retry_after_seconds=_retry_seconds(pool.cooldown_until, current),
        )


def _check_concurrency(
    pool: Any,
    active_count: int,
    earliest_expiry: datetime | None,
    concurrency_headroom: int,
    current: datetime,
) -> None:
    if active_count >= max(1, pool.max_concurrency - concurrency_headroom):
        # A release may free capacity before the protective lease expires.
        retry_after = min(_retry_seconds(earliest_expiry, current), 5)
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted",
            "模型供应商并发额度已占满",
            pool_key=pool.pool_key,
            retry_after_seconds=retry_after,
        )


def check_model_quota_available(
    db: Session,
    *,
    quota_pool_id: int,
    concurrency_headroom: int = 0,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Read a concurrency hint without reserving, expiring or flushing rows.

    Success cannot authorize dispatch: acquire_model_quota must still reserve
    under the pool lock after preparation. Rate budgets are checked there too.
    """
    _validate_pool_id(quota_pool_id)
    _validate_concurrency_headroom(concurrency_headroom)
    current = _now(now)
    with db.no_autoflush:
        # Select columns so a previously cached ORM pool cannot hide current
        # configuration, and unrelated pending writes remain untouched.
        pool = db.execute(select(
            ModelQuotaPool.id, ModelQuotaPool.pool_key, ModelQuotaPool.enabled,
            ModelQuotaPool.cooldown_until, ModelQuotaPool.max_concurrency,
        ).where(ModelQuotaPool.id == quota_pool_id)).one_or_none()
        if pool is None:
            raise ModelQuotaUnavailable("quota_pool_missing", "模型配额池不存在")
        _check_pool_ready(pool, current)
        active_count, earliest_expiry = db.execute(select(
            func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id), func.min(ModelQuotaLease.expires_at),
        ).where(
            ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
            ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
            ModelQuotaLease.expires_at > current,
        )).one()
        _check_concurrency(pool, active_count, earliest_expiry, concurrency_headroom, current)


def acquire_model_quota(
    db: Session,
    *,
    quota_pool_id: int,
    owner_key: str,
    reserved_tokens: int = 0,
    reserved_images: int = 0,
    concurrency_headroom: int = 0,
    batch_submission: bool = False,
    submission_estimate: dict | None = None,
    fair_ticket: str | None = None,
    fairness_exempt: bool = False,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> QuotaReservation:
    """Reserve concurrency and the current UTC-minute budget atomically.

    The caller must commit this transaction before making the provider request.
    """

    normalized_owner = _validate_acquisition(
        quota_pool_id, owner_key, reserved_tokens, reserved_images
    )
    _validate_concurrency_headroom(concurrency_headroom)
    from .runtime_observability import observe_pool_acquisition
    with observe_pool_acquisition(db, quota_pool_id):
        reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
        pool = _locked_pool(db, quota_pool_id)
    # Waiting for another process may span a resume, cooldown or UTC minute.
    # Use a single post-lock clock for all authoritative quota/pacing checks.
    current = _now(now)
    _check_pool_ready(pool, current)

    _expire_leases(db, pool_id=pool.id, now=current)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
            ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
            ModelQuotaLease.owner_key == normalized_owner,
        )
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if existing is not None and existing.status == "active":
        if (
            existing.reserved_tokens != reserved_tokens
            or existing.reserved_images != reserved_images
        ):
            raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
                "quota_lease_idempotency_conflict",
                "相同模型调用身份已预留不同额度",
                pool_key=pool.pool_key,
            )
        return _snapshot(existing, pool, reused=True)

    # The pool lock serializes lease writers. The supported MySQL runtime
    # enforces READ COMMITTED (PostgreSQL also defaults to READ COMMITTED), so
    # this statement sees admissions committed before we obtained that lock.
    # SQLite holds BEGIN IMMEDIATE. Return one row without locking/hydrating
    # every live lease; aggregate FOR UPDATE is also invalid on PostgreSQL.
    active_count, earliest_expiry = db.execute(
        select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id), func.min(ModelQuotaLease.expires_at))
        .where(
            ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
            ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
        )
    ).one()
    _check_concurrency(pool, active_count, earliest_expiry, concurrency_headroom, current)

    window = _minute_window(db, pool_id=pool.id, now=current)
    _check_rate_limit(
        pool=pool,
        window=window,
        reserved_tokens=reserved_tokens,
        reserved_images=reserved_images,
        now=current,
    )
    submission_state = None
    if batch_submission and pool.provider == 'doubao':
        from .batch_submission import check_submission
        # This pool lock also serializes the invocation evidence written by
        # reserve_model_invocation before its commit. A denied submission has
        # not consumed a lease, minute budget, or paid invocation identity.
        submission_state = check_submission(db, pool=pool,
            reserved_tokens=reserved_tokens, now=current,
            submission_estimate=submission_estimate, fair_ticket=fair_ticket,
            active_leases=active_count, fairness_exempt=fairness_exempt)
    window.request_count += 1
    window.token_count += reserved_tokens
    window.image_count += reserved_images
    window.updated_at = current

    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    expires_at = current + timedelta(seconds=pool.lease_seconds)
    if existing is None:
        existing = ModelQuotaLease(
            pool_id=pool.id,
            lease_token=token,
            owner_key=normalized_owner,
            status="active",
            reserved_tokens=reserved_tokens,
            reserved_images=reserved_images,
            acquired_at=current,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )
        db.add(existing)
    else:
        existing.lease_token = token
        existing.status = "active"
        existing.reserved_tokens = reserved_tokens
        existing.reserved_images = reserved_images
        existing.acquired_at = current
        existing.expires_at = expires_at
        existing.released_at = None
    db.flush()
    return replace(_snapshot(existing, pool, reused=False), submission_state=submission_state)


def release_model_quota(
    db: Session,
    *,
    lease_token: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> QuotaReservation:
    current = _now(now)
    normalized_token = str(lease_token or "").strip()
    if not normalized_token or len(normalized_token) > 80:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_lease_token_invalid", "模型配额租约标识无效"
        )
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    pool_id = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaLease.pool_id).where(
            ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == normalized_token
        )
    )
    if pool_id is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_lease_missing", "模型配额租约不存在"
        )
    pool = _locked_pool(db, pool_id)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaLease)
        .where(ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == normalized_token)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if row is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_lease_missing", "模型配额租约不存在"
        )
    if row.status in {"released", "expired"}:
        return _snapshot(row, pool, reused=True)
    row.status = "released"
    row.released_at = current
    db.flush()
    return _snapshot(row, pool, reused=False)


def cool_down_model_quota(
    db: Session,
    *,
    quota_pool_id: int,
    retry_after_seconds: int,
    reason: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if (
        not isinstance(retry_after_seconds, int)
        or isinstance(retry_after_seconds, bool)
        or retry_after_seconds < 1
    ):
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_cooldown_invalid", "模型配额池冷却时间无效"
        )
    normalized_reason = str(reason or "").strip()
    if _COOLDOWN_REASON.fullmatch(normalized_reason) is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_cooldown_reason_invalid", "模型配额池冷却原因无效"
        )
    current = _now(now)
    bounded_seconds = min(retry_after_seconds, _MAX_COOLDOWN_SECONDS)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    pool = _locked_pool(db, quota_pool_id)
    proposed = current + timedelta(seconds=bounded_seconds)
    if pool.cooldown_until is None or _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) < proposed:
        pool.cooldown_until = proposed
    pool.cooldown_reason = normalized_reason
    pool.updated_at = current
    db.flush()
    return model_quota_snapshot(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, now=current)


def model_quota_snapshot(
    db: Session,
    *,
    quota_pool_id: int,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    current = _now(now)
    pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, quota_pool_id)
    if pool is None:
        raise ModelQuotaUnavailable(
            "quota_pool_missing", "模型配额池不存在"
        )
    active = [
        row
        for row in db.scalars(
            select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                ModelQuotaLease.pool_id == pool.id,
                ModelQuotaLease.status == "active",
            )
        ).all()
        if _as_utc(row.expires_at) > current
    ]
    start = current.replace(second=0, microsecond=0)
    window = db.scalar(
        select(ModelQuotaMinuteWindow).where(
            ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.pool_id == pool.id,
            ModelQuotaMinuteWindow.window_start == start,
        )
    )
    cooling = (
        pool.cooldown_until is not None
        and _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until) > current
    )
    return {
        "pool_id": pool.id,
        "pool_key": pool.pool_key,
        "provider": pool.provider,
        "enabled": pool.enabled,
        "cooling_down": cooling,
        "cooldown_until": (
            _as_utc(pool.cooldown_until).isoformat()
            if pool.cooldown_until is not None
            else None
        ),
        "max_concurrency": pool.max_concurrency,
        "active_concurrency": len(active),
        "requests_per_minute": pool.requests_per_minute,
        "requests_reserved_this_minute": window.request_count if window else 0,
        "tokens_per_minute": pool.tokens_per_minute,
        "tokens_reserved_this_minute": window.token_count if window else 0,
        "images_per_minute": pool.images_per_minute,
        "images_reserved_this_minute": window.image_count if window else 0,
    }


# Provider-neutral names make the same database admission contract reusable for
# non-model APIs (for example reverse-image search) without adding a second,
# competing concurrency counter.  The original names remain stable for model
# callers and migrations.
acquire_provider_quota = acquire_model_quota
release_provider_quota = release_model_quota
cool_down_provider_quota = cool_down_model_quota
provider_quota_snapshot = model_quota_snapshot

===== FILE backend/app/model_snapshot_drift.py =====
"""评测机制版本冻结的模型快照 vs 模型管理里的当前配置。

run178 的教训：机制版本 wv53 于 8 月 27 日冻结了 `doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215`，
9 月 1 日运营在模型管理里把模型 ID 改成 `…260428`；执行器逐条判定
``model_config_snapshot_drift``，400 条素材一次模型都没调就全部失败，而发起页、
用量预估、版本下拉框事前都没有任何提示。这里把执行器的判定抽成可复用的
「事前」检查，供发起预检、用量预估和版本列表共用。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import ModelConfig
from .strategy_bundle import StrategySecretError, build_model_config_snapshot
from .workflow_prompt_execution import prompt_execution_node

# 与 workflow_model_executor 的判定字段保持一致：任一变化都会让执行器拒绝调用。
TRANSPORT_FIELDS = ("provider", "protocol", "base_url", "api_path", "model_id")

_FIELD_LABELS = {
    "provider": "供应商",
    "protocol": "协议",
    "base_url": "调用端点",
    "api_path": "调用端点",
    "model_id": "模型 ID",
}


def model_snapshot_drift_issues(
    db: Session,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """返回每个提示词节点冻结模型与当前配置的偏差；空列表即可安全发起。"""

    nodes_value = manifest.get("nodes")
    nodes = (
        [item for item in nodes_value if isinstance(item, Mapping)]
        if isinstance(nodes_value, list)
        else []
    )
    issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    current_cache: dict[int, dict[str, Any] | None] = {}
    for node in nodes:
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        executable = prompt_execution_node(node)
        if executable is None or not isinstance(node_id, str):
            continue
        config = executable.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot")
        model_snapshot = (
            model_snapshot if isinstance(model_snapshot, Mapping) else {}
        )
        source_id = model_snapshot.get("source_model_config_id")
        if not isinstance(source_id, int) or isinstance(source_id, bool):
            continue
        frozen_runtime = model_snapshot.get("runtime_config")
        frozen_runtime = (
            frozen_runtime if isinstance(frozen_runtime, Mapping) else {}
        )
        model_name = str(model_snapshot.get("name") or f"模型 #{source_id}")
        frozen_model_id = str(
            frozen_runtime.get("model_id") or model_snapshot.get("model_id") or ""
        )
        if source_id not in current_cache:
            current = db.get(ModelConfig, source_id)
            if current is None:
                current_cache[source_id] = None
            else:
                try:
                    current_cache[source_id] = build_model_config_snapshot(current)
                except (StrategySecretError, ValueError):
                    current_cache[source_id] = {"__invalid__": True}
        current_runtime = current_cache[source_id]
        if current_runtime is None:
            issues.append(
                {
                    "node_instance_id": node_id,
                    "model_name": model_name,
                    "frozen_model_id": frozen_model_id,
                    "current_model_id": None,
                    "changed_fields": ["deleted"],
                    "message": f"该版本冻结的「{model_name}」已在模型管理中删除",
                }
            )
            continue
        if current_runtime.get("__invalid__"):
            issues.append(
                {
                    "node_instance_id": node_id,
                    "model_name": model_name,
                    "frozen_model_id": frozen_model_id,
                    "current_model_id": None,
                    "changed_fields": ["invalid"],
                    "message": f"「{model_name}」的当前配置未通过安全校验",
                }
            )
            continue
        changed = [
            field
            for field in TRANSPORT_FIELDS
            if current_runtime.get(field) != frozen_runtime.get(field)
        ]
        if not changed:
            continue
        current_model_id = str(current_runtime.get("model_id") or "")
        labels = sorted({_FIELD_LABELS[field] for field in changed})
        if "model_id" in changed:
            detail = (
                f"当时冻结的是 {frozen_model_id or '未知'}，"
                f"模型管理里现在是 {current_model_id or '未知'}"
            )
        else:
            detail = "、".join(labels) + "已变化"
        changed_text = "、".join(labels)
        # 「模型 ID 已变」在 ASCII 结尾处补一个空格，中文结尾（调用端点已变）不补
        changed_text += " 已变" if changed_text[-1].isascii() else "已变"
        issues.append(
            {
                "node_instance_id": node_id,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "frozen_model_id": frozen_model_id,
                "current_model_id": current_model_id,
                "changed_fields": changed,
                "message": f"「{model_name}」{detail}（{changed_text}）",
            }
        )
    return issues


def model_snapshot_issue_summary(issues: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str | None:
    """压成一句给下拉框/错误提示用的人话；无偏差返回 None。"""

    if not issues:
        return None
    seen: list[str] = []
    for issue in issues:
        message = str(issue.get("message") or "")
        if message and message not in seen:
            seen.append(message)
    return "；".join(seen)

        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
