class DownstreamPublishLedger(Base):
    """下游写入变更日志（ADR-0064）：每次向下游发布一版就追加一行，永不覆盖。

    下游目标表只保留素材最新一行；平台侧凭本表还原「谁、何时、因何、把哪些字段改成了什么」。
    outbox_event_id 一对一绑定 Outbox 事件；投递结果回写到 delivery_status/delivered_at。
    """

    __tablename__ = "downstream_publish_ledger"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("outbox_event_id", name="uq_downstream_publish_ledger_event"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "trigger_kind IN ('auto_run','human_correction')",
            name="ck_downstream_publish_ledger_trigger",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "review_state IN ('auto','reviewed')",
            name="ck_downstream_publish_ledger_review_state",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "delivery_status IN ('pending','retrying','delivered','rejected','dead_lettered')",
            name="ck_downstream_publish_ledger_delivery_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("revision >= 1", name="ck_downstream_publish_ledger_revision"),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    outbox_event_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    target_contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    target_contract_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    business_idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(240), index=True)
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default="1")
    review_state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="auto", server_default="auto", index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True, index=True)
    source_type: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    source_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    asset_identity: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(240), default="", server_default="")
    mechanism_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    origin_run_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    node_instance_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), default="", server_default="")
    trigger_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="auto_run", server_default="auto_run")
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="", server_default="")
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    correction_event_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="", server_default="")
    previous_revision: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    previous_payload_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), default="", server_default="")
    changed_paths_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]", server_default="[]")
    payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    payload_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), default="", server_default="")
    delivery_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="pending", server_default="pending", index=True)
    rejection_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="")
    external_record_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(240), default="", server_default="")
    delivered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc), index=True
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )


class ModelQuotaPool(Base):
    """Shared provider quota scope used by every model credential bound to it."""

    __tablename__ = "model_quota_pools"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "provider",
            "scope_kind",
            "scope_identifier",
            name="uq_model_quota_pools_provider_scope",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "max_concurrency >= 1 AND lease_seconds >= 1",
            name="ck_model_quota_pools_positive_limits",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(requests_per_minute IS NULL OR requests_per_minute >= 1) AND "
            "(tokens_per_minute IS NULL OR tokens_per_minute >= 1) AND "
            "(images_per_minute IS NULL OR images_per_minute >= 1)",
            name="ck_model_quota_pools_rate_limits",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    pool_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), unique=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    provider: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    scope_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    scope_identifier: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    max_concurrency: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=8, server_default="8")
    requests_per_minute: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    tokens_per_minute: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    images_per_minute: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    lease_seconds: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=180, server_default="180")
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, default=True, server_default=sql_text("1"), index=True
    )
    cooldown_until: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    cooldown_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, server_default=sql_text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=utcnow,
        onupdate=utcnow,
        server_default=sql_text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"),
    )

    models: Mapped[list["ModelConfig"]] = relationship(back_populates="quota_pool")
    leases: Mapped[list["ModelQuotaLease"]] = relationship(back_populates="pool")
    minute_windows: Mapped[list["ModelQuotaMinuteWindow"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="pool"
    )

class ImageFirstSeenCache(Base):
    """Redacted, content-addressed facts returned by an image-search provider."""

    __tablename__ = "image_first_seen_cache"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "provider_key",
            "provider_version",
            "image_digest",
            name="uq_image_first_seen_provider_version_digest",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('found','not_found','timeout','rate_limited','unknown','failed')",
            name="ck_image_first_seen_cache_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "evidence_count >= 0",
            name="ck_image_first_seen_cache_evidence_count",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    provider_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    provider_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    image_digest: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), index=True)
    earliest_discovered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    evidence_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    response_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=utcnow,
        server_default=sql_text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"),
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=utcnow,
        onupdate=utcnow,
        server_default=sql_text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"),
    )

        back_populates="model", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    quota_pool: Mapped[ModelQuotaPool | None] = relationship(back_populates="models")


# 迁移 129：唯一约束对 NULL 不生效（全局绑定 category_key IS NULL），用存储生成列把
# NULL 折成 '' 再约束。表达式在 SQLite 与 MySQL 通用；约束名在迁移与模型间共享。
MODEL_NODE_CATEGORY_SCOPE_SQL = "COALESCE(category_key, '')"
MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE = "uq_model_node_bindings_node_scope"
PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE = "uq_prompt_versions_category_stage_version"


def _unique_violation(exc: BaseException, names: tuple[str, ...], column_sets: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    # MySQL: "Duplicate entry '…' for key 'table.<name>'"；SQLite 列约束："UNIQUE constraint
    # failed: t.a, t.b"，表达式索引："UNIQUE constraint failed: index '<name>'"。
    message = str(getattr(exc, "orig", None) or exc)
    return any(name in message for name in names) or any(
        f"UNIQUE constraint failed: {columns}" in message for columns in column_sets
    )


def is_model_node_binding_conflict(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
    return _unique_violation(
        exc,
        (MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE, "uq_model_node_bindings_node_category"),
        (
            "model_node_bindings.node_key, model_node_bindings.category_scope",
            "model_node_bindings.node_key, model_node_bindings.category_key",
        ),
    )


def is_prompt_version_conflict(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
    return _unique_violation(
        exc,
        (PROMPT_VERSION_UNIQUE,),
        ("prompt_versions.category_key, prompt_versions.stage, prompt_versions.version",),
    )


class ModelNodeBinding(Base):
    """Maps a pipeline node to a registered model without changing old snapshots."""

    __tablename__ = "model_node_bindings"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("node_key", "category_key", name="uq_model_node_bindings_node_category"),
        UniqueConstraint("node_key", "category_scope", name=MODEL_NODE_SCOPE_UNIQUE),
        CheckConstraint(
            "node_key IN ('evaluation_main','pdf_summary','optimization','benchmark','diagnostic')",
            name="ck_model_node_bindings_node_key",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    node_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    model_config_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("model_configs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True, index=True)
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=sql_text("1"))
    updated_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    # 只读生成列，放最后一列：MySQL 升级步 ADD COLUMN 追加在表尾，与基线建表列序一致。
    category_scope: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), Computed(MODEL_NODE_CATEGORY_SCOPE_SQL, persisted=True), nullable=False
    )
    model: Mapped[ModelConfig] = relationship(back_populates="node_bindings")

class ModelRegistryEntry(Base):
    """Unified non-secret model registry for main and tuning roles.

    Existing ``ModelConfig`` and ``OptimizerConfig`` rows remain the runtime
    compatibility stores for this phase.  Registry entries carry a stable,
    role-aware management projection and may optionally point back to one of
    those legacy rows.  Historical task snapshots never depend on a live
    registry row.
    """

    __tablename__ = "model_registry_entries"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("source_model_config_id", name="uq_model_registry_source_model"),
        UniqueConstraint("source_optimizer_config_id", name="uq_model_registry_source_optimizer"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "role IN ('main','tuning','benchmark')",
            name="ck_model_registry_role",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "protocol IN ('openai_chat','openai_responses','anthropic_messages','custom_json')",
            name="ck_model_registry_protocol",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(vision_capability_profile_json) "
            "AND json_type(vision_capability_profile_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_model_registry_vision_capability_profile",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(pricing_contract_json) "
            "AND json_type(pricing_contract_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_model_registry_pricing_contract",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="main", index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    provider: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="doubao")
    protocol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="openai_chat")
    capabilities_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default='["text","vision","structured_output"]', server_default='["text","vision","structured_output"]'
    )
    vision_capability_profile_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    pricing_contract_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    base_url: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    api_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    model_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), index=True)
    encrypted_api_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    temperature: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.1)
    max_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=4096)
    timeout_seconds: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=120)
    max_retries: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    max_concurrency: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=8)
    max_requests_per_minute: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=sql_text("0"))
    max_input_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=sql_text("0"))
    input_micros_per_million_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=sql_text("0"))
    output_micros_per_million_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=sql_text("0"))
    monthly_budget_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=sql_text("0"))
    thinking_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="auto", server_default="auto")
    level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="standard", server_default="standard")
    structured_output: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=sql_text("1"))
    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, index=True)
    source_model_config_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("model_configs.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    source_optimizer_config_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("optimizer_configs.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow)

    )

class Asset(Base):
    __tablename__ = "assets"
    # NAS 来源按评测类目唯一：同一张图可以分别进入不同类目各自评测，
    # 与本地上传按类目去重的口径一致。
    __table_args__ = (
        Index(
            "uq_assets_source_uri_category",
            "source_uri",
            "category_key",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("source_uri IS NOT NULL"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    original_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500))
    stored_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), unique=True)
    storage_backend: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="local", server_default="local", index=True
    )
    source_uri: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(1000), nullable=True)
    mime_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    size_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    width: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    height: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    sha256: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="space_image", server_default="space_image", index=True
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="uploaded", index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class DesignCaseSample(Base):
    """素材（案例）维度样本：一行一个案例，挂在 assets 上但没有图片文件。

    案例文本与图片聚合摘要（总张数/分数分布/类型分布）都存在这里，
    让案例能像图片素材一样进基准集、跑回归、走纠偏。
    """

    __tablename__ = "design_case_samples"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("asset_id", name="uq_design_case_samples_asset"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "input_kind IN ('text_only','aggregated')",
            name="ck_design_case_samples_input_kind",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    cloud_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="", index=True)
    case_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), default="")
    input_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="text_only", server_default="text_only", index=True
    )
    case_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    image_summary_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    # 图片维度：逐图人工档位与该图在平台的资产身份。
    # {"images": [{"cloud_id": "...", "level": "L2", "asset_id": 1, "asset_sha256": "..."}]}
    # 素材维度的案例没有平台图片，这里保持空对象。
    image_grades_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    source_package_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("material_packages.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    source_row_number: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), default="", server_default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class AssetVersion(Base):
    """Immutable source snapshot for one asset revision."""

    __tablename__ = "asset_versions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("asset_id", "version", name="uq_asset_versions_asset_version"),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "source_system",
            "source_content_id",
            "source_version",
            name="uq_asset_versions_source_identity",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(asset_sha256) = 64",
            name="ck_asset_versions_sha256",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "snapshot_kind IN ('materialized','deleted')",
            name="ck_asset_versions_snapshot_kind",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    asset_sha256: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    source_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    storage_backend: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="local", server_default="local"
    )
    source_uri: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(1000), nullable=True
    )
    source_system: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    source_content_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(160), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    mime_type: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    size_bytes: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    occurred_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    payload_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    supersedes_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("asset_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    snapshot_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="materialized")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )

    asset: Mapped[Asset] = relationship()


class EvaluationCategoryProfile(Base):
    """Per-category pipeline contract; configuration never leaks across categories."""

    __tablename__ = "evaluation_category_profiles"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','active','retired')",
            name="ck_evaluation_category_profiles_status",
        ),
        UniqueConstraint("category_key", name="uq_evaluation_category_profiles_key"),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="active", index=True)
    allowed_mime_types_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    preprocess_config_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    pipeline_config_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    pipeline_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default="1")
    prompt_a_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    prompt_b_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    model_config_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("model_configs.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    optimizer_config_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("optimizer_configs.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    automation_config_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text,
        default=(
            '{"enabled":true,"case_threshold":1,"cooldown_seconds":0,'
            '"max_candidates":1}'
        ),
        server_default='{"enabled":true,"case_threshold":1,"cooldown_seconds":0,"max_candidates":1}',
    )
    automation_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default="1")
    automation_last_triggered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    rubric_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), default="rubric-v2.1")
    dimension_schema_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    dimension_schema_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class CategoryEvaluationV3Config(Base):
    """Persisted ADR-0033 category-evaluation contract.

    Every new full or baseline run freezes the category's active row. Its
    contract and subcategory dimensions are the sole runtime authority; legacy
    profile dimension fields remain historical metadata.
    """

    __tablename__ = "category_evaluation_v3_configs"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','active','retired')",
            name="ck_category_evaluation_v3_configs_status",
        ),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "category_key", name="uq_category_evaluation_v3_configs_key"
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="draft", server_default="draft", index=True
    )
    contract_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    classification_map_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    subcategory_dimensions_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    # v3 rule-deduction mirror.  The authoritative definitions remain nested in
    # ``subcategory_dimensions_json``; this column is derived on every write so
    # migrations and operators can inspect/back up the rule set without parsing
    # the complete dimension contract.  ``dimension_grade_points_json`` is not
    # removed from historical schema variants; grade_points inside the frozen
    # JSON stay supported as the deprecated fallback path.
    dimension_deduction_rules_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    media_penalty_enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, default=True, server_default="1"
    )
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default="1")
    contract_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), default="")
    projected_revision_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("category_evaluation_v3_revisions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(Base):
    """Append-only category mechanism artifact revision.

    ``CategoryEvaluationV3Config`` remains the runtime projection consumed by
    workers.  These rows preserve every candidate and projected artifact so an
    editor can append changes without mutating the active runtime contract.
    Database triggers installed by migration 63 freeze identity and artifact
    fields; only lifecycle ``status`` and ``updated_at`` may change later.
    """

    __tablename__ = "category_evaluation_v3_revisions"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','candidate','active','retired')",
            name="ck_category_evaluation_v3_revisions_status",
        ),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "category_key",
            "revision",
            name="uq_category_evaluation_v3_revisions_key_revision",
        ),
        Index(
            "uq_category_evaluation_v3_revisions_active",
            "category_key",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("status = 'active'"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="candidate", server_default="candidate", index=True
    )
    parent_revision_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("category_evaluation_v3_revisions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    contract_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    classification_map_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    subcategory_dimensions_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    dimension_deduction_rules_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    media_penalty_enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, default=True, server_default="1"
    )
    contract_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class BaselineExportJob(Base):
    """一次存量回归结果导出。

    嵌缩略图要逐张读 NAS 原图，400 条实测 99 秒，做不成同步下载，因此落成
    后台任务：接口先建行、后台跑、完成后凭 id 下载。产物写在磁盘上，行里只
    存路径与大小，避免把几 MB 的二进制塞进库。
    """

    __tablename__ = "baseline_export_jobs"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('queued','running','succeeded','failed')",
            name="ck_baseline_export_jobs_status",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="queued", server_default="queued", index=True
    )
    include_images: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, default=True, server_default="1"
    )
    row_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    image_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    missing_image_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    file_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), default="", server_default="")
    file_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="", server_default="")
    file_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="", server_default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    started_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )


class MaterialPackage(Base):
    __tablename__ = "material_packages"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "source IN ('manual_upload','production_import','legacy_backfill')",
            name="ck_material_packages_source",
        ),
        Index(
            "ix_material_packages_incremental_lane_state",
            "status",
            "incremental_lane_key",
            "id",
        ),
        Index(
            "ix_material_packages_incremental_due_state",
            "status",
            "incremental_due_at",
            "id",
        ),
        Index(
            "ix_material_packages_incremental_retry_state",
            "status",
            "incremental_retry_at",
            "id",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="active", server_default="active", index=True
    )
    package_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), unique=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="manual_upload", index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="space_image", server_default="space_image", index=True
    )
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    mechanism_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    incremental_event_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("content_ingress_events.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    incremental_snapshot_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    incremental_lane_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    incremental_batch_size: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=True
    )
    incremental_due_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    incremental_retry_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    incremental_failure_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    items: Mapped[list["MaterialPackageItem"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="package",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        order_by="MaterialPackageItem.position",
    )

class EvaluationProductionRun(Base):
    """Mutable orchestration record; the final EvaluationPackage stays separate."""

    __tablename__ = "evaluation_production_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "idempotency_key", name="uq_evaluation_production_runs_key"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ("
            "'preparing','queued','evaluating','first_review','optimizing',"
            "'regressing','awaiting_review','approved','rejected','published',"
            "'blocked','failed','archived'"
            ")",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "workflow_kind IN ('incremental','stock')",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_workflow_kind",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(category_profile_snapshot_json) AND "
            "json_type(category_profile_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_profile_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(job_ids_json) AND json_type(job_ids_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_job_ids_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(blockers_json) AND json_type(blockers_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_blockers_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(request_hash) = 64 AND request_hash = lower(request_hash) "
            "AND request_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_request_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(category_profile_hash) = 64 "
            "AND category_profile_hash = lower(category_profile_hash) "
            "AND category_profile_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_profile_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "audit_revision >= 1",
            name="ck_evaluation_production_runs_audit_revision",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(160), unique=True, index=True
    )
    request_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    material_package_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("material_packages.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    workflow_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="incremental", server_default="incremental", index=True
    )
    category_profile_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    category_profile_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    correction_contract_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=True
    )
    correction_contract_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    job_ids_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    batch_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), unique=True, index=True)
    automation_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("automation_optimization_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    regression_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    evaluation_package_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("evaluation_packages.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        unique=True,
        index=True,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="preparing", index=True
    )
    current_stage: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="preparing", index=True
    )
    blockers_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    audit_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )
    started_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    last_reconciled_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    archived_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    material_package: Mapped[MaterialPackage] = relationship()
    automation_run: Mapped["AutomationOptimizationRun | None"] = relationship()
    regression_run: Mapped["PromptRegressionRun | None"] = relationship()
    evaluation_package: Mapped["EvaluationPackage | None"] = relationship()

class ScriptVersion(Base):
    __tablename__ = "script_versions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "script_definition_id", "version", name="uq_script_versions_key"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','validating','active','deprecated','retired','blocked')",
            name="ck_script_versions_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "executor_kind = 'deterministic_fixture'",
            name="ck_script_versions_executor",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(artifact_sha256) = 64 AND artifact_sha256 = lower(artifact_sha256) "
            "AND artifact_sha256 NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_script_versions_sha256",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "timeout_seconds BETWEEN 1 AND 3600",
            name="ck_script_versions_timeout",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 5",
            name="ck_script_versions_attempts",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "concurrency_limit >= 1",
            name="ck_script_versions_concurrency",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    script_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("script_definitions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    executor_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="deterministic_fixture"
    )
    artifact_sha256: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    input_schema_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    output_schema_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    required_permissions_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    idempotency_template: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(320))
    timeout_seconds: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=300)
    max_attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    retry_policy_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    concurrency_limit: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    rate_limit_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(160), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    estimated_cost_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="draft", index=True)
    validation_report_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    blocked_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )

    definition: Mapped[ScriptDefinition] = relationship()


class WorkflowDefinition(Base):
    __tablename__ = "workflow_definitions"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('active','retired')",
            name="ck_workflow_definitions_status",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    workflow_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    owner: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    allowed_categories_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="active", index=True)
    # 该工作流当前绑定的豆包推理接入点（doubao_inference_endpoints.id）；一个接入点可被多个同类目工作流绑定。
    # 不声明外键，避免与接入点表形成循环依赖；引用完整性由 doubao_endpoints 服务层保证。
    doubao_endpoint_row_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    doubao_inference_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="auto", server_default="auto")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )


class WorkflowVersion(Base):
    __tablename__ = "workflow_versions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "workflow_definition_id", "version", name="uq_workflow_versions_key"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','validating','active','deprecated','retired','blocked')",
            name="ck_workflow_versions_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(canonical_hash) = 64 AND canonical_hash = lower(canonical_hash) "
            "AND canonical_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_workflow_versions_hash",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_definitions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="draft", index=True)
    workflow_schema_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="workflow-v1"
    )
    step_manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    edge_manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)

    )

    __tablename__ = "workflow_snapshot_blobs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "snapshot_hash", name="uq_workflow_snapshot_blobs_hash"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(snapshot_hash) = 64 AND snapshot_hash = lower(snapshot_hash) "
            "AND snapshot_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_workflow_snapshot_blobs_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "canonical_size_bytes >= 0 AND compressed_size_bytes >= 0",
            name="ck_workflow_snapshot_blobs_sizes",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "compression = 'gzip'",
            name="ck_workflow_snapshot_blobs_compression",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    snapshot_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), unique=True, index=True
    )
    canonical_size_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    compressed_size_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    compression: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="gzip", server_default="gzip"
    )
    payload_bytes: Mapped[bytes] = mapped_column(LargeBinary)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )

class ProductionRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "production_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_production_runs_pool_revision_status", "execution_pool", "executor_revision", "status", "id"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "queue_class IN ('validation','interactive','production_batch','canary','recovery')",
            name="ck_production_runs_queue_class",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('planned','queued','running','waiting_external','paused','succeeded','failed','retryable','blocked','canceled')",
            name="ck_production_runs_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "environment = 'dry_run'",
            name="ck_production_runs_environment",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "model_execution_mode IN ('simulated','real')",
            name="ck_production_runs_model_execution_mode",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(snapshot_hash) = 64 AND snapshot_hash = lower(snapshot_hash) "
            "AND snapshot_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_production_runs_snapshot_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "total_steps >= 0 AND completed_steps >= 0 AND failed_steps >= 0",
            name="ck_production_runs_step_counts",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "attempt_count >= 0",
            name="ck_production_runs_attempt_count",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    run_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(200), unique=True, index=True
    )
    source_type: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(60), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    source_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    # 冻结的增量 SLA 投影列（迁移 131）：派发排序/高优先谓词改读这两列，不再对每条
    # 排队 run 的 snapshot_json（平均 96 KB）做 JSON_EXTRACT。值只在创建时由
    # freeze_incremental_sla 写入，与 snapshot.runtime_context.incremental_sla 同源，之后不改。
    sla_service_class: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    sla_received_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    source_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("production_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    workflow_definition_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_definitions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    snapshot_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    snapshot_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    snapshot_blob_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_snapshot_blobs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(40), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    queue_class: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="validation", index=True
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="planned", index=True)
    current_step_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    blockers_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    requested_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    owner: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    environment: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="dry_run")
    # 本次运行首次真实调用时冻结的豆包接入点；改绑只影响之后创建的运行。
    doubao_endpoint_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True
    )
    model_execution_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="simulated", server_default="simulated", index=True
    )
    total_steps: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    completed_steps: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    failed_steps: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    last_checkpoint_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    lease_owner: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    lease_token: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    lease_expires_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    heartbeat_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    #: 因某次发布而暂存时写入该发布的 revision；恢复时只放出带本次标记的运行。
    #: 不能靠 status='paused' 区分——运营自己暂停的运行不该被发布顺带放出来。
    held_by_release: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    #: 冻结快照里 runtime_context.run_mode 的副本（incremental / stock_regression …）。
    #: 增量纠偏页曾靠 JSON_EXTRACT(snapshot_json) 过滤——每次列表都要解析两万多份 87KB
    #: 的快照，一页 14 秒（2026-09-17）。迁移 118 回填，建运行时写入；快照仍是事实来源。
    run_mode: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True, index=True)
    # Immutable execution contract, separate from the historical model snapshot.
    # Unknown legacy provenance stays in a visible compatibility pool.
    execution_pool: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="compatibility", server_default="compatibility")
    executor_revision: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="runtime-v1", server_default="runtime-v1")
    #: 上游（工厂）任务身份的副本：分类 class_id_1 与 task/img/ll 三个 ID。增量纠偏页按上游分类筛、
    #: 按 ID 溯源；冻结清单在 snapshot_json.runtime_context.input_manifest.factory_task 里，扫 JSON 太慢。
    #: 迁移 119 只回填 run_mode='incremental' 的行；建运行时写入；存量回归行保持 NULL。
    upstream_class_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True, index=True)
    upstream_task_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True, index=True)
    upstream_img_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True, index=True)
    upstream_ll_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True, index=True)
    next_retry_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )
    started_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    workflow_definition: Mapped[WorkflowDefinition] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[workflow_definition_id]
    )
    workflow_version: Mapped[WorkflowVersion] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[workflow_version_id]
    )
    snapshot_blob: Mapped[WorkflowSnapshotBlob | None] = relationship()


@event.listens_for(ProductionRun, "before_update")
def _freeze_execution_contract(_mapper, _connection, run):
    for name in ("execution_pool", "executor_revision"):
        if attributes.get_history(run, name).has_changes():
            raise ValueError("ProductionRun execution contract is immutable")

# evidence_json.failure.retry_after_seconds 的数值投影（迁移 121）。两种方言的 json_type
# 签名不同：SQLite 是 json_type(json, path)，MySQL 是 JSON_TYPE(json_value)；模型里写 SQLite
# 形式（本地测试、create_all），mysql_metadata() 与 MySQL 升级步换成 MySQL 形式。
RETRY_AFTER_HINT_SQL = (
    "CASE WHEN json_type(evidence_json, '$.failure.retry_after_seconds') IN ('integer','real') "
    "THEN CAST(json_extract(evidence_json, '$.failure.retry_after_seconds') AS DECIMAL(12,3)) END"
)
RETRY_AFTER_HINT_SQL_MYSQL = (
    "CASE WHEN JSON_TYPE(JSON_EXTRACT(evidence_json, '$.failure.retry_after_seconds')) "
    "IN ('INTEGER','DOUBLE','DECIMAL','UNSIGNED INTEGER') "
    "THEN CAST(JSON_EXTRACT(evidence_json, '$.failure.retry_after_seconds') AS DECIMAL(12,3)) END"
)

class WorkflowModelInvocation(Base):
    __tablename__ = "workflow_model_invocations"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "step_attempt_id",
            "invocation_key",
            name="uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(trim(invocation_key)) BETWEEN 1 AND 240",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_key",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('reserved','succeeded','failed','rejected')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "reserved_input_tokens >= 0 AND reserved_output_tokens >= 0 "
            "AND input_tokens >= 0 AND output_tokens >= 0 AND total_tokens >= 0",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_tokens",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "estimated_cost_micros >= 0",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_cost",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "actual_cost_micros >= 0",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_actual_cost",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(pricing_snapshot_json) "
            "AND json_type(pricing_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_pricing_snapshot",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(evidence_json) "
            "AND json_type(evidence_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_workflow_model_invocations_evidence_json",
        ),
        # 迁移 120：批量提交控制器在池行锁内读「本池最近 180 秒的 200/429」和「本池 24 小时内
        # 带长 Retry-After 的 429」。单列 quota_pool_id 索引基数为 2，两条查询每次扫全池
        # 2 万余行（2026-09-17 正式服：104 ms + 35 ms，各 4 万次），把池锁平均等待推到 1.86 秒。
        Index(
            "ix_workflow_model_invocations_pool_feedback",
            "quota_pool_id", "upstream_status_code", "finished_at",
        ),
        # 迁移 121：「24 小时内带长 Retry-After 的 429」那条反馈查询原先在池锁内对每条
        # 429 逐行解析 JSON（索引 120 之后仍扫 3,770 行、91 ms）。retry_after_hint 是
        # 从 evidence_json 派生的存储生成列，带索引后只碰真正带长提示的极少数行。
        Index(
            "ix_workflow_model_invocations_pool_retry_hint",
            "quota_pool_id", "upstream_status_code", "retry_after_hint", "finished_at",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    baseline_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    production_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("production_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    step_attempt_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("production_step_attempts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    invocation_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(240), default="root", server_default="root"
    )
    node_instance_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="reserved", server_default="reserved", index=True
    )
    provider: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    model_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    # 豆包推理接入点（ep-xxx）：渠道成本归因主键；无接入点路由时为 NULL。
    endpoint_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    quota_pool_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey(
            "model_quota_pools.id",
            ondelete="RESTRICT",
            name="fk_workflow_model_invocations_quota_pool",
        ),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    quota_lease_token: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    request_correlation_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(200), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    upstream_status_code: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=True
    )
    reserved_input_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    reserved_output_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    input_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    output_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    total_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    # 供应商上报的缓存命中输入 token；未上报或无效时保持 0。
    cached_input_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    estimated_cost_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    pricing_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    pricing_tier_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(80), default="", server_default=""
    )
    actual_cost_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(80), default="", server_default=""
    )
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    evidence_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    # evidence_json.failure.retry_after_seconds 的数值投影；非数值或缺失为 NULL。
    retry_after_hint: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(12, 3),
        Computed(RETRY_AFTER_HINT_SQL, persisted=True),
        nullable=True,
    )

    baseline_run: Mapped["BaselineRegressionRun | None"] = relationship()
    production_run: Mapped[ProductionRun] = relationship()
    step_attempt: Mapped[ProductionStepAttempt] = relationship()
    quota_pool: Mapped[ModelQuotaPool | None] = relationship()

class WorkflowModelMediaUsage(Base):
    __tablename__ = "workflow_model_media_usage"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "invocation_id",
            "media_role",
            "ordinal",
            name="uq_workflow_model_media_usage_identity",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "media_role IN ('target','anchor')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_role",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("ordinal >= 0", name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_ordinal"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(media_role = 'target' AND anchor_level IS NULL) OR "
            "(media_role = 'anchor' AND anchor_level IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_anchor_level",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "original_width >= 0 AND original_height >= 0 "
            "AND original_bytes >= 0 AND sent_width >= 0 "
            "AND sent_height >= 0 AND sent_bytes >= 0",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_dimensions",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(content_hash) = 64 AND content_hash = lower(content_hash) "
            "AND content_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_content_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(sent_hash) = 64 AND sent_hash = lower(sent_hash) "
            "AND sent_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_sent_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "compression_cache_key IS NULL OR ("
            "length(compression_cache_key) = 64 "
            "AND compression_cache_key = lower(compression_cache_key) "
            "AND compression_cache_key NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_cache_key",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "cache_outcome IN ('not_applicable','hit','miss','fallback_source')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_cache_outcome",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "usage_source IN ('provider_actual','official_estimate','unavailable')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_source",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "image_tokens >= 0 AND estimated_cost_micros >= 0 "
            "AND (actual_cost_micros IS NULL OR actual_cost_micros >= 0)",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_cost",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(evidence_json) "
            "AND json_type(evidence_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_usage_evidence_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "fidelity_mode IN ('legacy_default','detail','pixel_limit') "
            "AND preprocess_mode IN "
            "('legacy_default','original','short_edge','custom') "
            "AND size_gate_result IN ('legacy_default','pass','blocked')",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_vision_policy",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(policy_hash IS NULL OR (length(policy_hash) = 64 "
            "AND policy_hash = lower(policy_hash) "
            "AND policy_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*')) "
            "AND (normalized_pixel_hash IS NULL OR "
            "(length(normalized_pixel_hash) = 64 "
            "AND normalized_pixel_hash = lower(normalized_pixel_hash) "
            "AND normalized_pixel_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'))",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_vision_hashes",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "compression_ms >= 0 AND saved_bytes >= 0 "
            "AND saved_ratio_ppm BETWEEN 0 AND 1000000 "
            "AND provider_max_bytes >= 0 AND operational_max_bytes >= 0 "
            "AND (provider_max_bytes = 0 "
            "OR operational_max_bytes <= provider_max_bytes)",
            name="ck_workflow_model_media_transport_metrics",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    invocation_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_model_invocations.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    asset_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    media_role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    ordinal: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    anchor_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    original_width: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    original_height: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    original_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    original_format: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="", server_default="")
    sent_width: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    sent_height: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    sent_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    sent_format: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="", server_default="")
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    sent_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    compression_cache_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    cache_outcome: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="not_applicable", server_default="not_applicable", index=True
    )
    fidelity_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="legacy_default", server_default="legacy_default"
    )
    fidelity_value: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(120), default="", server_default=""
    )
    preprocess_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="legacy_default", server_default="legacy_default"
    )
    preprocess_value: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(120), default="", server_default=""
    )
    policy_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    capability_profile_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(128), default="", server_default="", index=True
    )
    normalized_pixel_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    compression_ms: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    saved_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    saved_ratio_ppm: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    provider_max_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    operational_max_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    size_gate_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="legacy_default", server_default="legacy_default", index=True
    )
    estimation_formula_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(80), default="legacy_default", server_default="legacy_default"
    )
    image_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    usage_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="unavailable", server_default="unavailable", index=True
    )
    estimated_cost_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    actual_cost_micros: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    provider_correlation_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(200), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    evidence_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    settled_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    invocation: Mapped[WorkflowModelInvocation] = relationship()
    asset: Mapped["Asset | None"] = relationship()

    __tablename__ = "runtime_dispatch_items"

        CheckConstraint(

            name="ck_runtime_dispatch_items_status",

            "attempt_count >= 0 AND max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 100",
            name="ck_runtime_dispatch_items_attempts",
        ),
    )

        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    max_attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=3, server_default="3"

        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )

        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    leased_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    heartbeat_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True

        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True

    )

        Text, default="", server_default=""
    )

        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True

        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow

class RuntimeOutboxEvent(Base):
    __tablename__ = "runtime_outbox_events"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("event_key", name="uq_runtime_outbox_events_key"),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "target_contract_key",
            "business_idempotency_key",
            "revision",
            name="uq_runtime_outbox_events_business_identity",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('queued','leased','delivered','dead_lettered','canceled')",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "revision >= 1",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_revision",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "review_state IN ('auto','reviewed')",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_review_state",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "attempt_count >= 0 AND max_attempts BETWEEN 1 AND 100",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_attempts",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(payload_hash) = 64 AND payload_hash = lower(payload_hash) "
            "AND payload_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_payload_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(delivery_receipt_json) "
            "AND json_type(delivery_receipt_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_runtime_outbox_events_receipt_json",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    event_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), unique=True, index=True)
    target_contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    target_contract_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("connector_contract_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    business_idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(240), index=True
    )
    # ADR-0064：同一素材身份的多版本事件并存；revision 单调递增，下游按它做覆盖护栏
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=1, server_default="1"
    )
    # auto=模型自动结果；reviewed=人工纠偏后重发（只能由纠偏产生）
    review_state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="auto", server_default="auto", index=True
    )
    node_instance_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(160), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    mechanism_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    payload_blob_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_snapshot_blobs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    payload_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="queued", server_default="queued", index=True
    )
    available_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=0, server_default="0"
    )
    max_attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=5, server_default="5"
    )
    lease_owner: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    lease_token: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    leased_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    heartbeat_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    lease_expires_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    last_error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(80), default="", server_default=""
    )
    last_error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="", server_default=""
    )
    delivery_receipt_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    delivered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    dead_lettered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )

    mechanism_version: Mapped[WorkflowVersion] = relationship()
    payload_blob: Mapped[WorkflowSnapshotBlob] = relationship()
    target_contract_version: Mapped["ConnectorContractVersion | None"] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[target_contract_version_id]
    )


class ConnectorContractVersion(Base):
    __tablename__ = "connector_contract_versions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "contract_key", "version", name="uq_connector_contract_version"
        ),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "contract_key", "contract_hash", name="uq_connector_contract_hash"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "direction IN ('upstream','downstream')",
            name="ck_connector_contract_direction",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','active','retired')",
            name="ck_connector_contract_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "batch_size BETWEEN 1 AND 1000 AND timeout_seconds BETWEEN 1 AND 600",
            name="ck_connector_contract_limits",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(schema_fingerprint) = 64 AND schema_fingerprint = lower(schema_fingerprint) "
            "AND schema_fingerprint NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_connector_contract_schema_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(contract_hash) = 64 AND contract_hash = lower(contract_hash) "
            "AND contract_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_connector_contract_hash_shape",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(field_mapping_json) AND json_type(field_mapping_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_connector_contract_field_mapping",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(cursor_contract_json) AND json_type(cursor_contract_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_connector_contract_cursor",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(idempotency_contract_json) "
            "AND json_type(idempotency_contract_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_connector_contract_idempotency",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    direction: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    logical_locator: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    adapter_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    schema_fingerprint: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    field_mapping_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    cursor_contract_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    idempotency_contract_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    batch_size: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=100, server_default="100")
    timeout_seconds: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=30, server_default="30")
    owner: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="draft", server_default="draft", index=True)
    contract_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system", server_default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)

        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(payload_hash) = 64 AND payload_hash = lower(payload_hash) "
            "AND payload_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",

        ),
        CheckConstraint(

    received_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    mechanism_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="", server_default="", index=True)
    payload_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    disposition: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="accepted", server_default="accepted", index=True)
    replay_of_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("upstream_intake_events.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True
    )
    production_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("production_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system", server_default="system")

    contract_version: Mapped[ConnectorContractVersion] = relationship()
