class AutomationOptimizationRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "automation_optimization_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("run_key", name="uq_automation_optimization_run_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ("
            "'planned','awaiting_executor','processing','succeeded','running','awaiting_release_review',"
            "'failed','cancelled'"
            ")",
            name="ck_automation_optimization_run_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "estimated_cost_micros >= 0 AND actual_cost_micros >= 0",
            name="ck_automation_optimization_run_costs",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="space_image", server_default="space_image", index=True
    )
    run_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    base_prompt_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    policy_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="planned", index=True
    )
    dry_run: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    trigger_reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    case_ids_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    frozen_input_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    result_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    candidate_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    estimated_cost_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    actual_cost_micros: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    budget_settled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, default=False, server_default=sql_text("0")
    )
    input_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    output_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    total_tokens: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    retryable: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="automation")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

class SourceIdentityVerification(Base):
    """Human-approved evidence for one upstream source identity contract."""

    __tablename__ = "source_identity_verifications"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "result IN ('verified','conflict')",
            name="ck_source_identity_verifications_result",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','approved','superseded','rejected')",
            name="ck_source_identity_verifications_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(probe_hash) = 64",
            name="ck_source_identity_verifications_probe_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "scoped_row_count >= 0 AND duplicate_key_count >= 0 "
            "AND res_id_conflict_count >= 0",
            name="ck_source_identity_verifications_counts",
        ),
        Index(
            "uq_source_identity_verifications_approved_source",
            "contract_key",
            "source_system",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("status = 'approved'"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    source_system: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    key_fields_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    probe_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    data_window: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    scoped_row_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    duplicate_key_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    res_id_conflict_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="draft", index=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    approved_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    approved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )


class ContentRecord(Base):
    """Local projection of an upstream content item, never a remote DB mirror."""

    __tablename__ = "content_records"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "source_system", "source_content_id", name="uq_content_records_source_content"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('awaiting_material','ready','deleted')",
            name="ck_content_records_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "identity_status IN "
            "('legacy_unverified','pending_verification','verified','conflict')",
            name="ck_content_records_identity_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "source_res_type IS NULL OR source_res_type IN (1, 6)",
            name="ck_content_records_source_res_type",
        ),
        Index(
            "uq_content_records_verified_key",
            "content_key",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("content_key IS NOT NULL"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    source_system: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    source_content_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    source_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    source_occurred_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    content_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(320), nullable=True)
    source_res_type: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    source_ll_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=True)
    source_res_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=True)
    identity_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30),
        default="legacy_unverified",
        server_default="legacy_unverified",
        index=True,
    )
    identity_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True, index=True)
    identity_verification_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("source_identity_verifications.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    asset_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    current_asset_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("asset_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="awaiting_material", index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )

    asset: Mapped[Asset | None] = relationship()
    current_asset_version: Mapped[AssetVersion | None] = relationship()
    identity_verification: Mapped[SourceIdentityVerification | None] = relationship()

class SemanticTagFact(Base):
    """Append-only field-level Canonical semantic fact."""

    __tablename__ = "semantic_tag_facts"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "asset_version_id",
            "field_key",
            "fact_version",
            name="uq_semantic_tag_facts_asset_field_version",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "field_status IN ('required','optional','not_applicable','not_detected','needs_review')",
            name="ck_semantic_tag_facts_field_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('candidate','approved','rejected')",
            name="ck_semantic_tag_facts_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(values_json) AND json_type(values_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_semantic_tag_facts_values_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(evidence_json) AND json_type(evidence_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_semantic_tag_facts_evidence_json",
        ),
        Index(
            "uq_semantic_tag_fact_review_approval",
            "source_evaluation_id",
            "source_review_id",
            "asset_version_id",
            "field_key",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("status = 'approved'"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    asset_version_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("asset_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    field_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    fact_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    field_status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    supersedes_fact_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("semantic_tag_facts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    values_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    evidence_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    source_evaluation_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("evaluation_results.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    source_review_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("human_reviews.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    contract_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("tag_demand_contracts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    normalization_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    mapping_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    payload_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )

class ProjectionContract(Base):
    __tablename__ = "projection_contracts"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "contract_key", "version", name="uq_projection_contract_version"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "target_role IN ('unified_dimension','search_labels','quality_governance')",
            name="ck_projection_contract_target_role",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "environment IN ('local','test','shadow')",
            name="ck_projection_contract_environment",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "write_policy IN ('local_only','shadow_only')",
            name="ck_projection_contract_write_policy",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "mode IN ('snapshot','incremental_outbox')",
            name="ck_projection_contract_mode",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('draft','active','retired')",
            name="ck_projection_contract_status",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    target_role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    table_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    environment: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="local", index=True)
    adapter_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(80), default="local-sqlite", server_default="local-sqlite"
    )
    target_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(120), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    write_policy: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(20), default="local_only", server_default="local_only"
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(40), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    field_contract_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("field_demand_contracts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    max_batch_size: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=500, server_default="500"
    )
    primary_key_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    field_mappings_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    input_versions_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="snapshot")
    idempotency_key_template: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    checkpoint_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    reconciliation_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    rollback_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    owner: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="draft", index=True)
    contract_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True, index=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    items: Mapped[list["SampleSetItem"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="sample_set",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        order_by="SampleSetItem.created_at",
    )

class BaselineTruthRevision(Base):
    """One append-only authoritative truth revision for an imported item."""

    __tablename__ = "baseline_truth_revisions"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "baseline_set_item_id",
            "revision",
            name="uq_baseline_truth_revisions_item_revision",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "revision >= 1",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_revision",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "truth_kind IN ('level','structured_fields')",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_kind",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(truth_kind = 'level' AND "
            "expected_level IN ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L5') AND "
            "json_type(expected_result_json, '$.level') = 'text' AND "
            "json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level') = expected_level) OR "
            "(truth_kind = 'structured_fields' AND expected_level IS NULL)",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_shape",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(expected_result_json) AND "
            "json_type(expected_result_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_result_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(workflow_input_json) AND "
            "json_type(workflow_input_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_input_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(previous_hash) = 64 AND previous_hash = lower(previous_hash) "
            "AND previous_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_previous_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(canonical_hash) = 64 AND canonical_hash = lower(canonical_hash) "
            "AND canonical_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_baseline_truth_revisions_canonical_hash",
        ),
        Index(
            "ix_baseline_truth_revisions_item",
            "baseline_set_item_id",
            "revision",
        ),
        Index(
            "uq_baseline_truth_revisions_correction",
            "source_correction_key",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=sql_text("source_correction_key IS NOT NULL"),
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    baseline_set_item_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_set_items.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    truth_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30))
    expected_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(10), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    expected_result_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    truth_contract_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    workflow_input_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    source_baseline_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    source_production_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("production_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    source_correction_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(200), nullable=True
    )
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    previous_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    canonical_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )

    baseline_set_item: Mapped[BaselineSetItem] = relationship(
        back_populates="truth_revisions"
    )
    source_baseline_run: Mapped["BaselineRegressionRun | None"] = relationship()
    source_production_run: Mapped["ProductionRun | None"] = relationship()


class BaselineRegressionRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "baseline_regression_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "baseline_set_id", "sequence_no", name="uq_baseline_run_sequence"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('running','completed','partial_failed','failed')",
            name="ck_baseline_regression_runs_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "sequence_no >= 1", name="ck_baseline_regression_runs_sequence"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "execution_engine IN ('legacy_worker','native_workflow')",
            name="ck_baseline_regression_runs_execution_engine",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "execution_engine <> 'native_workflow' OR workflow_version_id IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_baseline_regression_runs_native_workflow_identity",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    baseline_set_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_sets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    sequence_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    previous_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    strategy_bundle_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("strategy_bundles.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    execution_engine: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30),
        default="legacy_worker",
        server_default="legacy_worker",
        index=True,
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="space_image", server_default="space_image", index=True
    )
    strategy_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    execution_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    correction_contract_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=True
    )
    correction_contract_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    baseline_set_fingerprint: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="running", index=True)
    total: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    completed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    valid_predictions: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    failed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    metrics_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    baseline_set: Mapped[BaselineSet] = relationship(back_populates="runs")
    previous_run: Mapped["BaselineRegressionRun | None"] = relationship(
        remote_side=[id]
    )
    strategy_bundle: Mapped[StrategyBundle | None] = relationship()
    workflow_version: Mapped[WorkflowVersion | None] = relationship()
    items: Mapped[list["BaselineRegressionItem"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="run",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        order_by="BaselineRegressionItem.id",
    )

class BaselineCorrectionRun(Base):
    """Independent correction analysis for one frozen baseline run."""

    __tablename__ = "baseline_correction_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("idempotency_key", name="uq_baseline_correction_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('processing','awaiting_decision','approved','rejected','failed')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "stage IN ('analysis','candidate_generation','candidate_validation','regression','decision')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_stage",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "decision IS NULL OR decision IN ('approved','rejected')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_decision",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("attempt_count >= 1", name="ck_baseline_correction_attempts"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "progress BETWEEN 0 AND 100",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_progress",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(selected_item_ids_json) AND "
            "json_type(selected_item_ids_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_selected_items",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(input_snapshot_json) AND "
            "json_type(input_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_input",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(report_json) AND json_type(report_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_report",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(blockers_json) AND json_type(blockers_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_blockers",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    baseline_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    selected_item_ids_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    input_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="processing", index=True)
    stage: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="analysis", index=True)
    progress: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    report_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    blockers_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    orchestration_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    candidate_revision_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("category_evaluation_v3_revisions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    regression_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    decision: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    decided_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    decided_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    decision_note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    baseline_run: Mapped[BaselineRegressionRun] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[baseline_run_id]
    )


class BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate(Base):
    """One immutable component replacement considered by a correction run."""

    __tablename__ = "baseline_correction_component_candidates"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "correction_run_id",
            "node_instance_id",
            name="uq_baseline_correction_component_node",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "selection_kind IN ('reused','generated')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_selection",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(source_prompt_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND candidate_prompt_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND source_rule_module_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND candidate_rule_module_version_id IS NULL) OR "
            "(source_prompt_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND candidate_prompt_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND source_rule_module_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND candidate_rule_module_version_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_version_pair",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(content_hash) = 64",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_hash",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    correction_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_correction_runs.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    node_instance_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_node_assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    source_prompt_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_prompt_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    source_rule_module_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("rule_module_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_rule_module_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("rule_module_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    selection_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    evidence_summary_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )

    correction_run: Mapped[BaselineCorrectionRun] = relationship()


class BaselineFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a frozen baseline definition is changed in place."""


@event.listens_for(BaselineSet, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineSet, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_set_mutation(
    _mapper: object, _connection: Connection, _target: BaselineSet
) -> None:
    raise BaselineFrozenError("BaselineSet 创建后不可修改或删除")


@event.listens_for(BaselineSetItem, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineSetItem, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_set_item_mutation(
    _mapper: object, _connection: Connection, _target: BaselineSetItem
) -> None:
    raise BaselineFrozenError("BaselineSet 条目创建后不可修改或删除")


class BaselineTruthFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when append-only baseline truth is changed in place."""


@event.listens_for(BaselineTruthRevision, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineTruthRevision, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_truth_revision_mutation(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    _target: BaselineTruthRevision,
) -> None:
    raise BaselineTruthFrozenError("BaselineTruthRevision 不可修改或删除")


class PromptRegressionRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "prompt_regression_runs"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    sample_set_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("sample_sets.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    trigger_prompt_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    prompt_a_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True
    )
    prompt_b_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(

class EvaluationPackage(Base):
    """Immutable review and release aggregate for one candidate strategy."""

    __tablename__ = "evaluation_packages"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "package_key", name="uq_evaluation_packages_package_key"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ("
            "'validating','awaiting_review','approved','rejected',"
            "'published','archived'"
            ")",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "prompt_mode IN ('single','dual')",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_prompt_mode",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(prompt_mode = 'single' AND prompt_b_id IS NULL) OR "
            "(prompt_mode = 'dual' AND prompt_b_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_prompt_mode_refs",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(canonical_manifest_json) AND "
            "json_type(canonical_manifest_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_manifest_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(canonical_manifest_hash) = 64 AND "
            "canonical_manifest_hash = lower(canonical_manifest_hash) AND "
            "canonical_manifest_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_manifest_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(request_hash) = 64 AND "
            "request_hash = lower(request_hash) AND "
            "request_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_request_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "review_revision >= 0",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_review_revision",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    package_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(160), unique=True, index=True
    )
    request_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    prompt_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)
    prompt_a_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    prompt_b_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    dimension_schema_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("dimension_schemas.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    dimension_route_policy_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("dimension_route_policies.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    sample_set_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("sample_sets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    baseline_strategy_bundle_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("strategy_bundles.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_strategy_bundle_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("strategy_bundles.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    regression_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        index=True,
    )
    automation_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("automation_optimization_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    metric_snapshot_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_metric_snapshots.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    canonical_manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    canonical_manifest_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), index=True
    )
    ai_recommendation: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(40), default="pending", index=True
    )
    change_summary: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(30), default="validating", index=True
    )
    review_revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    review_decision: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(20), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    review_note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    reviewed_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True
    )
    reviewed_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    published_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True
    )
    published_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    archived_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(80), nullable=True
    )

class MechanismRelease(Base):
    """Immutable activation record for the mechanism release axis."""

    __tablename__ = "mechanism_releases"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("release_key", name="uq_mechanism_releases_release_key"),
        UniqueConstraint("evaluation_package_id", name="uq_mechanism_releases_package"),
        UniqueConstraint(
            "category_key", "revision", name="uq_mechanism_releases_category_revision"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('active','superseded','rolled_back')",
            name="ck_mechanism_releases_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("revision >= 1", name="ck_mechanism_releases_revision"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(manifest_hash) = 64 AND manifest_hash = lower(manifest_hash) "
            "AND manifest_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_mechanism_releases_manifest_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(manifest_json) AND json_type(manifest_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_mechanism_releases_manifest_json",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    release_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    evaluation_package_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("evaluation_packages.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    previous_release_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("mechanism_releases.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    manifest_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    manifest_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="active", index=True)
    activated_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    activated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)

    evaluation_package: Mapped[EvaluationPackage] = relationship()
    previous_release: Mapped["MechanismRelease | None"] = relationship(
        remote_side=[id]
    )


class StockRerun(Base):
    """Control-plane record for an explicit, snapshot-frozen stock rerun."""

    __tablename__ = "stock_reruns"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("idempotency_key", name="uq_stock_reruns_idempotency_key"),

        ),

            "json_valid(material_scope_json) AND json_type(material_scope_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_material_scope_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(mechanism_snapshot_json) AND json_type(mechanism_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_mechanism_snapshot_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(model_snapshot_json) AND json_type(model_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_model_snapshot_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(prompt_snapshot_json) AND json_type(prompt_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_prompt_snapshot_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(rule_snapshot_json) AND json_type(rule_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_rule_snapshot_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(execution_snapshot_json) AND json_type(execution_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_stock_reruns_execution_snapshot_json",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    source_mechanism_release_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("mechanism_releases.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=True, index=True
    )
    target_mechanism_release_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("mechanism_releases.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    material_scope_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    mechanism_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    model_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    prompt_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    rule_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    execution_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30), default="planned", index=True)
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="", server_default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, index=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow)

    source_mechanism_release: Mapped[MechanismRelease | None] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[source_mechanism_release_id]
    )
    target_mechanism_release: Mapped[MechanismRelease] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[target_mechanism_release_id]
    )


class EvaluationPackageFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when frozen package identity or reviewed evidence is changed."""


_EVALUATION_PACKAGE_IDENTITY_FIELDS = (
    "package_key",
    "request_hash",
    "category_key",
    "prompt_mode",
    "prompt_a_id",
    "prompt_b_id",
    "dimension_schema_id",
    "dimension_route_policy_id",
    "sample_set_id",
    "baseline_strategy_bundle_id",
    "candidate_strategy_bundle_id",
    "regression_run_id",
    "automation_run_id",
    "metric_snapshot_id",
)


@event.listens_for(EvaluationPackage, "before_update")
def _protect_evaluation_package_update(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    target: EvaluationPackage,
) -> None:
    changed_identity = _changed_fields(
        target, _EVALUATION_PACKAGE_IDENTITY_FIELDS
    )
    if changed_identity:
        raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
            "EvaluationPackage 冻结引用禁止修改："
            + "、".join(sorted(changed_identity))
        )
    manifest_changed = _changed_fields(
        target,
        (
            "canonical_manifest_json",
            "canonical_manifest_hash",
            "ai_recommendation",
            "change_summary",
        ),
    )
    status_history = attributes.get_history(target, "status")
    previous_status = (
        str(status_history.deleted[0])
        if status_history.deleted
        else target.status
    )
    if manifest_changed and previous_status != "validating":
        raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
            "EvaluationPackage 进入二审后冻结清单禁止修改"
        )


@event.listens_for(EvaluationPackage, "before_delete")
def _protect_evaluation_package_delete(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    _target: EvaluationPackage,
) -> None:
    raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
        "EvaluationPackage 是永久审计记录，禁止删除"
    )

===== FILE backend/app/module_api.py =====
"""Explicit API deployment ownership, also used to generate nginx locations."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

PREFIXES = {
    "incremental": (
        "incremental-mechanisms", "incremental-materials", "incremental-evidence",
        "incremental-corrections", "incremental-correction-analysis", "failures",
        "source-recovery", "connectors", "connector-governance", "content-ingress",
        "incremental-automation", "incremental-dashboard", "incremental-controls",
    ),
    "stock": (
        "baseline-sets", "baseline-regressions", "baseline-truth-contracts", "cover-regressions",
        "baseline-corrections", "baseline-exports", "stock-exports", "prompt-regressions",
        "paired-regressions", "sample-sets", "design-case-samples",
        "design-case-dimension-samples", "dimension-schemas", "dimension-route-policies",
    ),
}
MODULES = ("common", "incremental", "stock")
SHARED_PREFIXES = ("/api/auth/", "/api/health/")


def owner(path: str) -> str:
    head = path.split("/")[2] if path.startswith("/api/") else ""
    for module, prefixes in PREFIXES.items():
        if head in prefixes:
            return module
    return "common"


def serves(module: str, path: str) -> bool:
    return owner(path) == module or path.startswith(SHARED_PREFIXES)


class InstanceActivity:
    """Observe in-flight HTTP, keep draining independent of fleet readiness."""
    def __init__(self, app, *, module: str, drain_file: Path):
        self.app, self.module, self.drain_file = app, module, drain_file
        self.active = 0

    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
        if scope["type"] != "http":
            return await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        path = scope["path"]
        if path == "/api/health/module":
            return await JSONResponse({"module": self.module, "active_requests": self.active,
                "draining": self.drain_file.exists(), "build_sha": os.getenv("LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA", "unknown")})(scope, receive, send)
        # Gate the resolved request path, not app.router.routes: recent FastAPI
        # versions retain include_router() as lazy containers without .path.
        # Filtering those containers silently removes all included business APIs.
        # Leave FastAPI's router/dependencies intact and reject other modules
        # before any handler or dependency runs.
        if not serves(self.module, path):
            return await JSONResponse({"detail": "Not Found"}, status_code=404)(scope, receive, send)
        if self.drain_file.exists():
            return await JSONResponse({"code": "instance_draining"}, status_code=503)(scope, receive, send)
        self.active += 1
        try:
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        finally:
            self.active -= 1


def create_app():
    from .main import app
    module = os.environ.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE", "common")
    if module not in MODULES:
        raise ValueError("invalid LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE")
    app.add_middleware(InstanceActivity, module=module,
        drain_file=Path(os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_API_DRAIN_FILE", "/tmp/label-api-draining")))
    return app

===== FILE backend/app/module_runtime.py =====
"""Same-host business execution boundaries; shared quota/ledger remain authoritative.

The database is the source of admission limits, never one limit per process.
All control changes and claims serialize on the existing scheduler row. A drain
stops new claims only: it does not mutate runs, leases, checkpoints or requests.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import ModuleRuntimeState, ProductionRun, RuntimeDispatchItem, ProductionStepAttempt, EvaluationJob, AutomationWorkerStatus

POOLS = ("incremental", "stock", "compatibility")
EXECUTOR_REVISION = "runtime-v1"
SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS = (EXECUTOR_REVISION,)


def execution_pool(source_type: str | None, run_mode: str | None) -> str:
    if source_type in {"content_ingress_event", "upstream_intake_event"} and run_mode == "incremental":
        return "incremental"
    if source_type in {"baseline_regression_item", "baseline_regression"} and run_mode in {None, "stock_regression"}:
        return "stock"
    # Missing/contradictory provenance is visible and executable in its own pool.
    return "compatibility"


def configured_pool() -> str | None:
    value = os.environ.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_EXECUTION_POOL", "")
    if value and value not in POOLS:
        raise ValueError("invalid LABEL_SYSTEM_EXECUTION_POOL")
    return value or None


def states(db: Session) -> list[ModuleRuntimeState]:
    return list(db.scalars(select(ModuleRuntimeState).order_by(ModuleRuntimeState.pool)))


def configuration(db: Session) -> dict[str, ModuleRuntimeState]:
    rows = {row.pool: row for row in states(db)}
    if not rows:
        return {}
    if set(rows) != set(POOLS):
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime requires all three pools")
    limits = {row.global_limit for row in rows.values()}
    if len(limits) != 1 or any(row.capacity < 1 for row in rows.values()):
        raise RuntimeError("invalid module runtime capacities")
    if sum(row.capacity for row in rows.values()) > next(iter(limits)):
        raise RuntimeError("module pool capacities exceed global limit")
    return rows


def runtime_predicate(pool: str):
    if pool not in POOLS:
        raise ValueError("invalid execution pool")
    return (
        ProductionRun.execution_pool == pool,
        ProductionRun.executor_revision.in_(SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS),
    )


def leased_counts(db: Session) -> dict[str, int]:
    counts = {pool: 0 for pool in POOLS}
    for pool, count in db.execute(
        select(ProductionRun.execution_pool, func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id))
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == ProductionRun.id)
        .join(RuntimeDispatchItem, RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == ProductionStepAttempt.id)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")
        .group_by(ProductionRun.execution_pool)
    ):
        # Unknown pool values consume global capacity and fail closed.
        counts[pool] = int(count)
    counts["compatibility"] += int(db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id)).where(
        EvaluationJob.status == "processing")) or 0)
    return counts


def control(db: Session, *, pool: str, release_id: str, action: str) -> dict:
    from .runtime_queue import _scheduler_state
    from .queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy
    if pool not in POOLS or action not in {"drain", "resume"}:
        raise ValueError("invalid module control action")
    if not release_id or len(release_id) > 80:
        raise ValueError("release_id required (max 80 chars)")
    rows = configuration(db)
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime is not configured")
    _scheduler_state(db, QueuePolicy(global_limit=rows[pool].global_limit))
    row = db.scalar(select(ModuleRuntimeState).where(ModuleRuntimeState.pool == pool)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if action == "drain":
        if row.release_id and row.release_id != release_id:
            raise RuntimeError("module is owned by another release")
        row.accepting = False
        row.release_id = release_id
    else:
        if row.release_id != release_id:
            raise RuntimeError("resume requires the owning release_id")
        row.accepting = True
        row.release_id = ""
    db.flush()
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db, category="module_release", action=action,
        subject_type="execution_pool", subject_id=pool, actor="release:" + release_id,
        payload={"release_id": release_id, "accepting": row.accepting})
    return {"pool": pool, "accepting": row.accepting, "release_id": row.release_id,
            "leased": leased_counts(db)[pool]}


def status(db: Session) -> dict:
    rows = configuration(db)
    counts = leased_counts(db)
    unsupported = list(db.execute(select(ProductionRun.execution_pool, ProductionRun.executor_revision,
        func.count()).where(ProductionRun.status.in_(("queued", "running", "retryable")),
        ProductionRun.executor_revision.not_in(SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS))
        .group_by(ProductionRun.execution_pool, ProductionRun.executor_revision)))
    workers=[]
    for worker in db.scalars(select(AutomationWorkerStatus).where(
            AutomationWorkerStatus.last_seen_at >= datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(seconds=45))):
        result=json.loads(worker.last_result_json or '{}')
        if result.get('execution_pool') in POOLS:
            workers.append({'worker_id':worker.worker_id,'pool':result['execution_pool'],
                'build_sha':result.get('build_sha'),'readiness':worker.readiness,
                'started_at':worker.started_at.replace(tzinfo=worker.started_at.tzinfo or timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                'active':result.get('active'), 'last_status':worker.last_status})
    return {"configured": bool(rows), "supported_executor_revisions": SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS,
        "workers":workers,
        "pools": [{"pool": pool, "capacity": row.capacity, "global_limit": row.global_limit,
                   "accepting": row.accepting, "release_id": row.release_id, "leased": counts[pool]}
                  for pool, row in rows.items()],
        "unsupported": [{"pool": p, "revision": r, "count": n} for p, r, n in unsupported]}


def initialize(db: Session, capacities: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    """One-time offline cutover; cannot silently overwrite a live configuration."""
    if set(capacities) != set(POOLS) or any(type(n) is not int or n < 1 for n in capacities.values()):
        raise ValueError("three positive integer capacities required")
    if states(db):
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime already initialized")
    if any(leased_counts(db).values()):
        raise RuntimeError("initial cutover requires all legacy executors drained")
    limit = sum(capacities.values())
    db.add_all([ModuleRuntimeState(pool=pool, capacity=n, global_limit=limit, accepting=True,
                                  release_id="") for pool, n in capacities.items()])
    db.flush()


def resize(db: Session, capacities: dict[str, int], *, actor: str = "operator:module_runtime") -> dict:
    """Online capacity change; the next claim reads the new rows under the scheduler lock.

    Growing is always safe. Shrinking is refused while a pool holds more leases
    than its new capacity (nothing is preempted). Refused while any pool is
    owned by a release, so a release window never races a capacity change.
    """
    if set(capacities) != set(POOLS) or any(type(n) is not int or n < 1 for n in capacities.values()):
        raise ValueError("three positive integer capacities required")
    from .runtime_queue import _scheduler_state
    from .queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy
    rows = configuration(db)
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime is not configured")
    # Same lock order as control()/claims: scheduler row first, then the pool rows.
    _scheduler_state(db, QueuePolicy(global_limit=next(iter(rows.values())).global_limit))
    rows = {row.pool: row for row in db.scalars(select(ModuleRuntimeState).order_by(ModuleRuntimeState.pool)
                                                .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))}
    owned = sorted(pool for pool, row in rows.items() if row.release_id)
    if owned:
        raise RuntimeError("resize refused: pools owned by a release: " + ", ".join(owned))
    counts = leased_counts(db)
    for pool in POOLS:
        if capacities[pool] < rows[pool].capacity and counts[pool] > capacities[pool]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"resize refused: pool {pool} has {counts[pool]} leased above new capacity {capacities[pool]}")
    before = {pool: {"capacity": rows[pool].capacity, "global_limit": rows[pool].global_limit} for pool in POOLS}
    limit = sum(capacities.values())
    for pool in POOLS:
        rows[pool].capacity = capacities[pool]
        rows[pool].global_limit = limit
    db.flush()
    after = {pool: {"capacity": capacities[pool], "global_limit": limit} for pool in POOLS}
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db, category="module_runtime", action="resize",
        subject_type="module_runtime", subject_id="pools", actor=actor,
        payload={"before": before, "after": after, "leased": counts})
    return {"before": before, "after": after, "leased": counts}


def main() -> None:
    import argparse
    from .database import session_scope
    from .service_entry import validate_database
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("action", choices=("status", "drain", "resume", "initialize", "resize"))
    parser.add_argument("--pool", choices=POOLS)
    parser.add_argument("--release-id")
    parser.add_argument("--capacities", help="JSON with three pool capacities")
    parser.add_argument("--offline-confirmed", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--actor", default="operator:module_runtime", help="audit actor for resize")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    validate_database()
    with session_scope() as db:
        if args.action == "initialize":
            if not args.offline_confirmed:
                parser.error("initialize requires --offline-confirmed")
            initialize(db, json.loads(args.capacities or "{}"))
        elif args.action == "resize":
            if not args.capacities:
                parser.error("resize requires --capacities")
            resize(db, json.loads(args.capacities), actor=args.actor)
        elif args.action != "status":
            control(db, pool=args.pool, release_id=args.release_id, action=args.action)
        print(json.dumps(status(db), ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== FILE backend/app/mysql_importer.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    Connection,
    DateTime,
    Engine,
    Integer,
    Table,
    and_,
    func,
    inspect,
    select,
    text,
)
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401
from .database import Base
from .invariant_catalog import INVARIANT_CATALOG
from .migrations.mysql_invariants import (
    MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS,
    install_mysql_invariants,
    installed_mysql_invariants,
)
from .snapshot_import_common import (
    ImportPlan,
    ImportReport,
    InjectedImportStop,
    SnapshotImportError,
    load_checkpoint,
    open_immutable_sqlite,
    resolve_source_snapshot,
    source_batch,
    source_schema_compatibility_version,
    validate_source_schema,
    verify_source_unchanged,
    write_checkpoint,
)


MySQLImportError = SnapshotImportError


def _mysql_identifier(value: str) -> str:
    if len(value) > 64 or re.fullmatch(r"[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*", value) is None:
        raise MySQLImportError(f"不安全的 MySQL 标识符：{value}")
    return f"`{value}`"


def _target_schema(connection: Connection) -> str:
    value = connection.scalar(text("SELECT DATABASE()"))
    schema = "" if value is None else str(value).strip()
    if (
        len(schema) > 64
        or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", schema) is None
    ):
        raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 导入目标 schema 名称不安全")
    return schema


def assert_mysql_import_schema_empty(connection: Connection) -> None:
    """Refuse to initialize an import target that already contains objects."""

    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    existing = sorted(
        set(inspector.get_table_names()) | set(inspector.get_view_names())
    )
    if existing:
        raise MySQLImportError(
            "MySQL 探针目标不是全空 schema，禁止初始化或覆盖"
        )


def _assert_target_ready(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    resumed: bool,
) -> None:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    actual_tables = set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
    missing = set(Base.metadata.tables) - actual_tables
    if missing:
        raise MySQLImportError(f"MySQL 目标缺少基线表：{sorted(missing)}")
    progress = checkpoint["tables"]
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        actual_count = int(
            connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
        )
        if not resumed and actual_count:
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 目标表 {table.name} 非空，禁止首次合并导入"
            )
        if resumed and table.name in progress:
            recorded_count = int(progress[table.name]["imported_rows"])
            if actual_count < recorded_count:
                raise MySQLImportError(
                    f"MySQL 目标表 {table.name} 少于检查点记录"
                )


def _comparison_value(value: Any, table: Table, column_name: str) -> Any:
    column = table.c[column_name]
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(column.type, Boolean):
        return bool(value)
    if isinstance(column.type, DateTime) and isinstance(value, datetime):
        if value.tzinfo is not None:
            value = value.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
        return value
    return value


def _rows_match(
    existing: dict[str, Any],
    incoming: dict[str, Any],
    table: Table,
) -> bool:
    return all(
        _comparison_value(existing[column_name], table, column_name)
        == _comparison_value(value, table, column_name)
        for column_name, value in incoming.items()
    )


def _insert_batch(
    connection: Connection,
    table: Table,
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
    primary_keys = [row[primary_key.name] for row in rows]
    existing_rows = connection.execute(
        select(table).where(primary_key.in_(primary_keys))
    ).mappings().all()
    existing_by_key = {
        row[primary_key.name]: dict(row)
        for row in existing_rows
    }
    missing_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    duplicate_rows = 0
    for row in rows:
        existing = existing_by_key.get(row[primary_key.name])
        if existing is None:
            missing_rows.append(row)
            continue
        if not _rows_match(existing, row, table):
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 导入表 {table.name} 发现同主键冲突；行内容已隐藏"
            )
        duplicate_rows += 1
    if missing_rows:
        connection.execute(table.insert(), missing_rows)
    return len(missing_rows), duplicate_rows


def _verify_imported_row_counts(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    plan: ImportPlan,
) -> None:
    for table_name in plan:
        table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
        expected = int(checkpoint["tables"][table_name]["imported_rows"])
        actual = int(
            connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
        )
        if actual != expected:
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 导入表 {table_name} 行数与源快照不一致"
            )


def _verify_no_foreign_key_orphans(connection: Connection) -> None:
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for ordinal, constraint in enumerate(table.foreign_key_constraints):
            elements = tuple(constraint.elements)
            if not elements:
                continue
            parent_table = elements[0].column.table
            parent = parent_table.alias(
                f"fk_parent_{table.name}_{ordinal}"
            )
            join_condition = and_(
                *(
                    element.parent
                    == parent.c[element.column.name]
                    for element in elements
                )
            )
            local_values_present = and_(
                *(element.parent.is_not(None) for element in elements)
            )
            missing_parent = parent.c[elements[0].column.name].is_(None)
            orphan_count = int(
                connection.scalar(
                    select(func.count())
                    .select_from(table.outerjoin(parent, join_condition))
                    .where(local_values_present, missing_parent)
                )
                or 0
            )
            if orphan_count:
                name = constraint.name or f"foreign_key_{ordinal}"
                raise MySQLImportError(
                    f"MySQL 导入后外键对账失败：{table.name}.{name}"
                )


def _reset_mysql_auto_increment(connection: Connection) -> None:
    next_values: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
    with connection.begin():
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
            if not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer):
                continue
            maximum = connection.scalar(select(func.max(primary_key)))
            next_values.append(
                (table.name, 1 if maximum is None else int(maximum) + 1)
            )
    for table_name, next_value in next_values:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"ALTER TABLE {_mysql_identifier(table_name)} "
            f"AUTO_INCREMENT = {next_value}"
        )
        connection.commit()


def _invariant_state(connection: Connection) -> str:
    expected_guards = {
        (spec.table_name, spec.guard_column)
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    installed_guards = {
        (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME))
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME "
                "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
            )
        )
    }
    expected_indexes = {spec.index_name for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS}
    installed_indexes = {
        str(row.INDEX_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT DISTINCT INDEX_NAME "
                "FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND INDEX_NAME LIKE 'uq_%'"
            )
        )
        if str(row.INDEX_NAME) in expected_indexes
    }
    expected_triggers = sum(
        contract.owner == "database_trigger"
        for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    )
    installed_triggers = len(installed_mysql_invariants(connection))
    if not installed_guards and not installed_indexes and not installed_triggers:
        return "absent"
    if (
        installed_guards == expected_guards
        and installed_indexes == expected_indexes
        and installed_triggers == expected_triggers
    ):
        return "complete"
    return "partial"


def finalize_mysql_import_target(target: Engine) -> None:
    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 导入终态只能安装到 MySQL")
    with target.connect() as connection:
        state = _invariant_state(connection)
        connection.commit()
        if state == "complete":
            return
        if state == "partial":
            raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 不变量处于部分安装状态，禁止自动修复")
        try:
            install_mysql_invariants(connection)
            connection.commit()
        except SQLAlchemyError:
            connection.rollback()
            raise MySQLImportError(
                "MySQL 不变量安装失败；数据库细节已隐藏"
            ) from None
        if _invariant_state(connection) != "complete":
            raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 不变量安装后内省不完整")
        connection.commit()


def import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
    stop_after_batches: int | None = None,
) -> ImportReport:
    if batch_size <= 0:
        raise MySQLImportError("batch_size 必须大于 0")
    if stop_after_batches is not None and stop_after_batches <= 0:
        raise MySQLImportError("stop_after_batches 必须大于 0")
    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(source)
    source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    inserted_rows = 0
    duplicate_rows = 0
    committed_batches = 0
    try:
        source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
            source_connection,
            Base.metadata,
        )
        source_schema_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        complete_plan = ImportPlan.from_metadata(Base.metadata)
        plan = ImportPlan(
            tuple(
                table_name
                for table_name in complete_plan
                if table_name in source_columns_by_table
            )
        )
        with target.connect() as connection:
            target_schema = _target_schema(connection)
            checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-mysql-import.json"
            checkpoint, resumed = load_checkpoint(
                checkpoint_path,
                source=resolved_source,
                source_size=source_stat.st_size,
                source_sha256=source_sha256,
                plan=plan,
                format_name="sqlite-to-mysql-import-v1",
                target_identity={"target_schema": target_schema},
            )
            _assert_target_ready(
                connection,
                checkpoint=checkpoint,
                resumed=resumed,
            )
            connection.commit()
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
            connection.commit()
            try:
                table_progress: dict[str, Any] = checkpoint["tables"]
                for table_name in plan:
                    table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
                    progress = table_progress.setdefault(
                        table_name,
                        {
                            "completed": False,
                            "imported_rows": 0,
                            "last_primary_key": None,
                        },
                    )
                    if progress["completed"]:
                        continue
                    while True:
                        rows = source_batch(
                            source_connection,
                            table,
                            last_primary_key=progress["last_primary_key"],
                            batch_size=batch_size,
                            source_columns=source_columns_by_table[table_name],
                            source_schema_version=source_schema_version,
                        )
                        if not rows:
                            progress["completed"] = True
                            write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                            break
                        transaction = connection.begin()
                        try:
                            inserted, duplicates = _insert_batch(
                                connection,
                                table,
                                rows,
                            )
                            transaction.commit()
                        except SnapshotImportError:
                            transaction.rollback()
                            raise
                        except SQLAlchemyError:
                            transaction.rollback()
                            raise MySQLImportError(
                                f"MySQL 导入表 {table.name} 的批次失败；"
                                "行内容已隐藏"
                            ) from None
                        inserted_rows += inserted
                        duplicate_rows += duplicates
                        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                        progress["last_primary_key"] = rows[-1][
                            primary_key.name
                        ]
                        progress["imported_rows"] = int(
                            progress["imported_rows"]
                        ) + len(rows)
                        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                        committed_batches += 1
                        if (
                            stop_after_batches is not None
                            and committed_batches >= stop_after_batches
                        ):
                            raise InjectedImportStop(
                                f"在第 {committed_batches} 个已提交批次后注入停止"
                            )
                with connection.begin():
                    _verify_imported_row_counts(
                        connection,
                        checkpoint=checkpoint,
                        plan=plan,
                    )
            finally:
                if connection.in_transaction():
                    connection.rollback()
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1"
                )
                connection.commit()

            with connection.begin():
                _verify_no_foreign_key_orphans(connection)
            _reset_mysql_auto_increment(connection)

        finalize_mysql_import_target(target)
        checkpoint["complete"] = True
        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
        table_rows = {
            table_name: int(progress["imported_rows"])
            for table_name, progress in checkpoint["tables"].items()
        }
        return ImportReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            inserted_rows=inserted_rows,
            duplicate_rows=duplicate_rows,
            table_rows=table_rows,
            completed_tables=sum(
                1
                for progress in checkpoint["tables"].values()
                if progress["completed"]
            ),
            resumed=resumed,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        verify_source_unchanged(
            resolved_source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_sha256,
        )


def resume_mysql_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
) -> ImportReport:
    return import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=batch_size,
    )
